01

方格上二维反铁磁Q状态Potts模型的机器学习研究#

原题：A Machine Learning study of the two-dimensional antiferromagnetic Q-state Potts model on the square lattice
作者：Shang-Wei Li、Kai-Wei Huang、Chien-Ting Chen、Fu-Jiun Jiang。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-09
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：利用监督神经网络（NN）研究了 Q=2、3、4、5 和 6 方格上二维反铁磁 Q 态 Potts 模型的临界现象。传统的监督和无监督神经网络要么失败，要么难以识别这些模型的正确关键行为。值得注意的是，尽管这里使用的神经网络是用特定的固定类型训练集进行训练的，该训练集不包含有关所考虑模型的信息（Q = 2 除外），但它们正确地计算了所研究的物理系统的临界温度。特别是，NN 结果令人信服地表明，Q=3 模型仅在零温度下至关重要，而 Q=4,…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

利用监督神经网络（NN）研究了 Q=2、3、4、5 和 6 方格上二维反铁磁 Q 态 Potts 模型的临界现象。传统的监督和无监督神经网络要么失败，要么难以识别这些模型的正确关键行为。值得注意的是，尽管这里使用的神经网络是用特定的固定类型训练集进行训练的，该训练集不包含有关所考虑模型的信息（Q = 2 除外），但它们正确地计算了所研究的物理系统的临界温度。特别是，NN 结果令人信服地表明，Q=3 模型仅在零温度下至关重要，而 Q=4,5,6 模型在所有温度下都保持无序。除了研究的模型之外，我们使用完全相同的训练集训练的神经网络已成功确定了另一个物理系统的临界温度和相关相变的性质，该系统与此处考虑的系统显着不同。因此，我们的神经网络有潜力成为研究相变的普遍适用的神经网络模型。

English Abstract

The critical phenomena of 2D antiferromagnetic Q-state Potts model on the square lattice with Q=2,3,4,5 and 6 are investigated using supervised neural networks (NN). Conventional supervised and unsupervised NNs either fail or have difficulty in identifying the correct critical behaviors of these models. Remarkably, despite the fact that the NNs employed here are trained with a particular fixed type training set which contains no information about the considered models (except for Q=2), they correctly compute the critical temperatures for the studied physical systems. Particularly, the NN outcomes suggest convincingly that the Q=3 model is critical only at zero temperature and Q=4,5,6 models remain disordered at all temperatures. Apart from the studied models, our NNs trained with exactly the same training set have successfully determined the critical temperature(s) and the nature of the associated phase transition(s) of another physical system differing dramatically from those considered here. Therefore, our NN has the potential to be a universally applicable NN model in studying phase transitions.

02

通过 SHAP 分析和 SISSO 建模对混合钙钛矿中的形成能和带隙进行可解释的机器学习#

原题：Interpretable machine learning of formation energies and band gaps in hybrid perovskites through SHAP analysis and SISSO modelling
作者：Christopher Xia、Hao Zhang、Qingyuan Jin。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-10
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：准确且可解释的性质预测对于探索有机-无机杂化钙钛矿至关重要。在这里，我们将机器学习筛选与 SHAP 分析和 SISSO 描述符发现相结合。使用 4,456 种化合物，优化的 XGBoost 模型在测试集上实现了形成能 R² 值为 0.99，带隙 R² 值为 0.93，并应用于超过 180,000 个候选化合物。对大约 17,000 种常见化合物的比较揭示了 XGBoost 和 CGCNN 的分布广泛重叠，但 UPET 的预测大大降低，这…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

准确且可解释的性质预测对于探索有机-无机杂化钙钛矿至关重要。在这里，我们将机器学习筛选与 SHAP 分析和 SISSO 描述符发现相结合。使用 4,456 种化合物，优化的 XGBoost 模型在测试集上实现了形成能 R² 值为 0.99，带隙 R² 值为 0.93，并应用于超过 180,000 个候选化合物。对大约 17,000 种常见化合物的比较揭示了 XGBoost 和 CGCNN 的分布广泛重叠，但 UPET 的预测大大降低，这表明其向混合有机-无机钙钛矿的可转移性存在潜在限制。 SHAP 分析和基于相关性的修剪保留了每个属性的八个主要特征，SISSO 从中导出了包含四个非线性描述符的表达式。这些表达式结合了形成能的分子和无机特性，并强调了带隙的相对原子尺寸和 B 位特征。该框架支持有效的候选优先级排序和对混合钙钛矿中学习的结构-性质关系的可解释分析。

English Abstract

Accurate and interpretable property prediction is essential for exploring hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. Here, we combine machine-learning screening with SHAP analysis and SISSO descriptor discovery. Using 4,456 compounds, optimized XGBoost models achieved test-set R² values of 0.99 for formation energy and 0.93 for band gap and were applied to over 180,000 candidates. Comparison across approximately 17,000 common compounds revealed broadly overlapping distributions from XGBoost and CGCNN but substantially lower predictions from UPET, suggesting potential limitations in its transferability to hybrid organic-inorganic perovskites. SHAP analysis and correlation-based pruning retained eight primary features for each property, from which SISSO derived expressions comprising four nonlinear descriptors. These expressions couple molecular and inorganic properties for formation energy and emphasize relative atomic sizes and B-site characteristics for band gap. This framework supports efficient candidate prioritization and interpretable analysis of learned structures-property relationships in hybrid perovskites.

PBE 辅助机器学习卤化物钙钛矿中 HSE06+SOC 光伏效率#

原题：PBE-assisted machine learning of HSE06+SOC photovoltaic efficiency in halide perovskites
作者：Azhar Ali Haidry、Courtney Rutendo Mandebvu、Qawareer Fatima。Preprints，2026-11-10

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：与半局域 PBE 计算相比，具有自旋轨道耦合的混合功能计算 (HSE06 + SOC) 提供了更准确的卤化物钙钛矿电子特性，但成本要高得多。在这里，我们评估低成本 PBE 信息是否改善了 HSE06 + SOC 带隙和光谱有限最大效率 (SLME) 的机器学习 (ML) 预测。使用 243 个纯和混合 ABX₃ 钙钛矿的公共数据集，我们使用嵌套交叉验证与随机、化学系统分组和 B 位点分组分割来比较 7 个 ML 模型系列。 PBE 输入…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

与半局域 PBE 计算相比，具有自旋轨道耦合的混合功能计算 (HSE06 + SOC) 提供了更准确的卤化物钙钛矿电子特性，但成本要高得多。在这里，我们评估低成本 PBE 信息是否改善了 HSE06 + SOC 带隙和光谱有限最大效率 (SLME) 的机器学习 (ML) 预测。使用 243 个纯和混合 ABX₃ 钙钛矿的公共数据集，我们使用嵌套交叉验证与随机、化学系统分组和 B 位点分组分割来比较 7 个 ML 模型系列。 PBE 输入显着改善了 SLME 预测：化学系统分组分割的平均绝对误差从 0.68 个百分点降至 0.45 个百分点，最佳模型达到 0.36 个百分点。与直接学习相比，Δ-学习没有任何优势。然而，当训练中缺少 B 位点化学反应时，表现就会恶化，并且保形间隔会变得校准不足。对源标签的审核进一步表明，HSE06 + SOC SLME 受到 HSE06 + SOC 带隙的强烈约束，并接近 16% 附近的饱和度，因为报告的光谱是通过移动 PBE 吸收光谱生成的。这些发现凸显了 PBE 辅助机器学习在钙钛矿特性多保真度预测方面的价值和局限性。

English Abstract

Hybrid-functional calculations with spin–orbit coupling (HSE06 + SOC) provide more accurate electronic properties of halide perovskites than semilocal PBE calculations but are substantially more expensive. Here, we assess whether low-cost PBE information improves machine-learning (ML) prediction of HSE06 + SOC band gaps and spectroscopic limited maximum efficiency (SLME). Using a public dataset of 243 pure and mixed ABX₃ perovskites, we compare seven ML model families using nested cross-validation with random, chemical-system-grouped, and B-site-grouped splits. PBE inputs substantially improve SLME prediction: the mean absolute error decreases from 0.68 to 0.45 percentage points on chemical-system-grouped splits, with the best model achieving 0.36 percentage points. Δ-learning provides no advantage over direct learning. However, performance deteriorates when the B-site chemistry is absent from training, and conformal intervals become under-calibrated. An audit of the source labels further shows that the HSE06 + SOC SLME is strongly constrained by the HSE06 + SOC band gap and approaches a saturation near 16%, because the reported spectrum was generated by shifting the PBE absorption spectrum. These findings highlight both the value and limitations of PBE-assisted ML for multi-fidelity prediction of perovskite properties.

CsPbI3 中碘空位对相互作用的应变相关动力学：DFT 和机器学习分子动力学研究#

原题：Strain-dependent Dynamics of Interactions of Iodine-vacancy pairs in CsPbI3: A DFT and Machine-Learning Molecular Dynamics Study
作者：Donggeun Kang、Seongju Lee、Yonggoon Jeon。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-10
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：碘空位迁移和缔合是卤化物钙钛矿结构稳定性的重要因素。尽管相邻的空位被认为可以形成能量上有利的对，但它们在有限温度晶格变形下的稳定性和重构仍不清楚。在这里，我们结合机器学习分子动力学 (MLMD) 和密度泛函理论 (DFT) 来研究各向同性和双轴应变下倾斜 CsPbI3 中的邻近碘空位。 MLMD 轨迹揭示了短时跳跃分数对应变的非单调依赖性，强各向同性压缩有利于点状分离，而张力促进了成对保留中继跳跃。值得注意的是，双轴压缩产生明显的方向相…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、density functional theory、perovskite、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

碘空位迁移和缔合是卤化物钙钛矿结构稳定性的重要因素。尽管相邻的空位被认为可以形成能量上有利的对，但它们在有限温度晶格变形下的稳定性和重构仍不清楚。在这里，我们结合机器学习分子动力学 (MLMD) 和密度泛函理论 (DFT) 来研究各向同性和双轴应变下倾斜 CsPbI3 中的邻近碘空位。 MLMD 轨迹揭示了短时跳跃分数对应变的非单调依赖性，强各向同性压缩有利于点状分离，而张力促进了成对保留中继跳跃。值得注意的是，双轴压缩产生明显的方向相关响应：最初与压缩平面对齐的空位对优先经历分离，而垂直于平面的空位对主要表现出中继跳跃。使用 DFT 和 SevenNet-0 进行互补静态计算，对所有检查的配置产生排斥相互作用。这些发现强调了有限温度动力学和应变相关的局部几何形状在理解卤化物钙钛矿中的空位对稳定性和重构方面的重要性。

English Abstract

Iodine-vacancy migration and association are important factors in the structural stability of halide perovskites. Although neighboring vacancies have been suggested to form energetically favorable pairs, their stability and reconfiguration under finite-temperature lattice deformation remain unclear. Here, we combine machine-learning molecular dynamics (MLMD) and density functional theory (DFT) to investigate neighboring iodine vacancies in tilted CsPbI3 under isotropic and biaxial strain. The MLMD trajectories reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of short-time hopping fractions on strain, with strong isotropic compression favoring point-like separation and tension promoting pair-preserving relay hopping. Notably, biaxial compression produces distinct orientation-dependent responses: vacancy pairs initially aligned with the compressed plane preferentially undergo separation, whereas those perpendicular to the plane predominantly exhibit relay hopping. Complementary static calculations using DFT and the SevenNet-0 potential yield repulsive interactions for all configurations examined. These findings highlight the importance of finite-temperature dynamics and strain-dependent local geometry in understanding vacancy-pair stability and reconfiguration in halide perovskites.

07

多铁性 Ba3HoRu2O9 中的磁耦合和晶格激发：中子散射、拉曼光谱和机器学习声子计算的见解#

原题：Coupled Magnetic and Lattice Excitations in Multiferroic Ba3HoRu2O9: Insights from Neutron Scattering, Raman Spectroscopy, and Machine-Learned Phonon Calculations
作者：Ekta Kushwaha、Sayan Ghosh、Gourab Roy、Mohit Kumar、Manh Duc Le、Jhuma Shannigrahi 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-10

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：multiferroic、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

相关 4d-4f 多铁氧化物的非弹性中子散射 (INS) 谱很难解释，因为磁场、晶体场和晶格激发在能量上经常重叠。由于 Ru2O9 分子单元和局域 Ho3+ 矩的共存，六方 6H 钙钛矿 Ba3HoRu2O9 为研究这种相互作用提供了一个理想的平台。为了确定这些不同激发的贡献，我们将 INS 与线性自旋波计算、晶体场分析、拉曼光谱和机器学习力场 (MLFF) 声子计算结合起来。仅在低温磁相中观察到的低于 6.2 meV 的磁激发是通过线性自旋波理论再现的，具有相当大小的内二聚体 Ru-Ru 和 Ru-Ho 交换以及 Ho 单离子各向异性，因此与耦合 Ru-Ho 磁网络的集体自旋波激发一致。在较高能量下，观察到以 20、39、70 和 90 meV 附近为中心的广泛激发，这些激发持续远高于磁有序温度。 39 meV 激励的磁性部分通过减去 Bose 校正的高温数据进行隔离，遵循 Ho3+ 磁性形状因数。基于史蒂文斯形式主义的晶体场计算在该能量范围内放置了一组强 Ho3+ 跃迁，而拉曼光谱和 MLFF 声子计算则识别出具有可比较能量的光学声子。总之，这些互补的结果表明，39 meV 附近的宽 INS 特征是由重叠的 Ho3+ 晶体场激发和晶格振动引起的。这些发现建立了一个微观框架，用于理解这种多铁性 4d-4f 化合物中自旋、晶体场和晶格自由度之间的相互作用

English Abstract

Inelastic neutron scattering (INS) spectra of correlated 4d-4f multiferroic oxides are difficult to interpret because magnetic, crystal-field, and lattice excitations frequently overlap in energy. The hexagonal 6H perovskite Ba3HoRu2O9 provides an ideal platform to investigate this interplay owing to the coexistence of Ru2O9 molecular units and localized Ho3+ moments. To identify the contributions from these different excitations, we combine INS with linear spin-wave calculations, crystal field analysis, Raman spectroscopy, and machine-learned force field (MLFF) phonon calculations. A magnetic excitation below 6.2 meV, observed only in the low-temperature magnetic phase, is reproduced by linear spin-wave theory with intradimer Ru-Ru and Ru-Ho exchange of comparable size and a Ho single-ion anisotropy, and is therefore consistent with a collective spin-wave excitation of the coupled Ru-Ho magnetic network. At higher energies, broad excitations centered near 20, 39, 70, and 90 meV are observed that persist far above the magnetic ordering temperatures. The magnetic part of the 39 meV excitation, isolated by subtracting the Bose-corrected high-temperature data, follows the Ho3+ magnetic form factor. Crystal-field calculations based on the Stevens formalism place a group of strong Ho3+ transitions in this energy range, while Raman spectroscopy and MLFF phonon calculations identify optical phonons with comparable energies. Together, these complementary results show that the broad INS feature near 39 meV arises from overlapping Ho3+ crystal-field excitations and lattice vibrations. These findings establish a microscopic framework for understanding the interplay between spin, crystal-field, and lattice degrees of freedom in this multiferroic 4d-4f compound

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

层状范德华半导体 CrSBr 将固有磁序与半导体和光学特性相结合，使其成为多功能电子和光子应用的有前途的材料。然而，由于相关的长度和时间尺度，了解缺陷和结构转变如何在实验相关条件下影响其性能对于第一原理模拟来说仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们开发了 Cr-S-Br 系统的机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 并对其进行了基准测试，从而实现了 CrSBr 的大规模原子模拟，其精度接近密度泛函理论 (DFT)。电荷感知变体 qNEP 在预测晶格常数、形成焓、空位能量和 o-CrSBr 的各向异性压力响应方面实现了接近 DFT 的精度。微推弹性带计算表明，预先存在的 S 空位可将 Cr 弗兰克尔对迁移势垒降低高达 1.1 eV，为束诱导 Cr 重新分布和结构转变提供原子机制。位移阈值计算进一步表明，Br 是最容易受到电子束引起的位移影响的物质，从而确定了去除不同原子物质的相对容易程度。总之，这些结果建立了一个经过验证的计算框架，用于研究 CrSBr 中的原子级无序和结构转变，为未来磁光电子应用的材料缺陷工程奠定了基础。

English Abstract

The layered van der Waals semiconductor CrSBr combines intrinsic magnetic order with semiconducting and optical properties, making it a promising material for multifunctional electronic and photonic applications. Understanding how defects and structural transformations affect its properties under experimentally relevant conditions, however, remains challenging for first-principles simulations due to the relevant length and time scales. Here, we develop and benchmark machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for the Cr-S-Br system, enabling large-scale atomistic simulations of CrSBr with accuracy approaching that of density functional theory (DFT). The charge-aware variant qNEP achieves near-DFT accuracy in predicting lattice constants, formation enthalpies, vacancy energetics, and the anisotropic pressure response of o-CrSBr. Nudged elastic band calculations reveal that pre-existing S vacancies lower the Cr Frenkel-pair migration barrier by up to 1.1 eV, providing an atomistic mechanism for beam-induced Cr redistribution and structural transformations. Displacement threshold calculations further show that Br is the most susceptible species to electron-beam-induced displacement, identifying the relative ease of removing different atomic species. Together, these results establish a validated computational framework for investigating atomic-scale disorder and structural transformations in CrSBr, providing a basis for defect engineering in materials for future magneto-optoelectronic applications.

新兴的低维拓扑动力学决定了十亿参数神经网络中的泛化和逃逸机制#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

现代超参数化神经网络的显着泛化挑战了经典统计学习的界限。在这里，我们确定十亿参数模型中的优化轨迹不会均匀地采样环境维度 R^N；相反，它们被动态地约束到低维执行子流形 Mₑₓₑc（根据经验观察到的有效维度 dₑff le 12）。通过为参数空间配备正则化的 Hessian 度量 gᵢⱼ = nablaᵢ nablaⱼ L + ϵ δᵢⱼ 并分析损失子级别集上的随机 Langevin 扩散，我们得出了基于投影子空间持久同源性的严格拓扑泛化界限。此外，通过将莫尔斯指数跃迁与贝蒂数湮灭（β₁ → 0）联系起来，我们制定了一种精确的数学机制，用于在各向异性噪声下从严格鞍点中指数逃逸。我们在 80 多种架构（ResNet、Vision Transformers 和 7B LLM）中验证了我们的框架。为了将理论拓扑与计算可行性联系起来，我们通过严格限制在活动 Hessian 子空间内的随机 Lanczos 迭代来计算投影持久同源代理，从而解决了维数灾难。在此数学基础上，我们引入了拓扑自适应优化（Top-SGD 和 Top-AdamW）——一系列使用主动曲率签名动态调节学习率的包装算法。广泛的基准测试表明，在充分考虑所有计算和内存开销的情况下，ImageNet 上的总挂钟训练时间减少了 14.1%。代码可在 https://github.com/imslab-research/Topological-SGD 上公开获取。

English Abstract

The remarkable generalization of modern over-parameterized neural networks defies classical statistical learning bounds. Here we establish that optimization trajectories in billion-parameter models do not sample the ambient dimension R^N homogeneously; instead, they are dynamically constrained to a low-dimensional execution submanifold Mₑₓₑc (with empirically observed effective dimension dₑff le 12). By equipping the parameter space with a regularized Hessian metric gᵢⱼ = nablaᵢ nablaⱼ L + ϵ δᵢⱼ and analyzing stochastic Langevin diffusion on loss sublevel sets, we derive a rigorous Topological Generalization Bound grounded in projected subspace persistent homology. Furthermore, by linking Morse index transitions to Betti number annihilation (β₁ → 0), we formulate an exact mathematical mechanism for exponential escape from strict saddles under anisotropic noise. We validate our framework across 80+ architectures (ResNets, Vision Transformers, and 7B LLMs). To bridge theoretical topology with computational feasibility, we resolve the curse of dimensionality by computing projected persistent homology proxies via randomized Lanczos iterations restricted strictly within active Hessian subspaces. Building upon this mathematical foundation, we introduce Topological-Adaptive Optimization (Top-SGD and Top-AdamW)---a family of wrapper algorithms that dynamically modulate learning rates using active curvature signatures. Extensive benchmarks demonstrate a 14.1% reduction in total wall-clock training time on ImageNet with all computational and memory overheads fully accounted for. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/imslab-research/Topological-SGD.

2026 年拓扑深度学习挑战赛：缩小差距#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

本文介绍了第二届数据科学中的拓扑、代数和几何 (TAG-DS) 会议主办的 2026 年版拓扑深度学习挑战：弥合差距。之前的版本发展了拓扑深度学习 (TDL) 的建模和数据生态系统，而本版本则将图神经网络 (GNN) 和拓扑神经网络 (TNN) 并列放置在单个评估管道下。参与者在 TopoBench 中实现了最新的架构，其中 Track 1 涵盖 GNN，Track 2 涵盖 TNN。他们在 GraphUniverse 生成的共享综合基准上评估了这些实现，GraphUniverse 独立控制同质性、平均度和度分布重量。此次挑战赛收到了来自 34 个团队的 68 份合格参赛作品，涵盖图、单纯形、元胞、超图和组合领域。我们还首次在公共排行榜上跟踪提交的内容，我们在此进行总结和分析。我们的分析发现，提交的轨道之间的差异很大程度上依赖于任务：社区检测的平均准确度差距不大，而在三角形计数上，轨道 1 的平均误差是轨道 2 平均值的 1.85 倍。一些结构信息丰富的 GNN 在三角形计数方面也优于 Track 2 均值。

English Abstract

This paper describes the 2026 edition of the Topological Deep Learning Challenge: Bridging the Gap, hosted at the second Topology, Algebra, and Geometry in Data Science (TAG-DS) Conference. Where previous editions grew the modelling and data ecosystem of Topological Deep Learning (TDL), this edition set out to place Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and Topological Neural Networks (TNNs) side by side under a single evaluation pipeline. Participants implemented recent state-of-the-art architectures in TopoBench, with Track 1 covering GNNs and Track 2 covering TNNs. They evaluated these implementations on shared synthetic benchmarks generated with GraphUniverse, which independently controls homophily, average degree and degree-distribution heaviness. The challenge yielded 68 qualifying submissions from 34 teams, spanning the graph, simplicial, cellular, hypergraph and combinatorial domains. For the first time, submissions were also tracked on a public leaderboard, which we summarize and analyze here. Our analysis finds that the differences between the submitted tracks are strongly task-dependent: the mean accuracy gap is modest on community detection, while on triangle counting the Track 1 mean error is 1.85 times the Track 2 mean. Several structurally informed GNNs also outperform the Track 2 mean on triangle counting.

人工智能驱动的自旋哈密顿量中纠缠见证的发现#

原题：AI-Driven Discovery of Entanglement Witness in Spin Hamiltonians

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：spin hamiltonian。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

纠缠是量子科学和工程中的重要资源，但从实验可观测到的多体态中检测纠缠仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们介绍 EntNet，这是一个人工智能驱动的框架，用于发现自旋可观测量的紧凑组合，该组合可以准确区分由短程哈密顿量控制的一维自旋 1/2 链中的纠缠态和完全可分离的多体混合态。我们构建了高达四体阶的可测量泡利相关器的局部酉不变表示，稀疏地选择 16 个候选不变特征通道，并使用具有旋转位置嵌入的 Transformer 架构来学习它们的空间结构。仅使用这些低阶可观测值的期望值，EntNet 在经过认证的纠缠态上实现了 >98% 的召回率，在可分离态上的误报率接近于零。值得注意的是，它表现出跨系统尺寸和不同哈密顿家族（包括受挫和无序自旋模型）的可转移性，并且精度损失最小。除了检测之外，EntNet 通过揭示配对相关性的选择性空间组织，提供对纠缠检测的物理洞察，同时其有限温度决策边界跟踪 Jordan-Wigner 准粒子的激发间隙。这些结果建立了一个可转移和物理可解释的框架，用于从局部相关函数中数据驱动地发现纠缠见证。

English Abstract

Entanglement is a crucial resource in quantum science and engineering, but detecting it in many-body states from experimentally accessible observables remains challenging. Here we introduce EntNet, an AI-driven framework to discover compact combinations of spin observables that accurately distinguish entangled from fully separable many-body mixed states in one-dimensional spin-1/2 chains governed by short-range Hamiltonians. We construct local-unitary-invariant representations of measurable Pauli correlators up to four-body order, sparsely select 16 candidate invariant feature channels, and learn their spatial structure using a Transformer architecture with rotary positional embeddings. Using only expectation values of these low-order observables, EntNet achieves >98% recall on certified entangled states with near-zero false-positive rates on separable states. Remarkably, it exhibits transferability across system sizes and distinct Hamiltonian families, including frustrated and disordered spin models, with minimal loss of accuracy. Beyond detection, EntNet provides physical insight into entanglement detection by revealing a selective spatial organization of pair correlations, while its finite-temperature decision boundary tracks the excitation gap of Jordan-Wigner quasiparticles. These results establish a transferable and physically interpretable framework for data-driven discovery of entanglement witnesses from local correlation functions.

非平稳监督下的拓扑动力学：神经网络权值演化的持久同调#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究神经元权重点云的持久拓扑如何在稳定和非平稳的监督下演化。在 Fashion-MNIST 上训练相同的多层感知器，并使用正确的标签、训练标签向量的固定随机排列或每个时期的新排列。 5 个配对种子共享初始化和小批量顺序，产生 15 个经过 100 个 epoch 训练的模型。使用 22 个检查点的 H0 和 H1 Vietoris Rips 持久性对原始和行归一化传入重量云进行分析。正确标签模型平均真实标签测试准确率达到 89.39%；固定随机模型符合 67.94% 的指定训练目标，但没有推广到原始任务。正确标签模型的时间平均归一化 fc3 H1 持久性明显高于任一随机标签条件，但配置的精确比较在 Holm 校正后未达到常规显着性。使用拓扑进行早期状况分类的平均平衡准确度为 60.0%，使用传统诊断的平均平衡准确度为 66.7%，使用两者的平均平衡准确度为 60.0%。因此，在这种情况下没有证明增量预测改进。该存档包含所有 3,300 个配置的拓扑案例。解释受到五个种子、单一架构和数据集、282 个上限 H1 距离比较以及延迟瓶颈分析的限制。

English Abstract

We investigate how the persistent topology of neuron- weight point clouds evolves under stable and non-stationary supervision. An identical multilayer perceptron is trained on Fashion-MNIST with correct labels, a fixed random permutation of the training-label vector, or a fresh permutation every epoch. Five paired seeds share initialisation and minibatch order, yielding 15 models trained for 100 epochs. Raw and row-normalised incoming weight clouds are analysed with H0 and H1 Vietoris Rips persistence at 22 checkpoints. Correct-label models reach 89.39% mean true-label test accuracy; fixed-random models fit 67.94% of their assigned training targets without generalising to the original task. Correct-label models have descriptively greater time-averaged normalised fc3 H1 persistence than either random-label condition, but the configured exact comparisons do not reach conventional significance after Holm correction. Early condition classification achieves 60.0% mean balanced accuracy using topology, 66.7% using conventional diagnostics, and 60.0% using both. Thus, no incremental predictive improvement is demonstrated in this setting. The archive contains all 3,300 configured topology cases. Interpretation is limited by five seeds, a single architecture and dataset, 282 capped H1 distance comparisons, and deferred bottleneck analysis.

MOTIF：LLM 定义的材料分类法，用于通过不变特征选择进行 OOD 属性预测#

原题：MOTIF: LLM-Defined Material Taxonomies for OOD Property Prediction via Invariant Feature Selection
作者：Gwangseon Jang、Sieun Yun、Dong Ho Lee、Kyong-Ha Lee、Chanuk Lim、Jin Woo Choi 等 7 人。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-10

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分布外（OOD）泛化仍然是材料特性预测中的一个核心挑战，其中模型必须推断出训练分布之外的新化学物质。虽然大型语言模型 (LLM) 已展现出强大的科学推理能力，但它们在定量预测中的直接使用仍然有限。为了解决这个问题，我们系统地评估了 LLM 协助的多个角色，发现只有基于分类的分组才能在强大的 kNN+GBT 基线上持续提高 OOD 泛化能力。在此基础上，我们在此提出了 MOTIF，这是一个框架，它不是利用法学硕士作为预测因子，而是作为候选分类法的生成器，将材料划分到具有化学意义的环境中。这些分类法通过仅训练的伪 OOD 验证进行评估，并且仅保留那些提高泛化性的分类法。然后，在每个接受的分类法中应用不变特征选择，然后跨分类法进行门控聚合。我们对二维卤化物钙钛矿进行评估，其中带隙预测必须推广到不同的有机间隔基家族。在此基准测试中，MOTIF 将平均 OOD MAE 从 0.122 eV 降低至 0.111 eV。在冻结的开发/测试协议下，可部署配置进一步将误差从 0.123 eV 改善到 0.117 eV（电子带隙），从 0.181 eV 改善到 0.173 eV（光学带隙）。除了这项主要任务之外，我们还验证了其他材料数据集的可转移性，包括 3D 钙钛矿太阳能电池效率和基于结构分割的 Matbench 基准。在跨领域中，MOTIF 不断提高分布变化下的鲁棒性，优于专家定义的分组、基于规则的启发式方法和聚类基线，同时保持与强大的经典选择方法的竞争力。这些结果表明，法学硕士最有效的不是作为预测工具，而是作为发现数据结构的工具。

English Abstract

Out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization remains a central challenge in materials property prediction, where models must extrapolate to novel chemistries beyond the training distribution. While large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong scientific reasoning, their direct use for quantitative prediction remains limited. To address this, we systematically evaluate multiple roles for LLM assistance, finding that only taxonomy-based grouping consistently improves OOD generalization over a strong kNN+GBT baseline. Building on this, we here propose MOTIF, a framework that leverages LLMs not as predictors, but as generators of candidate taxonomies that partition materials into chemically meaningful environments. These taxonomies are evaluated via training-only pseudo-OOD validation, and only those that improve generalization are retained. Invariant feature selection is then applied within each accepted taxonomy, followed by gated aggregation across taxonomies. We evaluate on 2D halide perovskites, where bandgap prediction must generalize across distinct organic spacer families. On this benchmark, MOTIF reduces mean OOD MAE from 0.122 to 0.111 eV. Under a frozen dev/test protocol, the deployable configuration further improves errors from 0.123 to 0.117 eV (electronic bandgap) and from 0.181 to 0.173 eV (optical bandgap). Beyond this primary task, we validate transferability across additional materials datasets, including 3D perovskite solar cell efficiencies and Matbench benchmarks with structure-based splits. Across domains, MOTIF consistently improves robustness under distribution shift, outperforming expert-defined groupings, rule-based heuristics, and clustering baselines, while remaining competitive with strong classical selection methods. These results demonstrate that LLMs are most effective not as predictors, but as tools for discovering structure in data.

原题：Partition of Unity Data-driven Finite Element Method for Anisotropic Diffusion in Magnetized Plasmas

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁化等离子体输运过程的数值模拟具有极端的各向异性，其中沿磁场线的扩散可能比垂直方向的扩散大多个数量级。这种各向异性会导致薄而陡的边界层和数值污染，而标准有限元方法在不进行严格的网格细化的情况下很难解决这些问题。在本文中，我们介绍了 Unity 数据驱动有限元法 (PU-DDFEM) 的划分，这是一种用于各向异性扩散的数据驱动局部模型简化方法。该方法采用自下而上的结构来实现完整的离线在线拆分。我们对局部磁场和边界条件进行参数化，以生成捕捉等离子体约束基本物理原理的快照空间。通过局部谱问题提取主模式以识别代表性特征。然后使用统一划分将这些模式映射到全局域，以形成一致的近似空间。这确保了基函数在不同的全局磁配置中完全可重用，从而显着减少大规模等离子体模拟的在线计算开销。

English Abstract

Numerical simulation of transport processes in magnetized plasmas is characterized by extreme anisotropy, where diffusion along magnetic field lines can be many orders of magnitude larger than in the perpendicular direction. This anisotropy leads to thin, steep boundary layers and numerical pollution that standard finite element methods struggle to resolve without prohibitive mesh refinement. In this paper, we introduce the Partition of Unity Data-Driven Finite Element Method (PU-DDFEM), a data-driven local model reduction approach, for anisotropic diffusion. The method utilizes a bottom-up construction to enable a complete offline-online splitting. We parametrize the local magnetic field and boundary conditions to generate a snapshot space that captures the essential physics of plasma confinement. Dominant modes are extracted via a local spectral problem to identify representative features. These modes are then mapped to the global domain using a partition of unity to form a conforming approximation space. This ensures that the basis functions are fully reusable across different global magnetic configurations, significantly reducing the online computational overhead for large-scale plasma simulations.

TopoSHAP：Shapley 用于可解释且高效的拓扑深度学习#

原题：TopoSHAP: Shapley for Explainable and Efficient Topological Deep Learning
作者：Mathilde Papillon、Nina Miolane。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-09

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

拓扑深度学习 (TDL) 将图（例如分子或社交网络）的学习扩展到高阶结构（例如分子中的环或社交网络中的基团）。解释哪些结构驱动模型的预测，以及哪些架构组件对其性能最重要，仍然具有挑战性。我们引入了 TopoSHAP，这是一个高效且通用的解释框架，可以解决跨拓扑域和模型架构的问题。 TopoSHAP-cell 将 Shapley 值分配给拓扑数据单元并提取紧凑的解释。这些解释在 GraphXAI 基准数据上匹配或优于基于图形的对应解释，同时运行速度提高了 112 倍。他们还揭示了有利的拓扑特征设计，从而在解释恢复和预测准确性方面取得了可测量的改进。在架构方面，TopoSHAP-neighbor 解释了邻域如何对模型性能做出贡献，并将这些评估转化为向后消除搜索，从而在训练早期识别更小的、有竞争力的架构。这比传统 TDL 训练扫描便宜 11.3 倍。

English Abstract

Topological deep learning (TDL) extends learning on graphs, such as molecules or social networks, to higher-order structures, such as rings in a molecule or groups in a social network. Explaining which of these structures drive a model's predictions, and which architectural components matter most for its performance, remains challenging. We introduce TopoSHAP, an efficient and general explanation framework that addresses both questions across topological domains and model architectures. TopoSHAP-cell assigns Shapley values to topological data cells and extracts compact explanations. These explanations match or outperform graph-based counterparts on GraphXAI benchmark data while running up to 112× faster. They also reveal advantageous topological feature design, leading to measured improvements in explanation recovery and predictive accuracy. On the architecture side, TopoSHAP-neighbor explains how neighborhoods contribute to model performance, and turns these evaluations into a backward-elimination search that identifies smaller, competitive architectures early in training. This is up to 11.3× cheaper than traditional TDL training sweeps.

基于 DFT 的可微逆材料设计框架#

原题：A Differentiable DFT-Based Framework for Inverse Materials Design
作者：Kohei Ishii、Hisazumi Akai、Tetsuya Fukushima、Hikari Shinya、Hyuga Hosoi、Noritsugu Sakuma 等 7 人。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-10

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

发现具有目标特性的固态材料仍然是计算材料科学的核心挑战。现有的数据驱动方法依赖于训练数据，并且在训练分布之外的泛化效果可能很差。在这里，我们开发了一个不需要训练数据的第一原理逆向设计框架。它将反向模式自动微分 (AD) 集成到 Korringa--Kohn--Rostoker 方法中，并结合相干势近似 (KKR-CPA)，其中元素浓度作为连续优化变量。反向模式 AD 在一次向后传递中计算所有浓度梯度，从而能够对数十个候选元素进行优化。 KKR-CPA 中可计算的任何可微量都可以作为优化目标。我们在永磁体和半金属设计中证明了这种普遍性，分别确定了候选材料，例如 (Nd₀.₉₈₃Pr₀.₀₁)(Co₀.₇2Fe₀.2₈)2Fe₃ 和 FeMn(As₀.₈₃P₀.₁₇)。我们还合成了在优化过程中选择的五种 SmCo₅ 基成分的材料，以获得更高的磁化强度。他们测量的磁化强度与计算值非常吻合。我们的框架提供了设计具有目标特性的材料的第一原理途径。

English Abstract

Discovering solid-state materials with target properties remains a central challenge in computational materials science. Existing data-driven approaches rely on training data and may generalize poorly outside the training distribution. Here we develop a first-principles inverse-design framework that requires no training data. It integrates reverse-mode automatic differentiation (AD) into the Korringa--Kohn--Rostoker method combined with the coherent potential approximation (KKR-CPA), in which elemental concentrations serve as continuous optimization variables. Reverse-mode AD computes all concentration gradients in one backward pass, enabling optimization over dozens of candidate elements. Any differentiable quantity computable within KKR-CPA can serve as an optimization objective. We demonstrate this generality in permanent-magnet and half-metal design, identifying candidates such as (Nd₀.₉₈₃Pr₀.₀₁₇)(Co₀.₇₂Fe₀.₂₈)₂Fe₃ and FeMn(As₀.₈₃P₀.₁₇), respectively. We also synthesized materials with five SmCo₅-based compositions selected during optimization for higher magnetization. Their measured magnetization agreed closely with calculated values. Our framework offers a first-principles route to designing materials with target properties.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、interatomic potential、perovskite。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

随着铁电器件接近纳米尺度，它们的行为对边界条件变得越来越敏感。在钙钛矿氧化物纳米电容器中，外延应变和静电屏蔽是公认的控制参数。然后，虽然预计有助于铁电超薄膜的外延稳定，但电极的晶格动力学状态尚未被隔离为独立的调谐旋钮。在这里，我们将这种探索不足的耦合界面声子偏置称为耦合界面声子偏置，电极的声子能量景观通过其共享界面重塑相邻铁电体的声子能量景观。利用第一原理精确的机器学习原子间势和分子动力学模拟，我们研究了与金属钙钛矿氧化物接触的对称 BiFeO{}₃ 纳米电容器中的这种机制。通过独立控制电极八面体倾斜景观的方向和振幅，同时保持传统的弹性和电边界条件固定，我们表明电极的声子能量景观可以改变竞争的 BiFeO{}₃ 相的稳定性。特别是，这使我们能够改变铁电转变温度，甚至在环境条件下诱导类似反铁电的切换。因此，我们展示了界面声子偏置作为一种补充且强大的策略来修改和优化超薄外延膜的性能。

English Abstract

As ferroelectric devices approach the nanometer scale, their behavior becomes increasingly sensitive to boundary conditions. In perovskite oxide nanocapacitors, epitaxial strain and electrostatic screening are well-established control parameters. Then, while expected to contribute to the epitaxial stabilization of ferroelectric ultrathin films, the lattice-dynamical state of the electrodes has not been isolated as an independent tuning knob. Here we call this poorly explored coupling interfacial phonon bias, whereby the electrodes' phonon energy landscape reshapes that of the adjacent ferroelectric across their shared interface. Using first-principles-accurate machine-learned interatomic potentials and molecular-dynamics simulations, we examine this mechanism in symmetric BiFeO{}₃ nanocapacitors in contact with a metallic perovskite oxide. By independently controlling the orientatio and amplitude of the electrodes' octahedral-tilt landscape, while keeping conventional elastic and electric boundary conditions fixed, we show that the electrodes' phonon energy landscape can modify the stability of competing BiFeO{}₃ phases. In particular, this allows us to shift the ferroelectric transition temperature and even induce antiferroelectric-like switching at ambient conditions. We thus showcase interfacial phonon bias as a complementary and powerful strategy to modify and optimize the properties of ultrathin epitaxial films.

作者：Yinan Wang、Teruyasu Mizoguchi。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-09

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、sliding ferroelectrics、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

滑动铁电体中电场驱动的极化切换取决于周围结构如何适应局部层间运动。然而，变化矢量场下的滑动、畴壁（DW）传播和集体域演化之间的关系仍不清楚。在这里，我们表明局部对称性控制着场感生力和层间滑动之间的耦合，而 DW 连接性则决定了局部运动如何促进网络域转换。这些效应在三个结构尺度上进行了研究，从单域和孤立的 DW 到重建的莫尔网络。我们利用分子动力学和机器学习原子间势（结合了场相关电焓）研究双层六方氮化硼 (h-BN)。最初的单域双层通过定向起皱和层间滑动进行切换，而沿受约束的平坦路径的势垒保持有限。预先存在的 DW 以其剪切和拉伸特性控制的速度传播，并且附加的面内场根据壁的方向调整壁的速度和宽度。在重建的三角莫尔网络中，共享结点和边界曲率介导场去除或反转后域的演变和恢复。最引人注目的是，旋转的面内场平移层并将莫尔节点移动约 1 AA，但 AB/BA 域面积比没有可检测到的变化，因为三重连通性抵消了各个壁的贡献。我们将这种响应称为极化无声滑动。这些结果将局部滑动、DW 平移和网域转换区分为对电场的不同响应，并确定局部结构对称性和 DW 连接性如何塑造其选择性控制。

English Abstract

Electric-field-driven polarization switching in sliding ferroelectrics depends on how local interlayer motion is accommodated by the surrounding structure. Yet the relationship between sliding, domain-wall (DW) propagation, and collective domain evolution under changing vector fields remains unclear. Here, we show that local symmetry governs the coupling between field-induced forces and interlayer sliding, while DW connectivity determines how local motion contributes to net domain conversion. These effects are examined across three structural scales, from monodomains and isolated DWs to reconstructed moire networks. We investigate bilayer hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN) using molecular dynamics with a machine-learning interatomic potential that incorporates field-dependent electric enthalpy. An initially monodomain bilayer switches through directional wrinkling and interlayer sliding, while the barrier along a constrained flat path remains finite. Pre-existing DWs propagate with velocities governed by their shear and tensile character, and an additional in-plane field tunes wall velocity and width according to its orientation. In reconstructed triangular moire networks, shared junctions and boundary curvature mediate domain evolution and recovery after field removal or reversal. Most strikingly, a rotating in-plane field translates the layers and displaces the moiré nodes by approximately 1 AA yet leaves the AB/BA domain-area ratio without detectable change, because threefold connectivity cancels the contributions of individual walls. We term this response polarization-silent sliding. These results distinguish local sliding, DW translation, and net domain conversion as different responses to electric fields and establish how local structural symmetry and DW connectivity shape their selective control.

竞争八面体倾斜和 B 位偏心控制 CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃ 钙钛矿合金的相图#

原题：Competing Octahedral Tilting and B-Site Off-Centering Govern the Phase Diagram of CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃ Perovskite Alloys
作者：Apinya Ngoipala、Erik Fransson、Paul Erhart、Julia Wiktor。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-13

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于Ge和Sn的无铅卤化物钙钛矿避免了Pb的毒性，但这两种阳离子有利于不同的结构扭曲。具有立体化学活性的 Ge 4s² 孤对电子驱动极性 B 位点偏心，而较大的 Sn²⁺ 阳离子则有利于八面体倾斜。在这里，我们使用大规模分子动力学模拟以及在混合功能数据上训练的电荷感知机器学习原子间势，绘制了这两种不稳定性如何在 CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃ 的整个组成范围内竞争。在低温下，该合金表现出 x=0.60 和 0.65 之间的同形相界，将铁电单斜 Cc 相与具有混合反极性和极性偏心的单斜 P2₁ 相分开，倾斜模式和偏心顺序均不连续变化。组成-温度相图再现了两个端元的已知转变序列和温度，并揭示了边界附近的中间四方 I4/mcm 相，该相在任一母体化合物中都没有对应物。高于转变温度时，B 阳离子仍然局部偏心，但方向无序。低温下，边界处的结构转变导致带隙跳跃，而在 350 K 时，带隙随 Sn 含量的增加而平滑减小。这些结果表明 B 位混合是调节无铅卤化物钙钛矿中极性和旋转顺序的一种手段。

English Abstract

Lead-free halide perovskites based on Ge and Sn avoid the toxicity of Pb, but the two cations favor different structural distortions. The stereochemically active Ge 4s² lone pair drives polar B-site off-centering, whereas the larger Sn²⁺ cation favors octahedral tilting. Here, we map how these two instabilities compete across the full composition range of CsGe₁₋ₓSnₓBr₃ using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations with a charge-aware machine-learned interatomic potential trained on hybrid-functional data. At low temperature the alloy exhibits a morphotropic phase boundary between x=0.60 and 0.65 that separates a ferroelectric monoclinic Cc phase from a monoclinic P2₁ phase with mixed antipolar and polar off-centering, with both the tilt pattern and the off-centering order changing discontinuously. The composition-temperature phase diagram reproduces the known transition sequences and temperatures of both end members and reveals an intermediate tetragonal I4/mcm phase near the boundary that has no counterpart in either parent compound. Above the transition temperatures the B cations remain locally off-centered but orientationally disordered. The structural transition at the boundary causes a jump in the band gap at low temperature, whereas at 350 K the gap decreases smoothly with Sn content. These results establish B-site mixing as a means to tune polar and rotational order in lead-free halide perovskites.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、first-principles calculations、graph neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

由集中太阳热驱动的两步热化学水分解是一种可扩展的制备可再生氢的途径，但它需要氧化物的氧空位形成能平衡容易的还原和有利的再氧化。钙钛矿固溶体可以调节这种平衡，但体积化学计量与局部缺陷能量学之间的关系仍然知之甚少。在这里，我们通过将第一性原理计算与覆盖范围约束的特殊准随机结构方法相结合，绘制了 Ca-Ce-Ti-Mn (CCTM) 钙钛矿中的氧空位形成能量学，该方法实现了所有 15 个对称性不同的氧最近邻环境，一个可解释的晶体特征模型，其拟合系数直接编码底层的 Born-Haber 热化学，以及一个微调的缺陷图神经网络。局部 B 位化学主导了氧空位形成能 Eᵥ：根据局部 Ce 含量，改变最近邻 Mn 分数会使 Eᵥ 偏移 1.0-1.5 eV，而 A 位 Ce 变化则贡献较小的、与 Mn 相关的 0.2-0.6 eV 偏移。因此，如果短程 B 位阳离子顺序能够通过加工建立并在动力学上保留，那么它是在不改变本体组成的情况下调节氧化还原性能的候选方法。组成空间图确定了 Ce/Mn 平衡区域（A 位上约 29-33% Ce，B 位约 58-67% Mn），将目标 Eᵥ 窗口内的高比例空位位与相稳定性和溶解度相结合，其预测的氧化还原循环容量匹配或超过 1350 ^circC 的二氧化铈基准，而不是大约 1600 ^circC 二氧化铈所需的氧化还原循环容量。对三种 CCTM 组合物的测量表明，在接近模型条件的方案下，循环容量随 Ce 含量单调增加。设计规则预计将转移到相关的钙钛矿系列。

English Abstract

Two-step thermochemical water splitting driven by concentrated solar heat is a scalable route to renewable hydrogen, but it requires oxides whose oxygen-vacancy formation energies balance facile reduction with favorable reoxidation. Perovskite solid solutions can tune this balance, but the relationship between bulk stoichiometry and local defect energetics remains poorly understood. Here we map oxygen-vacancy formation energetics across Ca-Ce-Ti-Mn (CCTM) perovskites by combining first-principles calculations with a coverage-constrained special quasirandom structure approach that realizes all fifteen symmetry-distinct oxygen nearest-neighbor environments, an interpretable crystal-feature model whose fitted coefficients directly encode the underlying Born-Haber thermochemistry, and a fine-tuned defect graph neural network. Local B-site chemistry dominates the oxygen-vacancy formation energy Eᵥ: varying the nearest-neighbor Mn fraction shifts Eᵥ by 1.0-1.5 eV depending on local Ce content, whereas A-site Ce variation contributes a smaller, Mn-dependent shift of 0.2-0.6 eV. Short-range B-site cation order, if it can be established and kinetically retained through processing, is therefore a candidate means of tuning redox performance without changing bulk composition. Composition-space maps identify a Ce/Mn-balanced region (~29-33% Ce on the A site and 58-67% Mn on the B site) combining a high fraction of vacancy sites within the targeted Eᵥ window with phase stability and solubility, whose predicted redox cycle capacity matches or exceeds the ceria benchmark at 1350 ^circC rather than the roughly 1600 ^circC ceria requires. Measurements on three CCTM compositions show cycle capacity increasing monotonically with Ce content under protocols close to the model conditions. The design rules are expected to transfer to related perovskite families.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Octopus 是一个开源包，在真实空间、实时公式中实现时间相关密度泛函理论。在此，我们报告自上次向电子结构软件专题期刊投稿以来的进展 [J.化学。物理。 152, 124119 (2020)]。新的交换相关泛函扩展了可处理系统的范围，而相对论和全电子方法的进步则为核心电子光谱打开了大门。晶格动力学的新功能——应力张量计算、几何优化、机器学习力场支持和声子计算——实现了光诱导晶格动力学的完全从头开始实时描述。我们还展示了超越偶极近似的进展，更忠实地捕捉光与物质的相互作用。激子动力学的改进方法补充了一系列算法进步——新的传播方案、虚时间演化和处理超过十亿点的重构网格——这些共同大大提高了性能和可扩展性。结合 AMD GPU 支持和现代化构建系统等技术改进，这些开发将 Octopus 巩固为多功能高性能平台，用于探索分子、纳米结构和扩展系统中的电子激发、光与物质相互作用以及非平衡动力学。

English Abstract

Octopus is an open-source package implementing time-dependent density functional theory in a real-space, real-time formulation. Here we report on the developments since our previous contribution to the Special Topic Issue on Electronic Structure Software [J. Chem. Phys. 152, 124119 (2020)]. New exchange-correlation functionals extend the range of systems that can be treated, while advances in relativistic and all-electron methods open the door to core-electron spectroscopy. New capabilities for lattice dynamics - stress-tensor calculation, geometry optimization, support for machine-learned force fields, and phonon calculations - enable a fully ab initio description of light-induced lattice dynamics in real time. We also present developments that move beyond the dipole approximation, capturing light-matter interaction more faithfully. Improved methods for exciton dynamics complement a set of algorithmic advances - new propagation schemes, imaginary-time evolution, and a refactored grid handling more than a billion points - that together substantially improve performance and scalability. Combined with technical improvements such as AMD GPU support and a modernized build system, these developments consolidate Octopus as a versatile, high-performance platform for exploring electronic excitations, light-matter interaction, and nonequilibrium dynamics in molecules, nanostructures, and extended systems.

PINNverse 遇上乔丹——维格纳：学习长自旋链的哈密顿量#

原题：PINNverse meets Jordan--Wigner: learning the Hamiltonian of long spin chains
作者：Gubio G De Lima。arXiv · physics，2026-11-11

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：逆物理信息神经网络 (PINNverse) 从测量的期望值中学习量子哈密顿量的参数，但网络输出随着量子位数量 N 的增加而增长为 4^N。我们表明，对于具有最近邻交换相互作用的自旋链，Jordan-Wigner 变换消除了这种指数成本：动力学在 N×N 单体密度矩阵上闭合，因此网络只需要 N² 输出，并且数据是在五个全局设置中测量的局部可观测量。寄生 ZZ 耦合（串扰）通过时间相关的 Hartree--Fock 闭包包含在内。利用合成数…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

逆物理信息神经网络 (PINNverse) 从测量的期望值中学习量子哈密顿量的参数，但网络输出随着量子位数量 N 的增加而增长为 4^N。我们表明，对于具有最近邻交换相互作用的自旋链，Jordan-Wigner 变换消除了这种指数成本：动力学在 N×N 单体密度矩阵上闭合，因此网络只需要 N² 输出，并且数据是在五个全局设置中测量的局部可观测量。寄生 ZZ 耦合（串扰）通过时间相关的 Hartree--Fock 闭包包含在内。利用合成数据，同一网络首先求解运动方程，然后恢复最多 50 个量子位的链的哈密顿参数，学习到的耦合优于 0.1%。通过从网络估计开始的直接最小二乘拟合来消除长链的局部频率中的误差。

English Abstract

Inverse physics-informed neural networks (PINNverse) learn the parameters of a quantum Hamiltonian from measured expectation values, but the network output grows as 4^N with the number of qubits N. We show that, for spin chains with nearest-neighbor exchange interactions, the Jordan-Wigner transformation removes this exponential cost: the dynamics closes on an N× N one-body density matrix, so the network needs only N² outputs and the data are local observables measured in five global settings. Parasitic ZZ couplings (crosstalk) are included through a time-dependent Hartree--Fock closure. With synthetic data, the same network first solves the equation of motion and then recovers the Hamiltonian parameters of chains of up to 50 qubits, with couplings learned to better than 0.1%. Errors in the local frequencies of long chains are removed by a direct least-squares fit started from the network estimate.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

模量平台被广泛认为是均聚物熔体中拓扑缠结的标志，并已用于识别半柔性共轭聚合物中的缠结。使用区域随机聚（3-烷基噻吩）作为模型系统，我们表明它们的平台弹性通过与链段和末端动力学解耦的阿伦尼乌斯过程松弛，在机械扰动下持续重组，并随着侧链长度系统地变化。这些行为很难与纯粹的拓扑纠缠图相协调，而是支持由可逆链间关联主导的瞬态网络，与共轭主链片段之间的 π-π 相互作用一致。因此，结果表明，平台弹性本身并不能唯一地识别共轭聚合物中的拓扑缠结。更广泛地说，他们将链间缔合的强度和寿命确定为分子设计参数，将主链堆积与共轭聚合物的应力松弛、粘弹性和可加工性联系起来，补充了它们在电子结构和电荷传输中的既定作用。

English Abstract

The modulus plateau is widely regarded as a hallmark of topological entanglement in homopolymer melts and has been used to identify entanglement in semiflexible conjugated polymers. Using regiorandom poly(3-alkylthiophenes) as a model system, we show that their plateau elasticity relaxes through an Arrhenius process decoupled from segmental and terminal dynamics, reorganizes persistently under mechanical perturbation, and varies systematically with side-chain length. These behaviors are hard to reconcile with a purely topological-entanglement picture and instead support a transient network dominated by reversible interchain associations, consistent with π-π interactions between conjugated backbone segments. The results thus demonstrate that plateau elasticity alone does not uniquely identify topological entanglement in conjugated polymers. More broadly, they identify the strength and lifetime of interchain association as molecular design parameters linking backbone packing to stress relaxation, viscoelastic properties, and processability in conjugated polymers, complementing their established role in electronic structure and charge transport.

由奇自旋霍尔角依赖性识别的磁绝缘体/重金属双层霍尔响应中的单自旋霍尔转换#

作者：Akashdeep Akashdeep、Ewiese Mohammad Ababneh、Botros Gerges Mikhail Fenhas、Duc Minh Tran、Mathias Kläui、Steven S. -L. Zhang 等 9 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-13

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

磁绝缘体/重金属双层的霍尔响应通常归因于两个连续的自旋霍尔转换，因此预计自旋霍尔角是均匀的。使用不同的磁场窗口，我们将具有相反自旋霍尔角的 Ta 和 Pt 的钇铁石榴石双层的霍尔电压分为磁化和磁场驱动的进动以及普通霍尔效应的贡献。场驱动组件反转 Ta 和 Pt 之间的符号，揭示霍尔响应奇数自旋霍尔角。除了最薄的重金属层外，磁化驱动成分对于 Ta 和 Pt 也具有相反的符号。具有界面生成的自旋电流的漂移扩散模型描述了这种行为：场驱动分量主要由单自旋霍尔转换承载，并且磁化驱动分量也与之一致。其他潜在的解释基于有限虚数自旋混合电导的描述，而不是界面生成的自旋电流，需要异常大的电导值，使得这种机制不太可能。我们的研究结果推进了对磁绝缘体/重金属双层中霍尔响应的理解，朝着更一般的描述方向发展。

English Abstract

The Hall response of magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers is usually ascribed to two successive spin Hall conversions and is therefore expected to be even in the spin Hall angle. Using distinct magnetic-field windows, we separate the Hall voltage of yttrium iron garnet bilayers with Ta and Pt of opposite spin Hall angle into contributions from magnetization- and magnetic-field-driven precession as well as from ordinary Hall effect. The field-driven component reverses sign between Ta and Pt, revealing a Hall response odd-in-spin-Hall-angle. The magnetization-driven component also has opposite signs for Ta and Pt, except for the thinnest heavy-metal layers. A drift-diffusion model with interface-generated spin currents describes this behavior: the field-driven component is predominantly carried by a single spin Hall conversion, and the magnetization-driven component is consistent with one as well. Other potential explanations, based on a description with a finite imaginary spin-mixing conductance rather than interface-generated spin currents, require unusually large values of this conductance, making this mechanism unlikely. Our findings advance the understanding of Hall responses in magnetic-insulator/heavy-metal bilayers toward a more general description.

路径积分扩散如何决定有限旋转玻璃#

原题：How Path Integral Diffusion Decides a Finite Spin Glass
作者：Michael Chertkov。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-10
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

当人工智能扩散型发生器对沮丧的自旋玻璃进行采样时，它首先决定哪些宏观替代方案，以及何时决定？我们研究了从点源开始的路径积分扩散（ PID ）类型的桥扩散模型。我们证明了每个各向同性PID都是一个随时间变化的高斯随机定位，相反，每个信噪比调度都是通过PID协议实现的。因此，连续时间生成器决定的内容不取决于协议，协议仅设置何时以及以何种成本进行设置。我们通过其部分承诺来描述生成器的状态：它在每个时刻分配给目标固定的嵌套类配置的概率。部分承诺是只沿着阶级对比移动的貂。他们的集合平均值以平方磁化对比度设定的精确速率提供有关每个级别的信息。每个分割大致像信噪比为1.04/λ_P的二进制高斯信道一样被决定，其中λ_P是父相关矩阵的前导特征值。该理论应用于Bethe-lattice和Sherrington-Kirkpatrick （ SK ）眼镜，最多可旋转64圈。一般观察到，轨迹首先将其权重转移到零场测量的两个镜像半之一，然后转移到其内部的玻璃类。这些类出现在玻璃转换附近，并保持其冷却特性。嵌套的玻璃质水平具有相似的λ_P ，并且是共同决定的，因此轨迹在一个或两个不同的事件中提交-镜像选择和复合玻璃质决策-正如光谱预测的那样，它们通过耦合和大小进一步分离。对于需要有限步骤的生成器，协议在后期、局部阶段而不是宏观决策中起重要作用。

English Abstract

When an AI diffusion-type generator samples a frustrated spin glass, which macroscopic alternatives does it decide first, and when? We study this for bridge-diffusion models of the Path Integral Diffusion (PID) type started from a point source. We show that every isotropic PID is a time-changed Gaussian stochastic localization, and conversely every signal-to-noise schedule is realized by a PID protocol. What a continuous-time generator decides therefore does not depend on the protocol, which only sets when and at what cost. We describe the generator's state by its partial commitments: the probabilities it assigns, at each moment, to nested classes of configurations fixed by the target. The partial commitments are martingales that move only along class contrasts. Their ensemble average gives the information about each level at an exact rate set by squared magnetization contrasts. Each split is decided, approximately, like a binary Gaussian channel at signal-to-noise ratio 1.04/λ_P, with λ_P the leading eigenvalue of the parent's correlation matrix. The theory is applied to Bethe-lattice and Sherrington--Kirkpatrick (SK) glasses with up to 64 spins. It is observed generically that a trajectory first shifts its weight to one of the two mirror halves of the zero-field measure and then to the glassy classes inside it. These classes appear near the glass transition and keep their identity on cooling. Nested glassy levels have similar λ_P and are decided together, so a trajectory commits in one or two distinct events---the mirror choice and a compound glassy decision---which separate further with coupling and size, as the spectra predict. For a generator that takes finitely many steps, the protocol matters through the late, local stage rather than the macroscopic decisions.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

发现具有目标特性的新材料是材料科学的中心目标，但候选材料的空间是巨大的。数据驱动的生成模型可以学习已知材料的分布，但它们的推断效果很差，并且生成的结构未知是否稳定。材料的属性源自其哈密顿量，量子计算机可以从中计算它们。我们引入了量子材料生成算法（QMage），这是一种由哈密顿量引导的相干搜索，针对原子组成、晶胞结构和原子位置的晶体构型，计算出的属性在给定的误差容限内满足目标。计算是单个相干量子电路，其中每个配置的哈密顿量被块编码，其目标本征态被隔离并评估属性，并且通过定点搜索放大和测量令人满意的配置。一个框架涵盖了许多目标，从形成能和带隙到弹性和电导率。我们为成熟的容错时代设计了算法，并从经典的常规基准到已建立的方法不可靠的目标，估计了逻辑量子位和 Toffoli 深度的成本。该方法根据第一原理生成候选材料，而不是根据已知材料进行概括，并且可以在目标特性难以经典预测时实现材料发现。

English Abstract

Discovering new materials with targeted properties is a central goal of materials science, yet the space of candidates is vast. Data-driven generative models learn the distribution of known materials, but they extrapolate poorly, and a generated structure is not known to be stable. A material's properties follow from its Hamiltonian, and a quantum computer can calculate them from it. We introduce the quantum material generative algorithm (QMage), a coherent search, guided by the Hamiltonian, over crystalline configurations of atomic composition, unit cell structure, and atomic positions, for one whose calculated properties meet the targets to within a given error tolerance. The computation is a single coherent quantum circuit in which each configuration's Hamiltonian is block-encoded, its target eigenstate isolated and properties evaluated, and the satisfying configurations amplified by fixed-point search and measured. One framework covers many targets, from formation energy and band gaps to elasticity and conductivity. We design the algorithm for the mature fault-tolerant era and estimate its cost in logical qubits and Toffoli depth from a classically routine benchmark to targets where established methods are unreliable. The approach generates candidate materials from first principles, rather than generalizing from those known, and could enable materials discovery when the target properties are difficult to predict classically.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

表面态是拓扑相的标志，其有效计算对于拓扑表征至关重要。传统的超胞方法（SCM）将半无限系统近似为有限周期系统，但存在计算成本高、人工表面间耦合以及带边缘附近收敛速度慢的问题。最近，半无限系统的边界问题已被改写为块三对角方程，并通过循环约简法（CRM）和传递矩阵法（TMM）来求解。然而，这些方法仍然是资源密集型的，因为细网格离散化会产生非常大的矩阵。认识到这些矩阵的内在稀疏性和低秩，我们开发了一种具有随机迹估计的低秩迭代方法（LIM），该方法可以在不牺牲准确性的情况下显着降低复杂性和内存，同时保持直接并行化。 2D 和 3D 拓扑系统的基准显示比 CRM 和 TMM 有了数量级的加速。 LIM 很容易扩展到异质结构、有缺陷的界面和非厄米系统，为拓扑和等离子体表面现象提供了一个有效的平台。

English Abstract

Surface states are hallmarks of topological phases, and their efficient computation is crucial for topology characterization. Conventional supercell methods (SCM) approximate semi-infinite systems as finite periodic ones, but suffer from high computational cost, artificial inter-surface coupling, and slow convergence near band edges. Recently, boundary problems for semi-infinite systems have been recast as block-tridiagonal equations and solved by the cyclic reduction method (CRM) and the transfer matrix method (TMM). However, these approaches remain resource-intensive, as fine-mesh discretization produces very large matrices. Recognizing the intrinsic sparsity and low rank of these matrices, we develop a low-rank iterative method (LIM) with stochastic trace estimation that substantially reduces complexity and memory without sacrificing accuracy, while remaining straightforwardly parallelizable. Benchmarks on 2D and 3D topological systems show orders-of-magnitude acceleration over CRM and TMM. LIM extends readily to heterostructures, defective interfaces, and non-Hermitian systems, providing an efficient platform for topological and plasmonic surface phenomena.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种嵌套集成学习框架，利用拓扑数据分析（TDA）从图数据中提取和集成拓扑特征，目标是提高分类和回归任务的性能。我们的方法使用由三个过滤函数引起的低星过滤来计算持久性图（PD）：接近度、介数和度2中心性。为了克服依赖单个过滤器的限制，这些 PD 通过数据驱动的三层架构进行集成。在 Level-0，不同的基础模型根据为每个过滤函数提取的拓扑特征进行独立训练。在 1 级，元学习器将每个过滤器的这些基本模型的预测结合起来，形成特定于过滤器的集成。最后，在 2 级，元学习器集成这些特定于过滤器的集合的输出以产生最终预测。我们在模拟和现实世界的图形数据集上评估我们的方法。实验结果表明，我们的框架始终优于基础模型和标准堆叠方法，实现了更高的分类精度和更低的回归误差。它还超越了现有的最先进方法，在所有基准测试中名列前三名。

English Abstract

We propose a nested ensemble learning framework that utilizes Topological Data Analysis (TDA) to extract and integrate topological features from graph data, with the goal of improving performance on classification and regression tasks. Our approach computes persistence diagrams (PDs) using lower-star filtrations induced by three filter functions: closeness, betweenness, and degree2 centrality. To overcome the limitation of relying on a single filter, these PDs are integrated through a data-driven, three-level architecture. At Level-0, diverse base models are independently trained on the topological features extracted for each filter function. At Level-1, a meta-learner combines the predictions of these base models for each filter to form filter-specific ensembles. Finally, at Level-2, a meta-learner integrates the outputs of these filter-specific ensembles to produce the final prediction. We evaluate our method on both simulated and real-world graph datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms base models and standard stacking methods, achieving higher classification accuracy and lower regression error. It also surpasses existing state-of-the-art approaches, ranking among the top three models across all benchmarks.

Kerr Quasinormal Modes without Variable Separation: A Two-Dimensional Hyperboloidal Teukolsky Solver with Physics-Informed Neural Networks#

作者：Antonio Ferrer-Sánchez、Daniela D. Doneva、José D. Martín-Guerrero、Stoytcho S. Yazadjiev、Roberto Ruiz de Austri-Bazan、Yolanda Vives-Gilabert 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-09

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We use physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) to solve the gravitational quasinormal-mode (QNM) eigenvalue problem for Kerr spacetime directly in the two-dimensional hyperboloidal formulation of the Teukolsky equation. This formulation does not require separation of variables and thus retains the coupled radial--angular structure. Such a scheme provides a prototype for calculating the QNMs of beyond-Kerr black holes for which the perturbation equations are non-separable. Sequences with increasing angular momentum are constructed, reaching close to the extremal limit. We focus on the fundamental modes (ell,m,n)=(2,0,0), (2,1,0), (2,2,0), (3,3,0) and (4,4,0), together with the first two overtones (2,2,1) and (2,2,2). After training, the frequency at every spin is refined by solving the discretized equation for it, so that it depends much less on where the stochastic training happens to stop. Comparison with continued-fraction values shows that every reported real and imaginary frequency component of the production network remains below 0.23% error, with a median deviation of 2×10⁻⁴%. For the fundamental modes, both components agree within about 10⁻³% up to a/M=0.97, and the real parts within 5×10⁻⁴% at every spin. Sub-percent accuracy is maintained in the near-extremal regime, where the damping rate becomes small and the modes are longest-lived; there the damping errors grow, and the largest, 0.23%, belongs to the (2,2,1) overtone. The results establish a non-spectral numerical route to multidimensional black-hole perturbation eigenproblems which does not match the substantially higher precision of dedicated Kerr solvers but offers greater flexibility and requires less analytical pre-processing. The proposed approach is designed to be applicable to other backgrounds whose perturbation equations do not separate.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

实验观察揭示了水分在 rm FAPbI₃ 相变中的双重作用。在高温制造过程中，湿度提高了钙钛矿（α相）薄膜的质量，而在低温下，湿度加速了光活性α相降解为光活性六方δ相。尽管有大量的实验证据，但水分调节相稳定性和转变途径的微观机制仍然未知。在这里，我们使用由机器学习势从头开始的大规模原子模拟，重现了实验观察到的湿度增加下 δ 到 α 转变温度的增加。我们的模拟揭示了沿着伯格斯转变路径的层状亚稳态中间体，它对水表现出很强的亲和力。该中间体和 δ 相的竞争吸水量决定了转变动力学的湿度依赖性。这些发现阐明了水分在钙钛矿相变中的双重作用，并证明了环境物种如何稳定亚稳态中间体并重塑转变路径，为奥斯特瓦尔德阶跃法则提供了依赖于环境的视角。

English Abstract

Experimental observations reveal a dual role of moisture in rm FAPbI₃ phase transitions. During fabrication at high temperatures, humidity improves the quality of the perovskite (α phase) films, whereas at low service temperatures, humidity accelerates degradation of the photoactive α phase into the photo-inactive hexagonal δ phase. Despite extensive experimental evidence, the microscopic mechanisms by which moisture modulates phase stability and transition pathways remain unknown. Here, using large-scale atomistic simulations powered by an ab initio machine-learned potential, we reproduce the experimentally observed increase in the δ-to-α transition temperature under increased humidity. Our simulations reveal a layered metastable intermediate along the Burgers transition pathway that exhibits a strong affinity for water. The competing water uptake of this intermediate and the δ phase governs the humidity dependence of transition kinetics. These findings elucidate the dual role of moisture in perovskite phase transitions and demonstrate how environmental species can stabilize metastable intermediates and reshape transition pathways, providing an environment-dependent perspective on Ostwald's step rule.

GLCR: Global-Local Collaborative Routing for Multi-Task Electromagnetic Signal Processing#

作者：Fang Lu、Yao Lu、Zhuangzhi Chen、Sicheng Zhang、Shanqing Yu、Yun Lin 等 8 人。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-13
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Deep neural networks for electromagnetic signal processing are typically developed as single-task experts with fixed inputs and objectives, limiting cross-task transferability. Electromagnetic foundation models offer bro…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Deep neural networks for electromagnetic signal processing are typically developed as single-task experts with fixed inputs and objectives, limiting cross-task transferability. Electromagnetic foundation models offer broader task coverage but incur substantial pretraining and inference costs. To balance accuracy and efficiency, we adopt model routing to dynamically dispatch unknown signals to matched experts within a heterogeneous pool. We construct HeteroSignal-7, a multi-task routing benchmark with 140,000 samples from seven datasets covering six task types, paired with seven classification and waveform-recovery experts and a unified evaluation protocol. We then propose GLCR, a two-stage routing method combining global dispatch with local discrimination of similar tasks. First, a lightweight one-dimensional convolutional neural network (CNN) with an additive-margin head predicts one of seven routing classes. Second, a gradient-boosted-tree specialist uses four physical statistics to re-evaluate samples whose top two candidates match a confusion pair automatically identified from validation errors. The pipeline is trained across the full signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) range and requires neither SNR labels nor noise estimation at inference time. GLCR outperforms all 19 baselines, achieving 96.16% routing accuracy and a 76.30% system score, exceeding the respective second-best baselines by 1.16% and 1.14%. It achieves the lowest routing latency among the compared methods at 1.43 ms per sample and reduces expected overall system FLOPs by 5.9 times compared with executing all experts. This work provides a public evaluation platform and an efficient, reproducible baseline for heterogeneous expert routing in electromagnetic signal processing.

Agent-Guided Discovery of Exchange-Correlation Functionals with Physics-based Validation#

作者：Achuth Chandrasekhar、Amir Barati Farimani。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-11
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配3

📄 摘要：Accurate, compact exchange--correlation functionals are central to predictive density functional theory. Symbolic search and language-model agents can propose new functional forms, but it remains unclear whether their ga…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Accurate, compact exchange--correlation functionals are central to predictive density functional theory. Symbolic search and language-model agents can propose new functional forms, but it remains unclear whether their gains survive self-consistency and external evaluation, whether the new terms carry physical content, whether agents outperform evolutionary search, and how language-model backbones compare. We connect frontier LLM agents to a GPU evaluator through the Model Context Protocol, with training-only fitting, persistent memory, and self-consistent and external evaluation. Opus-5.5 agents passed the screening gate with about 40-fold fewer tokens than Codex (GPT-6-Astra) agents and found a candidate with lower validation and held-out errors; only this candidate advanced to self-consistent evaluation. The resulting 13-parameter functional, O159, multiplies same-spin correlation by a smooth density window. Refitted controls favor a window that scales the entire same-spin factor, reducing it by 15--30% at its peak, and that acts on reaction energies mainly in near-iso-orbital regions; against a refitted no-window control, it lowers heavy-atom total-energy errors on GSCDB137 by 29%. With about 400 fits each, SyFES evolution did not improve on its GAS22 seed, whereas the agents found forms 0.10--0.13 kcal lower in validation error by adding an explicit density scale. Self-consistent O159 lowers the held-out MGCDB84 test error of ωB97M-V by 11.7%, and its mean normalized error ratio on 36 GSCDB137 datasets (1.139) is comparable to ωB97M-V (1.144) and below GAS22 (1.190). Frontier-LLM agents thus produced O159, a compact functional whose improvement over ωB97M-V survives self-consistent, physics-based validation.

An ensemble of graph generative models for assisted history matching of the Brugge field case#

作者：Gleb Shishaev、Anton Shirokov、Shadfar Davoodi、Mohammed Al-Shargabi、Vasily Demyanov、Daniel Arnold。arXiv · cs.LG，2026-11-11

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Assisted history matching remains non-unique because many geological realisations can reproduce equivalent production responses with substantial differences between individual well matches. This ambiguity increases when a field comprises several depositional settings and modelling strategies. The paper applied a graph autoencoder approach to history matching of a benchmark Brugge field that comprises different depositional zones. Each stratigraphic zone is modelled with its dedicated generative model that comprises a joint latent space for optimisation history matching loops. Graph-based generators operate directly on the active cells of the corner-point grid, so its irregular geometry is handled without conversion to a regular lattice. The optimisation search in the 160-dimensional joint latent space is done with the Covariance Matrix Adaptation Evolution Strategy. The multi-component objective function includes static well data mismatch, dynamic production mismatch, and a geological realism penalty based on the Decoder induced latent metric. Latent space diagnostics with topological data analysis are applied prior to history matching to test whether one generator is sufficient or whether a multiple generator formulation is required. The results show that the zone-based formulation gives coherent latent spaces, preserves the main porosity and permeability relations that vary from one zone to another, and supports history matching while keeping the solutions within the learned geological manifold. The matched models reproduce field oil and water production within the prior uncertainty range and provide a comparable well-level match to previously published Brugge studies based on Ensemble Kalman Filter, Particle Swarm Optimisation with principal component analysis of parameterisation, and Multiple Kernel Learning with Particle Swarm Optimisation.

LEMMO: A Large Electromagnetic Model leveraging Pretrained LLMs for Million-Sample Signal Understanding#

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Large language models have expanded beyond text to vision, audio, and other modalities, but understanding raw electromagnetic (EM) signals remains challenging. Wideband recordings can contain millions of samples, whereas existing waveform-based EM models are primarily evaluated on short in-phase and quadrature (IQ) segments, leaving long-recording understanding and detailed description insufficiently explored. We introduce LEMMO, a large EM model for instruction-driven recognition and description of long raw IQ recordings. LEMMO combines a linear-attention EM encoder with anti-aliased temporal resampling to process up to eight million complex samples. Its EM-text interaction module conditions learnable queries on instructions before retrieving signal evidence. Compact queries support recognition, while structured queries with auxiliary factual supervision organize scene-level properties, signal attributes, and cross-signal relationships for detailed description. We also construct and release a unified 1.38-TB corpus of real-world and synthetic EM signals, including LEMMO-ShortQA and LEMMO-LongDesc. Compared with MERLIN-LoRA, LEMMO improves mean EM-Bench perception accuracy by 15.77 %, mean strategy-generation ROUGE-L F1 by 4.38 points, and mean LEMMO-ShortQA accuracy by 14.31 %. It also improves categorical and numerical fact F1 on both LEMMO-LongDesc tasks while preserving its language backbone's MMLU performance. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/N1rv4n4/LEMMO and https://huggingface.co/datasets/DaftJun/RFLAS, respectively.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

赝能隙态是低能电子激发的反常动量依赖性部分抑制，其持续远高于未掺杂铜氧化物的超导临界温度，仍然是当今凝聚态物理学中最深刻的谜团之一。这篇综述总结了三十多年来的实验和理论进展。赝能隙似乎不仅仅是超导性的前身，而是一种独特的、相互交织的物质状态，其特征是竞争和合作电子秩序的复杂相互作用。在这篇综述中，我们严格检查了电阻率和磁化率等体探针以及通过角度分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）观察到的表面特征光谱特征。 ARPES 揭示了费米表面被截断为不连续的片段，扫描隧道显微镜 (STM) 可视化了费米能级周围的空间异质能隙。这些观察结果与输运异常（包括电阻率和体磁化率）结合在一起，构建了违反传统费米液体理论的相的现象学肖像。通过绘制电子相图，我们突出显示了上述最佳掺杂下的突变。最后，我们提出仍有待回答的开放性问题。这篇综述强调了赝能隙作为解锁空穴掺杂铜酸盐的非常规配对机制和强相关电子系统的更广泛物理学的关键的作用。

English Abstract

The pseudogap state, an anomalous momentum dependent partial suppression of low energy electronic excitations persisting well above the superconducting critical temperature in underdoped cuprates, remains one of the most profound enigmas in condensed physics today. This review summarizes over three decades of experimental and theoretical progress. It appears that pseudogap is not a mere precursor to superconductivity, but a distinct, intertwined state of matter characterized by a complex interplay of competing and cooperating electronic orders. We critically examine the bulk probes like resistivity and magnetic susceptibility and the surface hallmark spectroscopic signatures observed via angle resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) in this review. ARPES reveals a Fermi surface truncated into disconnected segments and scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) visualizes a spatially heterogeneous energy gap around the Fermi level. These observations are contextualized alongside transport anomalies, including resistivity and bulk magnetic susceptibility, to construct a phenomenological portrait of a phase that violates conventional Fermi liquid theory. By mapping the electronic phase diagram, we highlight the abrupt transition at above optimal doping. We conclude with open questions still to be answered. This review underscores the role of the pseudogap as a key to unlocking the unconventional pairing mechanism of hole doped cuprates and the broader physics of strongly correlated electron systems.

Characterizing Local Breadth in Language Generation#

作者：Jon Kleinberg、Fan Wei。arXiv · stat.ML，2026-11-13
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：Large language models sharpen the validity--breadth trade-off: a generator should avoid hallucinations without collapsing onto a narrow set of valid outputs. We study generation from positive examples of an unknown targe…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Large language models sharpen the validity--breadth trade-off: a generator should avoid hallucinations without collapsing onto a narrow set of valid outputs. We study generation from positive examples of an unknown target in the Kleinberg--Mullainathan model, with arbitrary, possibly adversarial order and no distributional or computational assumptions. Breadth is measured by lower Banach density, which rules out arbitrarily large sparse holes, unlike prefix-based lower asymptotic density. 1. Exact optima in one dimension: For each target K in a countable family, we characterize its optimal lower Banach density b(K) via equivalent combinatorial and topological formulations. One eventually valid generator attains all b(K) simultaneously. Every αin[0,1/2] occurs as b(K) in some countable family; finite Cantor--Bendixson point rank guarantees b(K)=1/2, even without a uniform rank bound. 2. Unavoidable trade-offs in higher dimensions: For dge2, there need not be a single best generator: optimizing density for one target may worsen the guarantee for another. An explicit family quantifies this intrinsically multi-objective trade-off. 3. Complete characterization of density profiles: We characterize the full region of simultaneously achievable guarantees, with equivalent combinatorial and topological formulations, after excluding geometrically degenerate rectangles. Every prescribed product prodᵢ[0,αᵢ], αᵢin[0,1/2] can occur as an exact achievable region for some language collection. 4. Near-half guarantees and the sharp endpoint: Under a near-logarithmic nondegeneracy condition, one generator achieves filtered density at least 1/2-ε at all finite-point-rank targets, for every ε>0, improving the previous polylogarithmic threshold. The exact 1/2 endpoint can fail even for a rank-zero singleton family.

GOPR-CAD: Graph-of-Operations Progressive Reasoning for CAD Generation#

作者：Yitian Pang、Yifan Li、Xiaowei Yue、Li Zheng。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-10
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：Generating accurate CAD models requires substantial engineering expertise and careful control of geometry, topology, and mechanical constraints. Text-to-CAD aims to simplify this process, but current LLM-based methods of…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Generating accurate CAD models requires substantial engineering expertise and careful control of geometry, topology, and mechanical constraints. Text-to-CAD aims to simplify this process, but current LLM-based methods often generate CAD programs directly. Language models remain unreliable when producing long code while coordinating coupled geometric, topological, and dimensional constraints. Consequently, executable programs may still produce structurally incorrect models, which execution rate and rendered appearance may fail to reveal. To address these limitations, we propose GOPR-CAD, an agentic Text-to-CAD pipeline based on Graph-of-Operations Progressive Reasoning. A language model acts as a decision agent over encapsulated CAD operations and progressively constructs an executable operation graph, which a deterministic compiler converts into the final CAD model. This separation requires no additional training and supports different language models. To evaluate structural fidelity, we construct MHCAD, a benchmark of 300 standard mechanical part requirements derived from Machinery's Handbook. Their explicit structures enable reference-free STEP/B-rep evaluation. GOPR-CAD achieves 82.7% Structural Yield on MHCAD, and 87.2% Structural Yield on an external CADPrompt subset.

Bridging Gaps with Tensor Networks: From the Haldane Gap and Beyond#

AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

阅读原文 ↗

English Abstract

Proofs generated by large language models are often difficult to understand, generalize, or formalize directly. Here we verify, digest, and formalize OpenAI's computer-assisted proof of a uniform gap in the periodic spin-1 Heisenberg chain using tensor-network diagrams and Lean 4. Reading the partition-function network along space and imaginary time shows how finite-ring bounds propagate across doubled lengths and inverse temperatures to all longer even rings, sharpening the spin-1 bound from the same finite inputs by a factor of 19 to Δ_L > 0.0729 for all even L ≥ 36. We identify reflection positivity as the general condition behind the proof and certify uniform gaps in seven additional spin chains and ladders. More broadly, digesting machine-generated proofs into transparent physical mechanisms yields sharper bounds, broader theorems, and reusable formal verifications.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

例外点 (EP) 是非厄米物理学的标志，可产生极高的灵敏度和增强的动态响应。这种独特的光谱奇点在经典光子学中已经在理论和实验层面上得到了广泛的研究，相关的可观察到的现象是显而易见的。在量子领域中情况不一定如此，在量子领域中，围绕 EP 的这种行为在很大程度上仍然难以捉摸。事实上，耗散和量子涨落之间的相互作用使得完整的林布拉德演化与有效的非厄米哈密顿描述有很大不同。在这种情况下，我们通过考虑适当归一化的长期平均值和不平衡来研究刘维尔 EP 在光谱和动态响应上的特征。尽管我们的方法是通用的，但我们重点关注两个范式模型——有损玻色-哈伯德晶格和耗散双自旋系统，我们在其中研究量子跃迁和刘维尔谱中相互作用的作用，从复杂和结构化的伪谱的角度来看。我们的结果展示了非厄米现象如何在现实量子晶格中出现，在经典 EP 物理和实验上可访问的开放量子系统之间架起了一座桥梁。

English Abstract

Exceptional points (EPs) are a hallmark of non-Hermitian physics, giving rise to extreme sensitivity and enhanced dynamic response. Such unique spectral singularities have been extensively studied in classical photonics, both at theoretical and experimental levels, where the relevant observables are evident. That is not necessarily the case in the quantum regime, where this kind of behavior around EPs, to a large extent, remains elusive. In fact, the interplay between dissipation and quantum fluctuations makes the complete Lindbladian evolution quite different from effective non-Hermitian Hamiltonian descriptions. In this context, we investigate the signatures of the Liouvillian EPs on spectral and dynamic response by considering properly normalized long-time averages and imbalances. Even though our approach is generic, we focus on two paradigmatic models-a lossy Bose-Hubbard lattice and a dissipative two-spin system, where we investigate the role of quantum jumps and interactions in the Liouvillian spectrum, in terms of complex and structured pseudospectra. Our results demonstrate how non-Hermitian phenomena emerge in realistic quantum lattices, providing a bridge between classical EP physics and experimentally accessible open quantum systems.

SignRot: LLM Quantization with Massive Outlier-Aware Sign-Adjusted Rotation#

作者：Jaehun Gim、Jaewoo Kim、Gyuwan Kim、Seo Yeon Park。arXiv · cs.AI，2026-11-13
⚡ 磁性/铁电🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉0方向匹配0

📄 摘要：While quantization compresses LLMs and accelerates inference, outlier activations impede efficient low-bit representation. In particular, massive outliers cause substantial performance degradation. Although rotation-base…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

While quantization compresses LLMs and accelerates inference, outlier activations impede efficient low-bit representation. In particular, massive outliers cause substantial performance degradation. Although rotation-based methods that rely on randomized Hadamard transforms for online inference aim to mitigate this, they can produce fragmented distributions with empty bins when a massive outlier dominates a token's activation. This fragmentation severely undermines uniform quantization performance. Moreover, massive outliers concentrate in the down-projection input, where the preceding nonlinearity requires the rotation to be applied at inference time. This constraint prevents training-based approaches such as SpinQuant from learning the online rotation at this position, as a learned dense matrix cannot use the Fast Walsh-Hadamard Transform and would require a full matrix multiplication during inference. To address this, we propose SignRot (Sign-adjusted Rotation), which identifies massive outlier channels and constructs a sign adjustment vector that unifies the signs of the corresponding Hadamard row, collapsing the bimodal distribution into a unimodal profile amenable to quantization. Since the aligned outlier shifts the activation mean away from zero, we pair SignRot with asymmetric activation quantization, whose zero-point absorbs this shift. As a lightweight, drop-in improvement compatible with any Hadamard-based quantization method, SignRot requires only a single calibration forward pass and adds only element-wise sign flips at inference, without an additional matrix multiplication. Applying SignRot to QuaRot achieves a WikiText2 perplexity of 5.88 and an average zero-shot reasoning accuracy of 72.73, surpassing SpinQuant on the LLaMA3-70B model. Code is available at https://github.com/HYU-ARK-Lab/SignRot.

中文摘要

分子极性可用于指导合理和有针对性地设计抑制金属卤化物钙钛矿晶体管中离子迁移的添加剂，从而实现超过40 cm2 V−1 s−1的迁移率和超过9,000次开/关循环的稳定性。

English Abstract

Perovskite transistors have recently achieved field-effect mobilities comparable with commercial polycrystalline silicon devices, but their practical applications remain limited because of poor operational stability and current–voltage hysteresis caused by ion migration. Unlike in optoelectronic devices, transistors have high gate electric fields that exert strong Coulombic forces on mobile ions, making robust ion-anchoring interactions essential for suppressing field-induced ionic motion. Here we report a data-driven additive design strategy that uses molecular polarity to strengthen binding to mobile ions. We perform the high-throughput screening of fluorinated ammonium-based molecules with dual hydrogen-bonding capability across a compound chemical space comprising >108molecules and identify 14 candidates that combine high molecular polarity with suitable size. Three of these additives allow us to fabricate transistors with field-effect mobilities exceeding 25 cm2V−1s−1, with 4-(trifluoromethyl)-phenethylammonium performing the best, having a mobility of 41.3 cm2V−1s−1and stability over 9,000 on/off switching cycles.

UniHam：材料中哈密顿学习的大规模 SOC 完整数据集和基准#

原题：UniHam: A Large-Scale SOC-Complete Dataset and Benchmark for Hamiltonian Learning in Materials
