📄 摘要（中文翻译）：电子哈密顿量的准确预测将实现广泛的属性推断，同时避免密度泛函理论（DFT）的高计算成本。然而，通用材料基础模型的进展受到数据瓶颈的限制：现有的哈密顿数据集通常很小，缺乏结构多样性，并且经常忽略基本的相对论物理，例如自旋轨道耦合（SOC）。因此，我们构建了 UniHam，这是一个大规模哈密顿量数据集和基准套件，包含 100,000 多个 DFT 计算的复值厄米特哈密顿量，具有完整的 SOC，涵盖 72 个元素和广泛的晶体几何形状和对称性（…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic hamiltonian。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子哈密顿量的准确预测将实现广泛的属性推断，同时避免密度泛函理论（DFT）的高计算成本。然而，通用材料基础模型的进展受到数据瓶颈的限制：现有的哈密顿数据集通常很小，缺乏结构多样性，并且经常忽略基本的相对论物理，例如自旋轨道耦合（SOC）。因此，我们构建了 UniHam，这是一个大规模哈密顿量数据集和基准套件，包含 100,000 多个 DFT 计算的复值厄米特哈密顿量，具有完整的 SOC，涵盖 72 个元素和广泛的晶体几何形状和对称性（跨越不同的晶格类型和空间群族）。在 UniHam 的基础上，我们在标准化协议下对两个代表性的最先进模型进行了基准测试，并引入了联合评估三个维度的补充评估指标：（i）哈密顿量重建精度，（ii）跨组成/对称性偏移的分布外（OOD）泛化，以及（iii）支持从预测的哈密顿量进行下游属性预测的能力。 UniHam 上的实验表明，所提出的基准和指标有效地区分了模型功能，揭示了内在的 SOC 和元素相关的故障模式、成分 OOD 鲁棒性的巨大变化，以及进行谱级评估以评估哈密顿量预测是否可靠支持下游电子结构特性的必要性。总体而言，UniHam 提供了一个可重复的、完整的 SOC 基准，可以加强模型比较并加速下一代量子材料基础模型的开发。

English Abstract

Accurate prediction of electronic Hamiltonians would enable broad property inference while avoiding the high computational cost of Density Functional Theory (DFT). However, progress toward general-purpose materials foundation models is limited by a data bottleneck: existing Hamiltonian datasets are typically small, lack structural diversity, and often omit essential relativistic physics such as spin--orbit coupling (SOC). We therefore construct UniHam, a large-scale Hamiltonian dataset and benchmark suite comprising 100,000+ DFT-computed complex-valued Hermitian Hamiltonians with full SOC, covering 72 elements and a wide range of crystal geometries and symmetries (spanning diverse lattice types and space-group families). Building on UniHam, we benchmark two representative state-of-the-art models under a standardized protocol and introduce complementary evaluation metrics that jointly assess three dimensions: (i) Hamiltonian reconstruction accuracy, (ii) out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization across composition/symmetry shifts, and (iii) the ability to support downstream property prediction from the predicted Hamiltonians. Experiments on UniHam demonstrate that the proposed benchmark and metrics effectively differentiate model capabilities, revealing intrinsic SOC- and element-dependent failure modes, large variations in compositional OOD robustness, and the necessity of spectral-level evaluation to assess whether Hamiltonian predictions reliably support downstream electronic-structure properties. Overall, UniHam provides a reproducible, SOC-complete benchmark that can sharpen model comparisons and accelerate the development of next-generation foundation models for quantum materials.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

分子反应性的准确预测需要对各种分子几何形状和电子态的电子关联和自旋进行准确且计算有效的描述。我们介绍 SATE，一种具有有效核心潜力的自旋适应可转移神经波函数架构。 SATE 将可转移神经波函数、自旋适应反对称方法和有效核心电势结合到单一基础模型架构中，并在包含从氢到溴等选定元素的成分上进行预训练。经过预训练和适应后，我们的模型能够通过解决两个关键问题来预测 3d 过渡金属自旋态间隙、键解离能和反应能。首先，将核心电势纳入模型使我们能够减少明确处理的电子数量。其次，使用自旋适应反对称可以通过构造强制执行规定的总自旋。通过使用 3d 过渡金属化学测试我们的模型，我们评估了模型对此类化合物中强电子相关性和竞争电子态的描述。我们发现，预训练在匹配的适应预算下提高了大多数测试的组件能量，而自旋态间隙、解离能和反应能的改进仍然依赖于系统。最终，我们的工作通过将有效核心势的计算效率与对总自旋的显式控制相结合，为更准确和可转移的分子系统建模奠定了基础。

English Abstract

Accurate prediction of molecular reactivity requires both accurate and computationally efficient description of electron correlation and spin across various molecular geometries and electronic states. We introduce SATE, a spin-adapted transferable neural wavefunction architecture with effective core potentials. SATE combines a transferable neural wavefunction, the spin-adapted antisymmetrization method, and effective core potentials into a single foundation model architecture pretrained on compositions containing selected elements from hydrogen to bromine. After pretraining and adaptation, our model is able to predict 3d transition-metal spin-state gaps, bond dissociation energies, and reaction energies by addressing two key issues. First, including the core potentials into the model allows us to reduce the number of explicitly treated electrons. Second, using spin-adapted antisymmetrization enforces the prescribed total spin by construction. By testing our model with 3d transition metal chemistry, we evaluate our model's description of strong electron correlation and competing electronic states in such compounds. We find that pretraining improves most tested component energies at matched adaptation budgets, while improvements in spin-state gaps, dissociation energies, and reaction energies remain system dependent. Ultimately, our work lays the foundation for more accurate and transferable modeling of molecular systems by combining the computational efficiency of effective core potentials with explicit control over total spin.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们使用基于神经网络的变分蒙特卡罗来研究阿哈罗诺夫-卡舍尔哈密顿量（莫尔材料的连续介质模型）中分数陈绝缘体与广义维格纳晶体之间的竞争。我们引入了一种受通量附件启发的变分波函数，它捕获了分数化相，并通过将其能量与捕获结晶相的 Slater-Jastrow ansatz 的能量进行比较，我们确定了强带混合状态下分数填充的相图，以及超出其他方法范围的系统尺寸。我们发现，以非均匀量子几何编码的离散平移对称性对填充分数 ν=1/3 和 ν=2/3 处的分数化态有不同的影响，并且能带混合可以稳定不相称的晶体基态。我们的结果进一步揭示了拓扑和非拓扑晶相之间的微妙竞争。

English Abstract

We use neural-network-based variational Monte Carlo to investigate the competition between fractional Chern insulators and generalized Wigner crystals in the Aharonov-Casher Hamiltonian, a continuum model for moiré materials. We introduce a variational wave function inspired by flux attachment that captures fractionalized phases and, by comparing its energy with that of a Slater--Jastrow ansatz that captures crystalline phases, we determine the phase diagram at fractional fillings in regimes of strong band mixing and for system sizes beyond the reach of other methods. We find that discrete translation symmetry, encoded in a nonuniform quantum geometry, affects fractionalized states at filling fractions ν=1/3 and ν=2/3 differently, and that band mixing can stabilize incommensurate crystalline ground states. Our results further reveal the delicate competition between topological and non-topological crystalline phases.

中文摘要

滑动铁电性为电可编程电子和光学功能提供了一种非易失性途径，但激子光致发光的确定性控制通常因莫尔条纹重构和畴平均而变得模糊不清。本文中，我们研究了一种人工组装的WSe₂双层，其在菱面体堆叠中具有近乎零的扭转，并通过激子光致发光观察到可逆的滞后性电学开关。在面外电场下，动量间接的QK激子发射表现出斯塔克斜率反转和突发能量跃迁，揭示了一个由两次能带重排和一次铁电翻转事件分隔的四阶段序列。极化反转还会选择与层内激子杂化的动量直接KK层间激子分支，从而产生依赖于历史的光致发光。共振拉曼散射的铁电调制提供了一种互补的读出方式，其滞后强度与依赖于极化的层间电场相关。这些结果建立了基于激子的铁电WSe₂电子结构定量方法，并为电可编程光电器能开辟了一条途径。

English Abstract

Sliding ferroelectricity offers a non-volatile route to electrically programmable electronic and optical functionality, but deterministic control of excitonic photoluminescence is often obscured by moiré reconstruction and domain averaging. Here we study an artificially assembled WSe₂ bilayer with near-zero twist in rhombohedral stacking and observe reversible, hysteretic electrical switching through excitonic photoluminescence. Under an out-of-plane electric field, momentum-indirect QK exciton emission exhibits Stark-slope reversals and abrupt energy jumps, resolving a four-stage sequence separated by two band realignments and one ferroelectric switching event. Polarization reversal also selects the momentum-direct KK interlayer-exciton branch that hybridizes with an intralayer exciton, producing history-dependent photoluminescence. Ferroelectric modulation of resonant Raman scattering provides a complementary readout, with hysteretic intensities correlated with the polarization-dependent interlayer field. These results establish an exciton-based quantification of the electronic structure in ferroelectric WSe₂ and open a route to electrically programmable optoelectronic functionality.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

层间激子（IX）将空间分离的电子和空穴波函数与电可调偶极矩结合在一起，但它们减少的波函数重叠通常会限制光发射，特别是对于高能态。在这里，我们报告了少层 WSe2 中接近 2.23 eV 的明亮、光谱窄的高能层间激子 (h-IX)，并研究了其光学活性如何随层数和滑动铁电极化而演变。在原始的 2H-WSe2 中，我们观察到高能激子发射的厚度依赖性增亮。 GW-BSE 计算揭示了混合的层内和层间结构，具有增强的光学重量，表明多层杂化有助于观察到的增亮。在包含滑动铁电界面的平行堆叠 3L/3L WSe2 中，h-IX 表现出偏振相关能量和区分 Pup 和 Pdown 域的 Stark 响应。电场驱动的畴壁运动在两个 h-IX 分支之间产生光谱权重的滞后转移，从而实现铁电开关的光学跟踪。这些结果将多层杂化确立为光学活性高能层间激子的途径，并将滑动铁电性作为控制其能量、偶极子和发射的补充自由度。

English Abstract

Interlayer excitons (IXs) combine spatially separated electron and hole wavefunctions with electrically tunable dipole moments, but their reduced wavefunction overlap generally limits optical emission, particularly for high-energy states. Here we report a bright, spectrally narrow high-energy interlayer exciton (h-IX) near 2.23 eV in few-layer WSe2 and investigate how its optical activity evolves with layer number and sliding-ferroelectric polarization. In pristine 2H-WSe2, we observe thickness-dependent brightening of high-energy exciton emission. GW-BSE calculations reveal mixed intralayer and interlayer configurations with enhanced optical weight, suggesting that multilayer hybridization contributes to the observed brightening. In parallel-stacked 3L/3L WSe2 containing a sliding-ferroelectric interface, the h-IX exhibits polarization-dependent energies and Stark responses that distinguish Pup and Pdown domains. Electric-field-driven domain-wall motion produces hysteretic transfer of spectral weight between the two h-IX branches, enabling optical tracking of ferroelectric switching. These results establish multilayer hybridization as a route to optically active high-energy interlayer excitons and sliding ferroelectricity as a complementary degree of freedom for controlling their energy, dipole and emission.

中文摘要

NiI2 是一种范德华半导体，具有复杂的电子相和磁相，有望实现功能应用。为此，详细了解其能带结构及其激发对于阐明电子和传输特性至关重要。我们通过第一性原理计算支持的时间和角度分辨光电子能谱，对块状 NiI2 中的能带结构和最低价态到导带的跃迁进行了全面的描述。我们描述了 Ni 和 I 流形的轨道特征，并确定了价带和导带浅极小值的主要动量分辨特征。通过这项研究，我们对 NiI2 的电子结构及其单粒子带间电子激发有了基本的了解，为理论模型奠定了基准，并为该材料的未来研究提供了框架。

English Abstract

NiI₂ is a van der Waals semiconductor characterized by complex electronic and magnetic phases, holding promise towards functional applications. For this purpose, a detailed understanding of its band structure and its excitations is essential to elucidate the electronic and transport properties. We present a comprehensive description of the band structure and of the lowest valence-to-conduction band transition in bulk NiI₂, by means of time- and angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy supported by first-principles calculations. We describe the orbital character of Ni and I manifolds, and identify the main momentum-resolved features of the valence band and the shallow minima of the conduction band. With this study, we provide a foundational understanding of the electronic structure of NiI₂ and its single-particle interband electronic excitation, a benchmark for theoretical models, and a framework for future investigations on this material.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure、ferroelectricity、point defects。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

α-In2Se₃是一种二维范德华材料，在铁电方面具有广阔的应用前景。然而，天然缺陷的存在会显着影响其结构和电子特性。在这项工作中，我们采用密度泛函理论和最先进的元广义梯度近似（meta-GGA）交换相关函数来研究富硒和富硒生长条件下块体α-In2Se3的电子结构以及中性点缺陷（包括空位和反位点）的形成。我们的计算重现了原始材料的实验结构参数，并揭示了中间层的 Se 空位在两种化学环境下都表现出出乎意料的低形成能。结构分析表明，邻近的四面体配位 In 原子沿三重轴经历了显着的局部弛豫，即使其配位从 4 个减少到 3 个，也保留了其 C₃ᵥ 位点对称性，为该缺陷的显着稳定性提供了微观解释。

English Abstract

α-In₂Se₃ is a two-dimensional van der Waals material with promising applications involving ferroelectricity. However, the presence of native defects can significantly influence its structural and electronic properties. In this work, we employ density functional theory with a state-of-the-art meta-generalized gradient approximation (meta-GGA) exchange-correlation functional to investigate the electronic structure of bulk α-In₂Se₃ and the formation of neutral point defects, including vacancies and antisites, under Se-rich and In-rich growth conditions. Our calculations reproduce the experimental structural parameters of the pristine material and reveal that the Se vacancy in the middle layer exhibits an unexpectedly low formation energy under both chemical environments. Structural analysis shows that the neighboring tetrahedrally coordinated In atom undergoes a substantial local relaxation along the three-fold axis, which preserves its C₃ᵥ site symmetry even as its coordination is reduced from four to three, providing a microscopic explanation for the remarkable stability of this defect.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure、perovskite、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们对 Ba₃CoNb2O₉ 的无序相进行了温度依赖性 X 射线吸收光谱 (XAS) 和 X 射线磁圆二色性 (XMCD) 测量，以确定 Co2+ 离子的局域电子结构。我们采用线拟合逆部分荧光产率（iPFY）方法从这种绝缘材料中获得可靠的光谱。通过比较实验光谱和完整的多重态簇计算，我们能够限制局部晶体场环境并评估 Co²⁺ 基态的自旋轨道纠缠性质。在这个化学无序系统中，Co²⁺ 离子平均保留有效的立方晶体场环境，支持 J_eff = 1/2 基态。这些结果进一步证实 Ba₃CoNb2O₉ 是研究无序对 J_eff = 1/2 磁性影响的有前途的平台。

English Abstract

We performed temperature-dependent X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS) and X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD) measurements on the disordered phase of Ba₃CoNb₂O₉ to determine the local electronic structure of the Co²⁺ ions. We employed the line-fitting inverse partial fluorescence yield (iPFY) method to obtain reliable spectra from this insulating material. From the comparison between the experimental spectra and full multiplet cluster calculations we were able to constrain the local crystal-field environment and assess the spin-orbit-entangled nature of the Co²⁺ ground state. In this chemically disordered system, the Co²⁺ ions retain on average an effectively cubic crystal-field environment that supports a J_eff = 1/2 ground state. These results further establish that Ba₃CoNb₂O₉ is a promising platform for investigating the effect of disorder on J_eff = 1/2 magnetism.

超越玻恩-奥本海默的相空间电子结构理论：重新审视几何力和穆迪-沙佩尔-威尔切克#

原题：A phase space electronic structure theory beyond Born-Oppenheimer: Geometric forces and Moody-Shapere-Wilczek revisited
作者：Mansi Bhati、D. Vale Cofer-Shabica、Jonathan I. Rawlinson、Robert G. Littlejohn、Joseph Subotnik、Nadine C. Bradbury。arXiv · physics，2026-11-09

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：electronic structure、electronic structure calculation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们重新审视二维和三维量子力学中的三体问题，生成哈密顿量的精确特征值和特征向量，以及使用各种不同方案计算的一系列近似解，以分离重（“核”）和轻（“电子”）粒子。我们证明，以最小的额外成本，通过执行由核位置 (mathbf X) 和核动量 (P) 参数化的电子结构计算，可以超越玻恩-奥本海默近似，这是一种所谓的电子结构相空间理论。特别是，我们证明这种相空间电子结构计算正确地结合了运动核框架中电子感受到的非惯性科里奥利力和离心力，从而导致比以前更准确的本征能和电子角动量。我们还证明，我们的方法自然地结合并推广了非阿贝尔贝里曲率的 Moody-Shapere-Wilczek 磁单极子（现在允许振动运动而不是固定长度的双原子）。我们认为，由此产生的方法对于角动量在原子核和电子之间流动的动力学传播非常有用。特别是，如果扩展到包括自旋自由度，本方法将提供一种通过手性声子和耦合核电子运动的透镜来研究手性诱导自旋选择性的实用方法。

English Abstract

We revisit the three-body problem in quantum mechanics in two and three dimensions, generating both exact eigenvalues and eigenvectors of the Hamiltonian and a series of approximate solutions as calculated with a variety of different schemes to separate heavy ("nuclear") and light ("electronic") particles. We show that, with minimal extra cost, one can go beyond the Born-Oppenheimer approximation by performing electronic structure calculations parameterized by both the nuclear position (mathbf X) and the nuclear momentum (P), a so-called phase space theory of electronic structure. In particular, we demonstrate that such phase space electronic structure calculations correctly incorporate the non-inertial Coriolis and centrifugal forces felt by electrons in a moving nuclear frame, thus leading to far more accurate eigenenergies and electronic angular momenta than has been possible before. We also demonstrate that our approach naturally incorporates and generalizes the Moody-Shapere-Wilczek magnetic monopole for the non-abelian Berry curvature (now allowing for vibrational motion rather than a diatomic of fixed length). We argue that the resulting approach should be extremely useful for propagating dynamics where angular momentum flows between nuclei and electrons; in particular, if extended to include spin degrees of freedom, the present approach will offer a practical means to study chiral induced spin selectivity through the lens of chiral phonons and coupled nuclear-electronic motion.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：ferroelectric、ferroelectricity。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子相关性支撑着物质中多种自发有序状态，其集体激发表现为编码潜在顺序的准粒子。虽然自旋交换相互作用产生磁序及其称为磁振子的准粒子激发，但它们在电子铁电体中的对应物仍然知之甚少。在这里，我们推导了电子铁电性的类似量子偶极-赝自旋交换模型，并识别了携带固有电偶极矩的相关玻色准粒子。与传统晶格铁电体中的铁子不同，这些偶极准粒子完全由集体电子动力学产生，不涉及晶格振动。它们的激发光谱在静电场下表现出线性斯塔克位移，而与振荡电场的耦合使它们能够相干激发，从而在太赫兹范围内产生巨大的、共振增强的二次谐波和光学整流。这些偶极准粒子为电可调非线性光学提供了有希望的机会，并开辟了一条将电子系统中的传输范式从传统充电电流扩展到电偶极电流的途径。

English Abstract

Electronic correlations underpin diverse spontaneously ordered states in matter, whose collective excitations emerge as quasiparticles that encode the underlying order. While spin exchange interactions give rise to magnetic order and its quasiparticle excitations known as magnons, their counterparts in electronic ferroelectrics remain poorly understood. Here, we derive an analogous quantum dipolar-pseudospin exchange model for electronic ferroelectricity and identify the associated bosonic quasiparticles that carry intrinsic electric dipole moments. Unlike ferrons in conventional lattice-based ferroelectrics, these dipolar quasiparticles arise entirely from collective electronic dynamics without involving lattice vibrations. Their excitation spectrum exhibits a linear Stark shift under a static electric field, while coupling to an oscillating electric field enables their coherent excitation, leading to colossal, resonantly enhanced second-harmonic generation and optical rectification in the terahertz regime. These dipolar quasiparticles offer promising opportunities for electrically tunable nonlinear optics and open a route toward extending the paradigm of transport in electronic systems beyond conventional charge currents to electric dipole currents.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：magnetism、monte carlo simulation、spin-orbit coupling。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

二维过渡金属二硫属化物（2D-TMD）为实现稀磁半导体提供了一个有前途的平台。然而，同时实现大磁各向异性能量（MAE）和高居里温度（T_c）仍然是一个主要挑战。在这项工作中，我们采用DFT+U系统地研究了掺杂过渡金属原子的2D-MoSe2和2D-WSe2的热力学稳定性和磁性能。我们发现成对的掺杂剂构型比之前归因的孤立掺杂剂更稳定。尽管 2D-MoSe2 和 2D-WSe2 的晶体结构几乎相同，但它们在掺杂时表现出截然不同的特性。 2D-MoSe2 中的所有成对掺杂剂均表现出长程铁磁耦合 (FMC)，这主要是由于载流子介导的交换相互作用，而 2D-WSe2 中的掺杂剂则表现出弱交换耦合。有趣的是，2Mn2W 显示出从短程中不太稳定的反铁磁耦合 (AFMC) 到长程中高度稳定的 FMC 的转变。自旋轨道耦合、交换相互作用和轨道杂化的综合作用产生了较大的 MAE，2V2Mo 的最大面内值为 -3.5 meV，2Mn2W 的最大面内值为 -3.2 meV。蒙特卡洛模拟估计 2Fe 2 Mo 的 T_c simeq 为 440 K，并确保其在 2D-MoSe 2 中的高温稳定性。我们的研究结果表明，掺杂剂团簇是在 2D-TMD 中设计高温铁磁性的有效途径，并强调了它们在纳米自旋电子应用中的潜力。

English Abstract

Two-dimensional transition metal dichalcogenides (2D-TMDs) offer a promising platform for realizing dilute magnetic semiconductors. However, simultaneously achieving large magnetic anisotropy energy (MAE) and high Curie temperature (T_c) remains a major challenge. In this work, we employ DFT+U to systematically investigate the thermodynamic stability and magnetic properties of 2D-MoSe₂ and 2D-WSe₂ doped with transition metal atoms. We found that the paired dopant configurations are more stable than previously attributed isolated dopants. Despite their nearly identical crystal structures, 2D-MoSe₂ and 2D-WSe₂ show contrasting properties upon doping. All paired dopants in 2D-MoSe₂ exhibit long-range ferromagnetic coupling (FMC) primarily due to carrier-mediated exchange interactions, whereas those in 2D-WSe₂ show weak exchange coupling. Interestingly, 2Mn₂W shows a transition from less stable antiferromagnetic coupling (AFMC) in the short range to highly stable FMC in the long-range. The combined effect of spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, and orbital hybridization gives rise to a large MAE with maximum in-plane values of -3.5 meV for 2V₂Mo and -3.2 meV for 2Mn₂W. Monte Carlo simulations estimate T_c simeq 440 K for 2Fe₂Mo and ensure its high-temperature stability in 2D-MoSe₂. Our results establish dopant clustering as an effective route to engineer high-temperature ferromagnetism in 2D-TMDs and highlight their potential for nano-spintronic applications.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们使用密度泛函理论 (DFT) 中的第一原理电子结构计算，研究了电子关联 (U) 和静水压力对 x = 0、0.5 和 1 时双钙钛矿 La2CoIr11₋ₓTiₓO6 电子性能的影响。在环境压力下，我们系统地改变电子相关性，即现场库仑相互作用，以研究其对带隙和结构参数的影响。增加 U 值会增强所有三种组合物的带隙，同时结构参数仅产生轻微变化。还研究了相同组合物的静水压力的影响。当 x = 0（U_Co = 5 eV 和 U_Ir = 2 eV）、0.5（U_Co = 5 eV 和 U_Ir = 2 eV）和 1（U_Co = 4 eV）时，环境压力下的估计带隙值分别约为 39、42 和 53 GPa，表明压力引起的绝缘体到金属的转变。这些结果表明，电子关联稳定了绝缘态，而静水压力通过缩小带隙有利于金属态。这些发现强调了相关氧化物通过电子相关性和压力的可调性，为材料设计提供了见解。

English Abstract

We study the effect of electron correlation (U) and hydrostatic pressure on the electronic properties of double perovskites La₂CoIr₁₋ₓTiₓO₆ for x = 0, 0.5, and 1, using first-principles electronic structure calculations within density functional theory (DFT). At ambient pressure, we systematically vary the electron correlation, i.e., on-site Coulomb interaction to study its effect on band gap and structural parameters. Increasing the value of U enhances the band gap of all three compositions while producing only a slight variation in structural parameters. The effect of hydrostatic pressure is also studied for the same compositions. The estimated band gap values at ambient pressure closes at approximately 39, 42, and 53 GPa for x = 0 (for U_Co = 5 eV and U_Ir = 2 eV), 0.5 (for U_Co = 5 eV and U_Ir = 2 eV), and 1 (for U_Co = 4 eV), respectively, indicating pressure-induced insulator-to-metal transitions. These results show that electron correlation stabilises the insulating state, whereas hydrostatic pressure favours the metallic state by closing the band gap. These findings highlight the tunability of correlated oxides through electron correlations and pressure, offering insights for materials design.

中文摘要

锰基硫族化物中的多晶型现象提供了获得具有不同晶体对称性、极性结构和不同磁序的磁性半导体的途径。特别是，纤锌矿 MnSe 表现出引人注目的电极化和包括交变磁序在内的几个竞争磁相的相互作用。然而，在实验有用的体积中稳定亚稳态多晶型物（例如纤锌矿锰硒）仍然具有挑战性。在这里，我们发现纳米级周期 MnSe/CdSe 超晶格可以在微米级总厚度上稳定纤锌矿 MnSe，同时抑制热力学上有利的岩盐相的形成。增加的磁体积使得能够对这种极性多晶型物进行体敏感表征。通过独立的实验技术研究了纤锌矿MnSe的室温晶格参数，确定为a=(4.18±0.03),mathringA和c=(6.78±0.03),mathringA。 SQUID 磁力测量揭示了 T_N = 62,K 的反铁磁有序性，这伴随着 MnSe 晶格参数的异常，如温度依赖性 X 射线衍射所观察到的。光学测量揭示了激子跃迁指向 3.4 eV 左右的带隙。这些结果凸显了异质结构工程对于磁性半导体亚稳相（特别是纤锌矿 MnSe）详细研究的实用性和重要性。

English Abstract

Polymorphism in Mn-based chalcogenides offers access to magnetic semiconductors with distinct crystal symmetries, polar structures, and different magnetic orders. In particular, wurtzite MnSe exhibits an appealing interplay of electric polarization and several competing magnetic phases including altermagnetic order. However, stabilizing metastable polymorphs, such as wurtzite MnSe, in experimentally useful volumes remains challenging. Here, we show that a nanoscale-period MnSe/CdSe superlattice stabilizes wurtzite MnSe over micrometer-scale total thickness while suppressing the formation of the thermodynamically favored rock-salt phase. The increased magnetic volume enables bulk-sensitive characterization of this polar polymorph. The room-temperature lattice parameters of wurtzite MnSe are studied by independent experimental techniques and determined to be a = (4.18 ± 0.03),mathringA and c = (6.78 ± 0.03), mathringA. SQUID magnetometry reveals antiferromagnetic ordering with T_N = 62,K, which is accompanied by an anomaly of the MnSe lattice parameter, as oberved by temperature-dependent X-ray diffraction. Optical measurements reveal excitonic transitions pointing to the band gap at around 3.4 eV. These results highlight the utility and importance of heterostructure engineering for detailed studies of metastable phases of magnetic semiconductors, in particular wurtzite MnSe.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：multiferroic、phase transition、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

铁电相变是由离子位移引起的对称性破缺驱动的，但它们的化学特征很少在原子水平上直接探测。在这里，我们通过跟踪局部化学环境中离子迁移引起的变化，建立了温度依赖性 X 射线光电子能谱 (XPS) 作为铁电相变的强大定量探针。使用范德华 (vdW) 层状 ABP2S₆ 材料作为模型系统，我们系统地比较了经历铁电相变的 CuCrP2S₆ (CCPS) 与在相同温度范围内保持顺电性的 AgCrP2S₆ (ACPS)。虽然 ACPS 仅表现出平滑的微不足道的热展宽和核心能级光谱的变化，但 CCPS 在整个铁电转变过程中表现出明显且巨大的温度依赖性 XPS 异常，这表明 XPS 本质上对铁电顺序敏感。对 Cu 2p 光谱的详细分析表明，这些异常现象源于与极化切换相关的温度驱动的 Cu⁺ 离子迁移，而 Cr 核心水平的并发变化反映了 Cu 位移引起的二次晶格挤压和 d 电子相关性的调制。在铁电结构模型的支持下，我们进一步将 XPS 结合能变化与铜离子迁移的幅度相关联，从而能够直接从光谱可观测值定量估计离子位移。这项工作还强调了温度依赖性 XPS 为检测和量化铁电相变和离子迁移提供的丰富信息，为铁电材料和离子位移驱动的相变提供了通用化学探针。

English Abstract

Ferroelectric phase transitions are driven by ion displacement-induced symmetry breaking, yet their chemical signatures are rarely probed directly at the atomic level. Here, we establish temperature-dependent X-ray photoelectron spectroscopy (XPS) as a powerful and quantitative probe of ferroelectric phase transitions by tracking ion migration-induced changes in local chemical environments. Using van der Waals (vdW) layered ABP₂S₆ materials as a model system, we systematically compare CuCrP₂S₆ (CCPS), which undergoes a ferroelectric phase transition, with AgCrP₂S₆ (ACPS), which remains paraelectric over the same temperature range. While ACPS exhibits only smooth trivial thermal broadening and shifts in core-level spectra, CCPS shows pronounced and enormously large temperature-dependent XPS anomalies across the ferroelectric transition, demonstrating that XPS is intrinsically sensitive to ferroelectric order. Detailed analysis of the Cu 2p spectra reveals that these anomalies originate from temperature-driven Cu⁺ ion migration associated with polarization switching, whereas concurrent changes in the Cr core levels reflect secondary lattice squeezing and modulation of d-electron correlation induced by Cu displacement. Supported by a ferroelectric structural model, we further correlate XPS binding-energy shifts with the magnitude of Cu-ion migration, enabling a quantitative estimation of ionic displacement directly from spectroscopic observables. This work also highlights the rich information provided by temperature-dependent XPS for detecting and quantifying ferroelectric phase transitions and ion migration, offering a general chemical probe for ferroic materials and ion-displacement-driven phase transitions.

中文摘要

Co2⁺基双钙钛矿Sr2CoTeO₆因其非凡的磁性而引起人们的关注。从头算密度泛函理论计算揭示了最近邻之间存在大量铁磁 Kitaev 交换 |K|sim J/3，与面心立方晶格相结合，导致明显的交换挫败，并将 Sr2CoTeO₆ 置于具有中等 Kitaev 相互作用强度的 Kitaev 材料中。此外，Sr2CoTeO6被发现具有交变磁性，具有相当大的非相对论性自旋分裂，这可以产生横向压磁效应和非平凡的磁光响应。

English Abstract

The Co²⁺-based double perovskite Sr₂CoTeO₆ attracts attention due to its nontrivial magnetic properties. Ab initio density functional theory calculations reveal a substantial ferromagnetic Kitaev exchange |K|sim J/3 between nearest neighbors that, combined with the face-centered cubic lattice, leads to pronounced exchange frustration and places Sr₂CoTeO₆ among Kitaev materials with moderate Kitaev interaction strength. In addition, Sr₂CoTeO₆ is found to be altermagnetic with a sizable non-relativistic spin splitting, which can give rise to a transverse piezomagnetic effect and a nontrivial magneto-optical response.

中文摘要

具有大线性电光（普克尔斯）效应的材料在制造节能光子器件方面具有巨大潜力。以前的策略主要依赖于与相界、畴壁或应变诱导相变相关的结构柔软度，以在铁电体中产生大的普克尔斯效应。在这封信中，我们表明多轴铁电体中的极化重定向可能会产生结构柔软性，从而引起大的普克尔斯效应。通过发展现象学理论，我们发现多轴铁电体在偏振方位角和电场之间表现出滞后关系，并且在适当的偏振方位角处可能会出现线性电光系数的发散。这提出了一种在多轴铁电体中设计大普克尔斯效应的全新策略。我们对几种多轴铁电体进行了第一性原理模拟，并预测偏振重新取向可以显着提高单层 SnSe 和块体 KNbO₃ 的电光系数。这验证了我们策略的合理性，并强调多轴铁电体作为实现大普克尔斯效应的有前途的平台。

English Abstract

Materials with large linear electro-optic (Pockels) effect have significant potential for fabricating energy-efficient photonic devices. Previous strategies have mostly relied on structural softness---associated with phase boundary, domain wall, or strain induced phase transition---to create large Pockels effect in ferroelectrics. In this Letter, we show that polarization reorientation in multiaxial ferroelectrics may create structural softness and thus induce large Pockels effect. By developing a phenomenological theory, we find that multiaxial ferroelectrics exhibit a hysteretic relationship between the azimuth angles of the polarization and the electric field, and a possible divergence of the linear electro-optic coefficient may occur at an appropriate polarization azimuth angle. This suggests a brand-new strategy for engineering large Pockels effect in multiaxial ferroelectrics. We perform first-principles simulations on several multiaxial ferroelectrics and predict that polarization reorientation can significantly enhance the electro-optic coefficients in monolayer SnSe and bulk KNbO₃. This validates the soundness of our strategy and emphasizes multiaxial ferroelectrics as promising platforms toward large Pockels effect.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、magnetism、dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在氧化铁 Fe3BO6 中，弱铁磁性源于两个位点 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用 (DMI) 的竞争贡献以及两个 4c 和 8d 类型非等价 Fe3+ 位点的现场各向异性。因此，Fe3BO6 中的自旋重定向 (SR) 跃迁 γ2 → γ4 独特地包含 90^circ 自旋切换和 35% 的弱铁磁矩变化。我们探索这种转变的超快动力学，作为飞秒激光脉冲修改的弱铁磁性贡献的代表。使用磁光泵浦探针技术，我们揭示了 SR 转变是由 Э 相中弱铁磁矩的突然减小触发的，并将其与激光诱导的 DMI 修改和各向异性对自旋倾斜的贡献联系起来。通过调整不同光学吸收范围之间的泵浦脉冲声子能量，我们表明激光驱动对自旋倾斜的影响具有非热特性，并且是通过 Fe3⁺O2⁻₆ 八面体内的电荷转移跃迁 2p→3d 实现的，而不是通过 Fe3⁺ 上的局域 3d→3d 跃迁实现的。减弱的弱铁磁矩控制着 SR 转变的阈值和延迟以及最终状态。后者是通过激光诱导加热激活的进动切换到 Ђ4 相来实现的。最终状态被发现是 Ђ4 和 2 相共存，并且修改后的自旋倾斜甚至持续数百皮秒。

English Abstract

In the iron oxide Fe₃BO₆ weak ferromagnetism stems from competing contributions from two-site Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions (DMI) and from on-site anisotropies at the two 4c and 8d types of nonequivalent Fe³⁺ sites. As a result, spin reorientation (SR) transition Γ₂ → Γ₄ in Fe₃BO₆ uniquely comprises 90^circ spin switching and a 35% change of weak ferromagnetic moment. We explore ultrafast kinetics of such a transition as a proxy for weak ferromagnetism contributions modified by femtosecond laser pulses. Using magneto-optical pump-probe technique we reveal that the SR transition is triggered by an abrupt decrease of the weak ferromagnetic moment in the Γ₂ phase, and relate it to the laser-induced modification of the DMI and anisotropy contributions to the spin canting. By tuning the pump pulse phonon energy between different optical absorption ranges, we show that laser-driven impact on spin canting possesses a nonthermal character and is achieved through the charge-transfer transitions 2p→3d within Fe³⁺O²⁻₆ octahedra rather than by localized 3d→3d transitions at Fe³⁺. The decreased weak ferromagnetic moment controls the threshold and the latency of the SR transition, as well as a final state. The latter is achieved via the precessional switching to the Γ₄ phase activated by the laser-induced heating. Final state is found to be a coexistence of Γ₄ and Γ₂ phase with modified spin canting persisting even at hundreds of picoseconds.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、magnetic materials、magnetism。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

交变磁体最近作为一类新型磁性材料出现，它将补偿磁序与自旋分裂电子能带结构相结合。在这项工作中，我们采用分子束外延在 InP(111)A 衬底上生长厚度受控的 MnTe 薄膜。使用角分辨光电子能谱，我们观察到远低于费米能级的体能带大约 230 meV 的大自旋分裂，并识别了穿过费米能级的表面态。电传输测量表明，强大的反常霍尔 (AH) 效应持续到 2 K，并且 AH 符号反转发生在 175 K 附近。通过系统地调整薄膜厚度、生长条件和界面结构，我们证明 MnTe 薄膜中的 AH 响应源自表面态而不是体能带。我们的第一性原理计算表明，这种表面主导的 AH 效应受到体相变磁序的影响，并且对于具有相反 Mn 磁取向的终端保持不变。我们的研究结果建立了一种独特的块体交变磁性表面输运探针，证明界面工程是产生和控制交变磁体中 AH 效应的有效途径，并提供了对交变磁 MnTe 薄膜中 AH 响应的统一理解。

English Abstract

Altermagnets have recently emerged as a new class of magnetic materials that combine compensated magnetic order with spin-split electronic band structures. In this work, we employ molecular beam epitaxy to grow MnTe thin films with controlled thickness on InP(111)A substrates. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, we observe a large spin splitting of approx 230 meV for bulk bands well below the Fermi level and identify surface states that cross the Fermi level. Electrical transport measurements show that a robust anomalous Hall (AH) effect persists down to 2 K and an AH sign reversal occurs near 175 K. By systematically tuning film thickness, growth conditions, and interfacial structure, we demonstrate that the AH response in MnTe films originates from surface states rather than bulk bands. Our first-principles calculations reveal that this surface-dominated AH effect is imprinted by the bulk altermagnetic order and remains unchanged for terminations with opposite Mn magnetic orientations. Our results establish a unique surface transport probe of bulk altermagnetism, demonstrate interface engineering as an effective route to generate and control the AH effect in altermagnets, and provide a unified understanding of the AH response in altermagnetic MnTe films.

评估规范不变的 PEPS ansatz 在拓扑相变中的表达能力#

原题：Assessing the expressive power of a gauge-invariant PEPS ansatz across a topological phase transition

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、monte carlo simulation。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们研究了规范高斯费米子投影纠缠对态 (GGFPEPS) ansatz 的表达能力，它是 (2+1) 维晶格规范理论的明显规范不变张量网络 ansatz。在 GGFPEPS ansatz 中，每个链路上的规范场通过将其配置耦合到位于链路两端的虚拟费米子的费米子数来表示。在之前的工作~citeEmonts:2022yom 中，已经通过变分蒙特卡罗 (VMC) 方法研究了这个 ansatz。然而VMC的统计误差使得变分参数的精确确定变得困难，从而无法准确检验拟像的表达能力。在这项工作中，我们将计算重新表述为格拉斯曼张量网络（TN），其中整个网络确定性地收缩，并引入高阶张量重整化群（HOTRG），这使得我们能够进行高达 L=34 的大量计算。我们还使用角转移矩阵重正化群（CTMRG）评估无限体积系统。我们发现只有八个复变分参数的 ansatz 准确地描述了纯 Z2 规范理论，包括拓扑相变区域，不仅在能量方面而且在拓扑纠缠熵方面。从它们的磁化率峰值获得的临界耦合在数量上与连续时间量子蒙特卡罗模拟预测的一致~citewu2012phase。此外，在过渡点附近，我们表明 ansatz 代表了 Plallette 乘积族之外的一种状态，这是在 ansatz 的构造中假设的单参数状态族，作为强耦合极限和弱耦合极限之间的简单插值。

English Abstract

We investigate the expressive power of the gauged Gaussian fermionic projected entangled pair state (GGFPEPS) ansatz, which is a manifestly gauge-invariant tensor network ansatz for (2+1)-dimensional lattice gauge theories. In the GGFPEPS ansatz, the gauge field on each link is represented by coupling its configuration to the fermion number of virtual fermions placed at both ends of the link. This ansatz has been studied by variational Monte Carlo (VMC) methods in previous work~citeEmonts:2022yom. However, the statistical error of VMC makes a precise determination of the variational parameters difficult, so that the expressive power of the ansatz could not be examined accurately. In this work, we reformulate the computation as a Grassmann tensor network (TN), in which the whole network is contracted deterministically, and introduce the higher-order tensor renormalization group (HOTRG), which allows us to perform calculations in large volumes up to L=34. We also evaluate the infinite volume system using the corner transfer matrix renormalization group (CTMRG). We find that the ansatz with only eight complex variational parameters accurately describes the pure Z₂ gauge theory including the region of the topological phase transition, not only in the energy but also in the topological entanglement entropy. The critical coupling obtained from the peaks of their susceptibilities agrees quantitatively with the one predicted by continuous-time quantum Monte Carlo simulations~citewu2012phase. Furthermore, near the transition point, we show that the ansatz represents a state outside the plaquette product family, which is a one-parameter family of states assumed in the construction of the ansatz as a simple interpolation between the strong and weak coupling limits.

超越微磁学的原子级尖锐磁畴壁#

原题：Atomically Sharp Magnetic Domain Walls beyond Micromagnetics
作者：Guokai Liao、Stefan Blügel。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-10

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：原子级狭窄的磁畴壁可能出现在超薄磁性薄膜、范德华磁体以及由于竞争交换相互作用而有效交换刚度降低的系统中。当它们的宽度接近原子晶格间距时，微磁学基础的连续近似变得越来越不可靠。我们在结合了海森堡交换、单轴各向异性和 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用 (DMI) 的一维最近邻 J-K-D 自旋晶格模型中研究了此类畴壁的结构和稳定性。在没有 DMI 的情况下，我们分析证明了在临界交换各向异性比 J/K = 3/2 时向原子…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：domain wall、dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction、van der waals magnets。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

原子级狭窄的磁畴壁可能出现在超薄磁性薄膜、范德华磁体以及由于竞争交换相互作用而有效交换刚度降低的系统中。当它们的宽度接近原子晶格间距时，微磁学基础的连续近似变得越来越不可靠。我们在结合了海森堡交换、单轴各向异性和 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用 (DMI) 的一维最近邻 J-K-D 自旋晶格模型中研究了此类畴壁的结构和稳定性。在没有 DMI 的情况下，我们分析证明了在临界交换各向异性比 J/K = 3/2 时向原子级尖锐易轴壁的连续转变，其特征是局部 Hessian 模式的软化。原子自旋动力学模拟证实了这种转变，并揭示了用传统微磁解决方案拟合离散壁轮廓（如实验分析中常见的做法）低估了交换相互作用并高估了各向异性。有限 DMI 删除了作为静止配置的完美共线壁，用平滑交叉代替过渡到强局部化但倾斜的壁，该壁仅在强各向异性极限下渐近地接近共线易轴配置。我们推导了该状态下 DMI 引起的倾斜的分析表达式，并证明了其在临界比率附近强烈增强的倾斜响应。这些发现确立了原子窄畴壁的微磁描述的局限性，并提出了通过各向异性和 DMI 控制其结构的机会。

English Abstract

Atomically narrow magnetic domain walls can arise in ultrathin magnetic films, van der Waals magnets, and systems with reduced effective exchange stiffness due to competing exchange interactions. As their width approaches the atomic lattice spacing, the continuum approximation underlying micromagnetics becomes increasingly unreliable. We investigate the structure and stability of such domain walls in a one-dimensional nearest-neighbor J-K-D spin-lattice model incorporating Heisenberg exchange, uniaxial anisotropy, and Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI). In the absence of DMI, we demonstrate analytically a continuous transition to an atomically sharp easy-axis wall at the critical exchange-to-anisotropy ratio J/K = 3/2, characterized by the softening of a localized Hessian mode. Atomistic spin-dynamics simulations confirm this transition and reveal that fitting discrete wall profiles with the conventional micromagnetic solution, as commonly done in experimental analyses, underestimates the exchange interaction and overestimates the anisotropy. Finite DMI removes the perfectly collinear wall as a stationary configuration, replacing the transition by a smooth crossover toward a strongly localized but canted wall that approaches the collinear easy-axis configuration only asymptotically in the strong-anisotropy limit. We derive an analytical expression for the DMI-induced canting in this regime and demonstrate its strongly enhanced canting response near the critical ratio. These findings establish the limitations of micromagnetic descriptions of atomically narrow domain walls and suggest opportunities for controlling their structure through anisotropy and DMI.

通过布里渊光散射和磁光克尔效应测量界面 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用的良好实践#

原题：Good Practices for Measuring the Interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Interaction by Brillouin Light Scattering and Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect
作者：Marco Madami、Silvia Tacchi、Adriano Di Pietro、Alessandro Magni、Giovanni Gandini、Christian Rinaldi 等 13 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-11

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：dzyaloshinskii-moriya interaction、domain wall、skyrmion。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 界面相互作用 (iDMI) 是稳定手性磁态的关键成分，例如斯格明子和手性畴壁，它们是下一代自旋电子技术的关键构建模块。尽管 iDMI 常数 D 很重要，但其定量测定常常受到显着差异的困扰。本文建立了一套使用两种主要技术测量 iDMI 的良好实践：布里渊光散射 (BLS) 和磁光克尔效应 (MOKE)。通过对 Co/Pt 基异质结构的分析，这项工作为测量程序、数据评估和不确定性分析提供了全面的路线图。我们的分析强调了用于提取 iDMI 常数及其不确定性传播的磁参数的关键作用，以及用于增强测量可靠性的测量模型。所提出的方法为提高不同实验室和实验技术的 iDMI 测量的一致性和可重复性提供了一个实用的框架。

English Abstract

The interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (iDMI) is a key ingredient for stabilizing chiral magnetic states, such as skyrmions and chiral domain walls, which are key building blocks for next-generation spintronic technologies. Despite its importance, the quantitative determination of the iDMI constant, D, is often plagued by significant discrepancies. This paper establishes a set of good practices for measuring iDMI using two primary techniques: Brillouin Light Scattering (BLS) and the Magneto-Optical Kerr Effect (MOKE). Through the analysis of Co/Pt-based heterostructures, this work provides a comprehensive roadmap for measurement procedures, data evaluation, and uncertainty analysis. Our analysis highlights the crucial role of magnetic parameters used to extract the iDMI constant and propagation of their uncertainties, and the measurement model for enhancing measurement reliability. The proposed methodology provides a practical framework for improving the consistency and reproducibility of iDMI measurements across different laboratories and experimental techniques.

双极性液体中的液-液相转变。对过冷水的见解#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、molecular dynamics、ferroelectric。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

双极性液体结合了两个耦合的自由度，平移和双极性。有趣的是，这两个部门可以处于不同的热力学状态，其中一个部门的状态可能有利于另一个部门的特定状态。双极液体中铁电有序的发生是这种相互稳定的一个例子。由于这种耦合通常编码在相互作用电位中，因此投射出一个扇区以获得另一个扇区的有效相互作用可以揭示这种现象背后的微观机制。在大维极限中求解的粗粒度理论，其中最近邻粒子之间的距离取决于它们通过密度依赖性机械顺应性的偶极相互作用，这表明铁电跃迁可以触发液-液跃迁。它还预测了准电相内的一阶液-液转变，这反过来又可以促进铁电有序化。各向异性双极依赖性软核相互作用显示出导致有效的Jagla样各向同性双长度尺度相互作用，已知支持液-液相变。然而，在这里，双长度图案取决于密度和偶极子配置，因此对偶极子顺序敏感。过冷TIP4P/冰水分子动力学模拟分析表明，对相关函数的一坐标-壳特征对偶极相互作用敏感，支持粗粒度理论的基础。低密度液态水和高密度液态水的比较揭示了低密度液体中第一配位壳的空间各向异性增强，在理论上与增加的有效排除体积相关联，表明液-液转变涉及第一壳内的结构变化，超出了间隙区域和四面体秩序的重组。

English Abstract

Dipolar liquids combine two coupled degrees of freedom, translational and dipolar. Intriguingly, these two sectors can reside in distinct thermodynamic states, with the state of one sector possibly favouring a specific state of the other. The onset of ferroelectric order in dipolar liquids is an example of this mutual stabilization. Since this coupling is often encoded in interaction potentials, projecting out one sector to derive an effective interaction for the other can uncover the microscopic mechanism underlying this phenomenon. A coarse-grained theory, solved in the large-dimensional limit, in which the distance between nearest-neighbour particles depends on their dipolar interaction through a density-dependent mechanical compliance shows that a ferroelectric transition can trigger a liquid-liquid transition. It also predicts a first-order liquid-liquid transition within the paraelectric phase that, in turn, can promote ferroelectric ordering. The anisotropic dipolar-dependent soft-core interaction is shown to lead to an effective Jagla-like isotropic two-length-scale interaction, known to support liquid-liquid phase transitions. Here, however, the two-length-scale pattern depends on density and dipolar configuration, and is therefore sensitive to dipolar order. Analysis of supercooled TIP4P/Ice water molecular dynamics simulations shows that first-coordination-shell features of the pair correlation functions are sensitive to dipolar interaction, supporting the foundations of the coarse-grained theory. Comparison of low- and high-density liquid water reveals enhanced spatial anisotropy of the first coordination shell in the low-density liquid, associated, within the theory, with increased effective excluded volume, showing that the liquid-liquid transition involves structural changes within the first shell, beyond reorganization of the interstitial region and tetrahedral order.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

自旋轨道耦合、交换相互作用和阳离子排序之间的微妙相互作用可能导致过渡金属离子中出现奇异的磁性状态。我们报告了结合同步加速器 X 射线衍射 (XRD)、磁化率、μ 子自旋弛豫 (μSR) 测量和密度泛函理论 (DFT) 计算的 Re 基 (5d1) 有序双钙钛矿氧化物 Sr2ZnReO₆ 的综合研究。 XRD表明Sr2ZnReO6在低温下以单斜结构（空间群P21/n）结晶。磁化率数据显示低于 sim13 K 的转变，M--H 环显示类似铁磁的磁滞和 2 K 时异常高的 23 kOe 矫顽场。零场 μSR 测量检测到低于 T_M simeq 12 K 的静态和空间无序内部场，与 Hubbard-I 计算中详细 DFT 和力定理确定的倾斜反铁磁基态一致。 μSR分析和局域场模拟得出的降低的高温有效力矩(sim0.76~μ_B)和非常小的静力矩(lesssim 0.2~μ_B)表明自旋轨道耦合的决定性作用。通过实验和计算相结合的方法，我们明确地确定了 Sr2ZnReO6 中的倾斜反铁磁序，表明非常小的有序矩与异常大的矫顽力共存。这些结果强调了自旋轨道耦合和轨道排序的关键作用，为 5d1 双钙钛矿的磁性提供了新的见解。

English Abstract

The subtle interplay between spin-orbit coupling, exchange interactions, and cation ordering can lead to exotic magnetic states in transition-metal ions. We report a comprehensive study of the Re-based (5d¹) ordered double perovskite oxide Sr₂ZnReO₆ combining synchrotron x-ray diffraction (XRD), magnetic susceptibility, muon spin relaxation (μSR) measurements, and density functional theory (DFT) calculations. XRD reveals that Sr₂ZnReO₆ crystallizes in the monoclinic structure (space group P2₁/n) at low temperature. Magnetic susceptibility data indicate a transition below sim13 K, with M--H loops showing ferromagnetic-like hysteresis and an unusually high coercive field of 23 kOe at 2 K. Zero-field μSR measurements detect static and spatially disordered internal fields below T_M simeq 12 K, consistent with a canted antiferromagnetic ground state determined by detailed DFT and force-theorem in Hubbard-I calculations. The reduced high-temperature effective moment (sim0.76~μ_B) and very small static moment (lesssim 0.2~μ_B) derived from μSR analysis and local-field simulations indicate a decisive role of spin-orbit coupling. Through a combined experimental and computational approach we unambiguously determine the canted antiferromagnetic order in Sr₂ZnReO₆, showing that a very small ordered moment coexists with an exceptionally large coercivity. These results underscore the crucial role of spin-orbit coupling and orbital ordering, providing new insights into magnetism in 5d¹ double perovskites.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

尼尔森-二宫不可行定理要求周期性布里渊区中的总拓扑电荷 (C) 消失，通常会导致外尔点 (WP) 的相反手性对。带电节点表面（NS）提供了一种补偿相同手性外尔对的替代机制，但控制这种配置及其电子实现的一般对称约束仍未得到探索。在这里，通过对所有 1651 个磁性空间群 (MSG) 的详尽分析，无论有无自旋轨道耦合，我们仅识别出 13 个 II 型 MSG，它们允许实现最小不成对的混合维度配置，包括单个 |C|=2 NS 和两个相同手性 |C|=1 WP。在这种对称框架的指导下，我们将手性硼同素异形体 THC-B₃₀ 确定为这种非常规拓扑的第一个电子实现。第一性原理计算显示只有两个 C=-1 的 WP 和一个 C=+2 的 NS，满足全局拓扑电荷中性，并表现出连接 WP 和 NS 投影的非常规费米弧。此外，应变引起的拓扑相变在不同的能带简并流形之间重新分配拓扑电荷。我们的研究结果建立了一条对称引导的途径，通过超越传统外尔节点配对的混合维电荷补偿来实现相同手性外尔对。

English Abstract

The Nielsen-Ninomiya no-go theorem requires the total topological charge (C) in a periodic Brillouin zone to vanish, conventionally leading to opposite-chirality pairs of Weyl points (WPs). A charged nodal surface (NS) provides an alternative mechanism for compensating a same-chirality Weyl pair, yet the general symmetry constraints governing this configuration and its electronic realization remain unexplored. Here, by an exhaustive analysis of all 1651 magnetic space groups (MSGs), with and without spin-orbit coupling, we identify only 13 type-II MSGs that permit the realization of a minimal unpaired mixed-dimensional configuration comprising a single |C|=2 NS and two same-chirality |C|=1 WPs. Guided by this symmetry framework, we identify the chiral boron allotrope THC-B₃₀ as the first electronic realization of this unconventional topology. First-principles calculations reveal only two WPs with C=-1 and one NS with C=+2, satisfying global topological charge neutrality and exhibiting unconventional Fermi arcs connecting the WP and NS projections. Moreover, strain-induced topological phase transitions redistribute topological charge among distinct band-degeneracy manifolds. Our findings establish a symmetry-guided route to realizing same-chirality Weyl pairs through mixed-dimensional charge compensation beyond conventional Weyl-node pairing.

多通道声子互变驱动氧化铪中稳定畴壁的 90 度偏振旋转#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

极化旋转是铁电材料的基本机制，但这种旋转在萤石铁电体中如何在微观上产生并产生不同的磁畴壁（DW）仍未解决。声子模式为这种结构转变提供了一条微观途径，因为当它们的特征波长与相关物理尺寸相当时，几何方向可以改变它们的频率、色散关系和振动本征态。在这里，通过将凝聚声子模式分解与第一原理计算相结合，我们研究了 HfO2 铁电相中 90° 偏振旋转的起源。以立方母相为参考，我们证明偏振旋转源自布里渊区不同波矢处不同本征态的多个对称等效声子模式，其耦合和协作重定向不断演化以产生不同的 90 Pca21 变体和切换路径。在 90 个变体中，通过 Pmn21 的最低能量转换路径表现出的能垒与通过 P42/nmc 相的 180 偏振反转路径相当，但略低，表明 90 偏振旋转是一种能量上可实现的替代转换路径。我们进一步分析 90 DW，发现受挫声子模式出现在壁中心，其中凝聚模式的共存和竞争促进了晶格畸变的局部重新配置，从而导致了 DW 能量的升高。重要的是，STEM 成像的实验观察提供了稳定 90 DW 中原子模式畸变的直接证据，与理论上确定的低能 DW 一致。这些发现提供了 90° 开关动力学、竞争畴壁物理和宏观铁电行为的基本结构机制。

English Abstract

Polarization rotation is a fundamental mechanism in ferroelectric materials, yet how such rotation arises microscopically in fluorite ferroelectrics and gives rise to distinct domain walls (DW) remains unresolved. Phonon modes offer a microscopic route to such structural transformations, as geometric orientations can modify their frequencies, dispersion relations, and vibrational eigenstates when their characteristic wavelengths become comparable to relevant physical dimensions. Here, by combining condensed phonon mode decomposition with first-principles calculations, we investigate the origin of 90 polarization rotation in the ferroelectric phase of HfO2. Using the cubic parent phase as reference, we show that polarization rotation originates from multiple symmetry equivalent phonon modes of distinct eigenstates at different wavevectors in the Brillouin zone, whose coupling and cooperative reorientation continuously evolve to generate distinct 90 Pca21 variants and switching pathways. Among the 90 variants, the lowest-energy switching pathway through the Pmn21 exhibits an energy barrier comparable to, but slightly lower than, that of the 180 polarization reversal pathway through the P42/nmc phase, indicating that 90 polarization rotation is an energetically accessible alternative switching route. We further analyze 90 DW and find that frustrated phonon modes emerge at the wall center, where the coexistence and competition of condensed modes promote local reconfiguration of the lattice distortions, contributing to the elevated DW energies. Importantly, experimental observations with STEM imaging provide direct evidence of atomic mode distortions in stable 90 DW, consistent with the theoretically identified low-energy DW. These findings provide the fundamental structural mechanism underlying 90 switching dynamics, competing domain-wall physics, and macroscopic ferroelectric behavior.

中文摘要

硒化锰既以岩盐 α-MnSe（一种 II 型反铁磁体）的形式生长，也以亚稳态纤锌矿 w-MnSe 的形式生长，其磁序尚未测量。我们利用密度泛函理论加上线性响应 Hubbard U 计算了中性 Mn 和 Se 空位以及两种多晶型物的两个反位点，介于由 MnSe2 界定的富硒极限和由测量的形成焓设定的富锰极限之间。在 α-MnSe 中，Mn 空位在大部分窗口中最低（富硒条件下为 0.92 eV），与其 p 型传导一致。在w-MnSe中，Se反位在富Se条件下最低，窄地呈A型顺序，明显呈条带顺序，Se空位在富Mn条件下最低。尽管 DFT+U 将纤锌矿置于岩盐之下，但与实验相反，Se 空位在两种多晶型物中具有几乎相同的形成能，而改变 Mn 原子数量的缺陷则相差 0.4-1.1 eV。自旋简并条带序位于 w-MnSe 的互磁 A 型有序以下 14 meV 每个分子式单元，并且形成能在这些有序之间移动高达 0.24 eV，大部分是 Mn 空位。四个缺陷中的三个留下了 3-5 μ_B 的未补偿力矩。在 A 型顺序中，能带展开显示最强分裂能带的符号反转分裂（比价带最大值低约 230 meV 和 3 eV）将其峰值高度保持在 3% 缺陷分析的 8% 散射范围内，而其肩部上的点移动高达约 70 meV。带边状态尚未解决。

English Abstract

Manganese selenide grows both as rock-salt α-MnSe, a type-II antiferromagnet, and as metastable wurtzite w-MnSe, whose magnetic order has not been measured. We compute the neutral Mn and Se vacancies and the two antisites of both polymorphs with density functional theory plus a linear-response Hubbard U, between the Se-rich limit bounded by MnSe₂ and the Mn-rich limit set by the measured formation enthalpy. In α-MnSe the Mn vacancy is lowest over most of the window (0.92 eV under Se-rich conditions), consistent with its p-type conduction. In w-MnSe, the Se antisite is lowest under Se-rich conditions, narrowly in the A-type order and clearly in the stripe order, and the Se vacancy is lowest under Mn-rich conditions. The Se vacancy has nearly the same formation energy in both polymorphs, whereas defects that change the number of Mn atoms differ by 0.4-1.1 eV, although DFT+U places wurtzite below rock salt, contrary to experiment. A spin-degenerate stripe order lies 14 meV per formula unit below the altermagnetic A-type order of w-MnSe, and the formation energies shift between these orders by up to 0.24 eV, most for the Mn vacancy. Three of the four defects leave an uncompensated moment of 3-5 μ_B. In the A-type order, band unfolding shows that the sign-reversing splitting of the most strongly split band, about 230 meV and 3 eV below the valence-band maximum, keeps its peak height within the 8% scatter of the analysis at 3% defects, while points on its shoulders move by up to about 70 meV. Band-edge states are not resolved.

静电和挠曲电驱动的极性向列液晶的相变#

原题：Phase Transitions of Polar Nematic Liquid Crystals Driven by Electrostatics and Flexoelectricity
作者：Lincoln Paik、Jonathan V. Selinger。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-13

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition、ferroelectric、domain wall。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

极性液晶可以表现出一系列相和相变，从高温下的传统向列相到中温下的调制向列相（反铁电），然后到较低温度下的均匀极性（铁电）相。在这里，我们开发了这种相行为的理论。该理论基于挠曲电（有利于调制阶段的展开）和静电（为展开提供能量损失）之间的竞争。最小化自由能导致微分方程描述指向矢、极序和电势的空间变化。通过数值求解微分方程，我们确定了反铁电相的结构。在高温下，调制结构为正弦曲线。在较低温度下，它会转变为由窄畴壁隔开的宽畴结构。这些数值解可以与相变附近应用的简化模型进行比较。

English Abstract

Polar liquid crystals can exhibit a series of phases and phase transitions, from the conventional nematic phase at high temperature to a modulated nematic (antiferroelectric) phase at intermediate temperature, and then to a uniform polar (ferroelectric) phase at lower temperature. Here, we develop a theory for this phase behavior. The theory is based on a competition between flexoelectricity (which favors splay in the modulated phase) and electrostatics (which provides an energy penalty for splay). Minimizing the free energy leads to differential equations that describe the spatial variation of the director, the polar order, and the electric potential. By solving the differential equations numerically, we determine the structure of the antiferroelectric phase. At high temperature, the modulated structure is sinusoidal. At lower temperature, it crosses over to a structure of wide domains separated by narrow domain walls. These numerical solutions can be compared with simplified models that apply near the phase transitions.

中文摘要

(Hf,Zr)O2 材料系统由于与现有电子制造工艺兼容，因此在铁电器件方面具有广阔的前景。技术计算机辅助设计（TCAD）模拟可用于器件设计和优化，但TCAD器件模拟的预测能力取决于材料参数的质量。令人惊讶的是，人们对亚稳态铁电相的了解仍然知之甚少，这主要是由于合成纯相样品的困难。在这里，我们进行高质量的第一性原理计算，以提取 HfO2、Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O2 和 ZrO2 六种最常见相的可靠晶格参数、介电常数和带隙。我们比较了 GGA、元 GGA 和混合泛函的结果，以阐明结果在理论层面的敏感性，并将结果与早期文献联系起来。在能带结构的情况下，我们还考虑引力场修正以及温度引起的带隙重正化和激子效应的影响。最后，我们在 (Hf,Zr)O2 FeCAP 的 TCAD 模拟中使用这些结果来证明非铁电中间层对磁滞曲线和能带图的影响。

English Abstract

(Hf,Zr)O₂ material system is promising for ferroelectric devices, owing to their compatibility with the existing electronics manufacturing processes. Technology computer-aided design (TCAD) simulations can be used for the device design and optimization, but the predictive power of TCAD device simulations depends on the quality of the material parameters. These are still surprisingly poorly understood, also for the metastable ferroelectric phase, largely owing to the difficulties in synthesizing phase-pure samples. Here, we carry out high-quality first-principles calculations to extract reliable lattice parameters, dielectric constants, and band gaps for six of the most common phases of HfO₂, Hf₀.₅Zr₀.₅O₂, and ZrO₂. We compare results from GGA, meta-GGA, and hybrid functionals to shed light on the sensitivity of the results on the level of theory and to connect the results to earlier literature. In the case of band structures, we also consider GW corrections as well as the effects of temperature-induced band gap renormalization and excitonic effects. Finally, we use these results in TCAD simulations for (Hf,Zr)O₂ FeCAP to demonstrate the effect that non-ferroelectric interlayers could have on the hysteresis curves and band diagrams.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：superlattices、skyrmion。

页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Moiré superlattices have recently been extended from electronic materials to photonic systems, enabling complex topological light fields such as skyrmion bags. However, these realizations remain confined to two dimensions. Here, we demonstrate 3D moiré superlattices formed by twisting multi-volume spatiotemporal optical lattices, giving rise to previously undiscovered 3D quasiparticle topologies, including hopfion bags, hopfion quasicrystals, and hopfion-hopfionium nested superlattices. By tailoring the interference polarization textures of the moiré spacetime lattices, we achieve controlled topological transitions among these states, and tailored topological numbers of hopfion bags. These spatiotemporal supertopologies offer a promising platform for high-capacity information encoding in higher dimensions.

Lattice dynamical properties and interatomic force constants of transition metal oxide perovskite superlattices#

AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：In this study, we investigate the construction of efficient model potentials for perovskite heterostructures by transferring relevant dynamic quantities from bulk systems and short-period superlattices. Through first-pri…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：superlattices、perovskite、first-principles calculations。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

In this study, we investigate the construction of efficient model potentials for perovskite heterostructures by transferring relevant dynamic quantities from bulk systems and short-period superlattices. Through first-principles calculations, we provide a comprehensive analysis and comparison of the dynamical properties of different perovskite-based superlattice structures, including (PbTiO3 )n /(SrTiO3 )m superlattices, (ATiO3 )n /(AO) 2 superlattices, and An+1 Tin O 3n+1 Ruddlesden-Popper phases, where A can be Sr, Ba, or Pb. We demonstrate that as the thickness of these structures increases, the dynamical properties of the perovskite blocks converge rapidly towards their bulk counterparts. We discuss this convergence in terms of phonon density of states in reciprocal space and inter-atomic force constants in real space. Our findings suggest a straightforward strategy for developing transferable model potentials that accurately capture the dynamics of layered oxide compounds with progressively thicker layers.

Dynamic Molecular Transformation Mediated Crystallization Regulation and Interface Passivation in Tin Based Perovskite Solar Cells#

⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：Tin-based perovskites are promising candidates for lead-free photovoltaics but suffer from labile Sn²⁺ coordination, rapid crystallization, and severe interfacial defects. Here, we develop a dynamic molecular transformat…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

Tin-based perovskites are promising candidates for lead-free photovoltaics but suffer from labile Sn²⁺ coordination, rapid crystallization, and severe interfacial defects. Here, we develop a dynamic molecular transformation using 1-acetylpiperazine (PX) to regulate crystallization and interface chemistry simultaneously. During nucleation and growth, the carbonyl group coordinates with Sn²⁺ to stabilize precursor complexes, moderate supersaturation, and enable controlled crystal growth. Upon annealing, PX undergoes in situ molecular transformation, suggested to form amidinium-functionalized species that preferentially occupy surface A-site vacancies, forming a passivation layer and suppressing nonradiative recombination. This coupled bulk-to-interface evolution yields compact, highly crystalline films with improved optoelectronic quality. Devices achieve a power conversion efficiency of 15.18% and retain 94.1% of initial efficiency after 500 h of continuous illumination. This work demonstrates a dynamic molecular engineering approach integrating crystallization control and interface passivation, providing a pathway toward efficient and stable lead-free perovskite photovoltaics.

X-ray parametric down-conversion probes radiation-induced dynamics in hybrid perovskite MAPbBr3#

作者：S. Sofer、O. Sefi、G. J. Man、H. N. Barad、N. P. Jasti、D. Cahen 等 7 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-10
⚡ 磁性/铁电
AI×科学交叉2方向匹配8

📄 摘要：We report minute-timescale time-resolved measurements of X-ray parametric down-conversion (PDC) of X-rays into ultraviolet photons in a MAPbBr3 single crystal under continuous irradiation. Despite the known radiation sen…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：perovskite、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
English Abstract

We report minute-timescale time-resolved measurements of X-ray parametric down-conversion (PDC) of X-rays into ultraviolet photons in a MAPbBr3 single crystal under continuous irradiation. Despite the known radiation sensitivity of hybrid perovskites and the high photon flux required to detect the intrinsically weak PDC signal, the nonlinear response remains measurable throughout the irradiation sequence. Both PDC and Bragg diffraction exhibit a slow decay consistent with diffusion-driven damage dynamics on timescales reported for bromide-vacancy and proton migration. However, despite their similar decay rates, the PDC and Bragg signals evolve toward markedly different steady states, while an additional fast decay component is observed in the Bragg response but absent in the PDC signal. Density functional theory calculations identify N 2s states of the methylammonium cation as the most likely contributors to the measured PDC response and show that their Fourier component is only weakly affected by bromide-vacancy formation. These results establish nonlinear X-ray diffraction as a channel-selective probe of radiation-induced dynamics and demonstrate that radiation damage can couple differently to nonlinear and linear X-ray responses.

English Abstract

Niobium oxide dihalide films show a second-harmonic generation (SHG) efficiency scaling with their thickness, owing to their weak interlayer electronic coupling, but their use in integrated nonlinear photonics has been limited by the lack of robust electrical tuneability. Here we integrate ferroelectric NbOBr2, of thicknesses ranging from 20 nm to 40 nm, with a silicon nitride microring resonator to realize electrically reconfigurable SHG on a photonic chip. NbOBr2combines strong second-order susceptibility from intrinsic inversion-symmetry breaking with electrically switchable in-plane ferroelectric polarisation. The device achieves an SHG conversion efficiency of 158% W−1under continuous-wave excitation and a 53% electrical modulation depth. The polarisation states can also be programmed over multiple levels and retained after switching, enabling non-volatile control of the optical response. Using the fundamental and frequency-converted outputs as complementary channels, we further demonstrate dual-channel edge detection.

中文摘要

结合拓扑绝缘体 Bi2Te₃ 和铁电 Rashba 半导体 GeTe 的构建模块和结构图案，我们合成并全面表征了所得的 Ge2Bi2Te5 化合物，该化合物表现出明显的阳离子混合。使用动量分辨光电子能谱和从头计算，我们演示了阳离子混合如何驱动其电子结构从狭窄的避免交叉演化到大幅扩大的倒置带隙。我们确定了这种行为的微观根源在于阳离子亚晶格内Ge和Bi原子的重新分布，这增加了价带最大值附近Bi衍生态的贡献，并增强了自旋轨道耦合的有效贡献，从而稳定了拓扑电子结构。我们的研究结果表明，在晶体生长过程中可以控制的阳离子无序为拓扑电子结构的工程提供了额外的自由度，为功能量子材料的开发开辟了新的机会。

English Abstract

Combining the building blocks and structural motifs of the topological insulator Bi₂Te₃ and the ferroelectric Rashba semiconductor GeTe, we synthesized and comprehensively characterized the resulting Ge₂Bi₂Te₅ compound, which exhibits pronounced cation intermixing. Using momentum-resolved photoemission spectroscopy and ab initio calculations, we demonstrate how cation intermixing drives the evolution of its electronic structure from a narrow avoided crossing to a substantially enlarged inverted band gap. We establish the microscopic origin of this behavior in the redistribution of Ge and Bi atoms within the cation sublattice, which increases the contribution of Bi-derived states near the valence-band maximum and enhances the effective contribution of spin-orbit coupling, thereby stabilizing the topological electronic structure. Our findings demonstrate that cation disorder, which can be controlled during crystal growth, provides an additional degree of freedom for engineering topological electronic structures, opening new opportunities for the development of functional quantum materials.

中文摘要

我们证明光学驱动的手性声子会在非磁性中心对称材料中引起异常霍尔响应。使用第一原理计算来参数化双层 MoSe2 的有效 kcdotp 哈密顿量，我们表明 Eᵤ 声子的圆形激发会产生圆形动力学 Rashba 自旋轨道耦合。由此产生的 Floquet 带在两个谷处具有相同符号的贝里曲率，产生霍尔响应，其符号遵循声子旋向性。我们获得了 σₓysim10⁻²-0.21 frace²2πhbar 的范围，用于实验上可获得的光脉冲。我们的结果表明，手性声子提供了一种在超快时间尺度上控制电子贝里曲率和传输的途径。

English Abstract

We demonstrate that optically driven chiral phonons induce an anomalous Hall response in a nonmagnetic, centrosymmetric material. Using first-principles calculations to parameterize an effective kcdotp Hamiltonian for bilayer MoSe₂, we show that circular excitation of an Eᵤ phonon generates a circular dynamical Rashba spin-orbit coupling. The resulting Floquet bands carry same-sign Berry curvature at the two valleys, producing a Hall response whose sign follows the phonon handedness. We obtain a range of σₓysim10⁻²-0.21 frace²2πhbar for experimentally accessible light pulses. Our results show that chiral phonons provide a route to controlling electronic Berry curvature and transport on ultrafast time scales.

原题：Dynamical Casimir emission from Floquet-driven Landau polaritons: Gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

线性系统的超强光-物质耦合，例如太赫兹腔中二维电子气形成的朗道极化子，由玻色霍普菲尔德模型描述，并且规范不变性禁止超辐射相变（SRPT）。据预测，单独调制磁场可以增强循环平均耦合并引发 Floquet SRPT。我们开发了周期性驱动朗道极化子的精确理论。包括调制通量（法拉第定律）感应的电场，根据科恩定理，问题精确地简化为耦合到腔的电子质心模式，其哈密顿量是每个时刻的平方和。 Kerin 定理保证了所有高于可计算驱动频率（即 Kerin 频率）的阶次的稳定性，对此我们给出了独立的证明。在它之下，除了在共振舌内，系统是稳定的，在共振舌内，驱动器从真空中产生对，即动态卡西米尔效应。在高驱动频率下，电子经历平均场加上有质动力陷阱（福克-达尔文问题），而不是增强耦合。规范定理排除了任何能带结构的 Floquet 光子凝聚，包括凯恩非抛物线性，为此我们推导出广义的 Thomas-Reiche-Kuhn (TRK) 求和规则。预测的转变是通过在固定的朗道级基础上对耦合进行平均而产生的，这会产生一个违反 TRK 求和规则的有效模型；在驱动下，规范不变性也需要感应法拉第场。调制和平均最小耦合哈密顿量的规则，而不是其投影的参数，适用于任何线性、最小耦合系统。磁调制代替 SRPT，产生动态卡西米尔发射：法拉第驱动的极化子 - 低阈值的磁等离子对，以及具有高达 5--10~dB 的双模压缩的双色光子对。

English Abstract

Ultrastrong light--matter coupling of linear systems, such as Landau polaritons formed by a two-dimensional electron gas in a terahertz cavity, is described by the bosonic Hopfield model, and gauge invariance forbids a superradiant phase transition (SRPT). Modulating the magnetic field alone has been predicted to enhance the cycle-averaged coupling and induce a Floquet SRPT. We develop an exact theory of periodically driven Landau polaritons. Including the electric field induced by the modulated flux (Faraday's law), the problem reduces exactly, by Kohn's theorem, to the electronic center-of-mass mode coupled to the cavity, whose Hamiltonian is a sum of squares at every instant. A theorem of Krein then guarantees stability to all orders above a computable drive frequency, the Krein frequency, for which we give a self-contained proof. Below it, the system is stable except within resonance tongues where the drive creates pairs from the vacuum, the dynamical Casimir effect. At high drive frequency the electrons experience the mean field plus a ponderomotive trap (the Fock--Darwin problem), not an enhanced coupling. A gauge theorem excludes a Floquet photon condensate for any band structure, including Kane nonparabolicity, for which we derive a generalized Thomas--Reiche--Kuhn (TRK) sum rule. The predicted transition arises from averaging the coupling in a fixed Landau-level basis, which yields an effective model that violates the TRK sum rule; under driving, gauge invariance also requires the induced Faraday field. The rule to modulate and average the minimal-coupling Hamiltonian, not the parameters of its projection, applies to any linear, minimally coupled system. Instead of an SRPT, magnetic modulation produces dynamical Casimir emission: Faraday-driven polariton--magnetoplasmon pairs at low threshold, and two-color photon pairs with up to 5--10~dB of two-mode squeezing.

原题：Phase transitions in the simplicial Ising model on hypergraphs

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：phase transition。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

复杂的高阶相互作用可以重塑复杂系统中的高阶动力学。对高阶相互作用可以触发的相变的系统理解仍然不完整。在这里，我们研究超图上的单纯伊辛模型中的相变，其中只有当所有成员自旋一致对齐时，每个超边内的能量才会降低。我们证明它的哈密顿量相当于低阶相互作用的加权和，并使用朗道自由能根据超边尺寸来识别不同的相变。对于 q 均匀超图，在三临界点 q = 4 处，跃迁的性质从连续转变为不连续。当成对边和大小 q > 2 的超边共存时，会出现不同的情况，包括混合阶和双跃迁（对于 q > 8）。我们进一步采用 Bethe--Peierls 方法来研究成对和高阶相互作用在实现全局磁化过程中的相互作用，阐明双跃迁的多尺度性质。

English Abstract

Complex higher-order interactions can reshape higher-order dynamics in complex systems. Systematic understanding of the repertoire of phase transitions that higher-order interactions can trigger remains incomplete. Here we study the phase transitions in the simplicial Ising model on hypergraphs, in which the energy within each hyperedge is lowered only when all the member spins are unanimously aligned. We show that its Hamiltonian is equivalent to a weighted sum of lower-order interactions and use the Landau free energy to identify diverse phase transitions depending on the hyperedge sizes. For q-uniform hypergraphs, the nature of transitions shifts from continuous to discontinuous at the tricritical point q = 4. When both pairwise edges and hyperedges of size q > 2 coexist, diverse scenarios emerge, including mixed-order and double transitions, for q > 8. We further adopt the Bethe--Peierls method to investigate the interplay between pairwise and higher-order interactions in achieving global magnetization, illuminating the multiscale nature of double transitions.

AI / 机器学习专题#

覆盖模型、方法与 AI for Science 应用工作。

01

具有机器学习潜力的电化学系统的原子模拟#

原题：Atomistic simulations of electrochemical systems with machine learning potentials
作者：Marialore Sulpizi、Jörg Behler。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-13
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：现代机器学习潜力 (MLP) 能够以前所未有的精度实现大规模原子模拟。显式物理相互作用（例如长程静电）的结合不断扩大了它们在化学和材料科学中日益复杂的系统的适用性。然而，将 MLP 扩展到电化学和电催化仍然具有挑战性，因为它需要考虑原子相互作用中的电极电位和电场。此类方法最近才出现，尽管该领域正在迅速发展，但仍需要进一步的概念进步，以便在施加电势下在有限温度下进行可靠的模拟。在这篇综述中，我们全面概述了使用 MLP 进行电气化界面原子模…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：machine learning potential、electronic structure calculation、born effective charges。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

现代机器学习潜力 (MLP) 能够以前所未有的精度实现大规模原子模拟。显式物理相互作用（例如长程静电）的结合不断扩大了它们在化学和材料科学中日益复杂的系统的适用性。然而，将 MLP 扩展到电化学和电催化仍然具有挑战性，因为它需要考虑原子相互作用中的电极电位和电场。此类方法最近才出现，尽管该领域正在迅速发展，但仍需要进一步的概念进步，以便在施加电势下在有限温度下进行可靠的模拟。在这篇综述中，我们全面概述了使用 MLP 进行电气化界面原子模拟的最新进展。当前的发展分为三个不同的类别。第一个包括描述极化、极化率和玻恩有效电荷的线性响应模型，将传统 MLP 扩展到有限电场。第二个包括直接学习场相关势能面的方法。第三种由一系列将经典模型与机器学习相结合的混合方法组成。尽管仍在积极开发中，但这些方法代表了朝着更真实的模拟接近电子结构计算质量的重要一步。最后，我们讨论了推进基于 MLP 的模拟的开放挑战和可能的未来方向，旨在自下而上地理解电气化接口的原子过程。

English Abstract

Modern machine learning potentials (MLPs) enable large-scale atomistic simulations at unprecedented accuracy. The incorporation of explicit physical interactions such as long-range electrostatics has continuously broadened their applicability to increasingly complex systems in chemistry and materials science. Extending MLPs to electrochemistry and electrocatalysis, however, is still challenging as it requires the consideration of electrode potentials and electric fields in the atomic interactions. Such approaches have emerged only recently, and although the field is evolving rapidly, further conceptual advances are still needed for carrying out reliable simulations at finite temperature under applied potential. In this review, we provide a comprehensive overview about recent progress in the atomistic simulation of electrified interfaces using MLPs. Current developments fall into three different categories. The first comprises linear response models that describe polarisation, polarisability, and Born effective charges, extending conventional MLPs to finite electric fields. The second includes methods that directly learn a field-dependent potential energy surface. The third consists of a series of hybrid approaches combining classical models with machine learning. Although still under active development, these methods represent a major step toward more realistic simulations approaching the quality of electronic structure calculations. Finally, we discuss open challenges and possible future directions for advancing MLP-based simulations aiming for a bottom-up understanding of atomistic processes at electrified interfaces.

02

具有不确定性量化的机器学习原子间势的物理学先验知识#

作者：George I. Simmons、Thomas D. Swinburne、Thomas Hudson、James R. Kermode。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-09

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、neural network potential、active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势的标准不确定性量化方法通常无法量化模型错误指定的影响，尽管它是一个突出的错误来源。为了解决这个问题，错误指定的不确定性方案已经引起了人们的关注，其中逐点最优参数集回归（POPS-Regression）就是其中之一。然而，没有给出对后验分布的物理性的保证，并且与真实误差相比，不确定性估计可以是保守的。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种模型错误指定感知方案，该方案基于 POPS 回归，允许用户指定在获得的模型集合中强制执行的物理先验。这使得用户能够在不影响物理特性的情况下纳入错误指定的不确定性。该方案适用于可以由一组凸参数表示的任何物理量，因此对于各种静态属性都具有鲁棒性，这有助于为动态模拟提供信息。在这里，我们演示了声子振动和弹性特性的方案。该方案还通过利用两种架构中可用的线性度，应用于线性和消息传递神经网络潜力；对于神经网络势，我们将读出层近似为基于固定非线性特征的线性模型。该方法的多功能性为数据集构建提供了一种替代手工或主动学习方法的方法，通过添加数据来纠正物理故障，从而减少为特定应用设计潜力的计算开销。

English Abstract

Standard uncertainty quantification methods for machine learning interatomic potentials often fail to quantify the effects of model misspecification despite its prominence as a source of error. To combat this, misspecification-aware uncertainty schemes have been gaining attention, pointwise optimal parameter set regression (POPS-Regression) among them. However, no guarantees on the physicality of the posterior distribution are given and uncertainty estimates can be conservative compared to the true error. To combat this, we present a model misspecification aware scheme that builds on POPS-Regression by allowing users to specify physical priors that are enforced across the obtained ensemble of models. This allows the user to incorporate misspecification-aware uncertainties without compromising on physical properties. The scheme is applicable to any physical quantity that can be represented by a convex set of parameters and so is robust for a wide variety of static properties which can help inform dynamical simulations. Here, we demonstrate the scheme for phononic vibrations and elastic properties. The scheme is also applied to both linear and message-passing neural network potentials by exploiting the linearity available in both architectures; for neural network potentials we approximate the readout layer as a linear model based on fixed non-linear features. The versatility of the method provides an alternative to hand-crafted or active learning approaches to data-set construction to correct physical failures by adding data thus reducing the computational overhead for designing potentials for specific applications.

MCSA：SO(3) 等变 GNN 中原子间势的多截止谱聚合#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、equivariant graph neural network、graph neural networks。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

基于等变图神经网络（EGNN）的机器学习原子间势（MLIP）在原子建模方面取得了显着进展。然而，准确地表示不同空间尺度上的原子相互作用仍然具有挑战性。在这项工作中，我们研究了 SO(3) 等变消息传递的径向谱特性，并确定截止半径控制径向相互作用的特征空间频率尺度。基于此分析，我们提出了多截止谱聚合（MCSA），这是一种即插即用的模块，可聚合具有不同截止半径的径向核以丰富多尺度几何表示。为了实现高效的实现，我们利用径向核的紧凑支持在角度张量积运算之前融合截止相关的径向权重，避免冗余的角度计算，同时保持旋转等方差。跨代表性 EGNN 主干的广泛实验证明了能量、力和应力预测的持续改进，以及高阶属性（包括声子色散和晶格热导率）的准确性的提高。对具有不同相互作用尺度的层状材料的进一步评估证明了物理驱动的截止选择的有效性。代码位于：https://github.com/JF-Tan/MCSA。

English Abstract

Machine Learning Interatomic Potentials (MLIPs) based on Equivariant Graph Neural Networks (EGNNs) have achieved remarkable progress in atomistic modeling. However, accurately representing atomic interactions across different spatial scales remains challenging. In this work, we investigate the radial spectral properties of SO(3)-equivariant message passing and establish that the cutoff radius controls the characteristic spatial-frequency scale of radial interactions. Based on this analysis, we propose Multi-Cutoff Spectral Aggregation (MCSA), a plug-and-play module that aggregates radial kernels with different cutoff radii to enrich multi-scale geometric representations. To achieve efficient implementation, we exploit the compact support of radial kernels to fuse cutoff-dependent radial weights before angular tensor-product operations, avoiding redundant angular computations while preserving rotational equivariance. Extensive experiments across representative EGNN backbones demonstrate consistent improvements in energy, force, and stress prediction, as well as enhanced accuracy in higher-order properties, including phonon dispersion and lattice thermal conductivity. Further evaluations on layered materials with distinct interaction scales demonstrate the effectiveness of physically motivated cutoff selection. Code is available at: https://github.com/JF-Tan/MCSA.

主动学习和显式静电学能够实现电解质的精确建模#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 将近乎从头计算的精度与经典力场的计算效率相结合，使其成为分子液体和电解质建模的有前途的工具。它们的预测准确性在很大程度上取决于训练集的多样性，而静电的显式处理越来越被认为是提高其性能的重要因素。在这项工作中，我们通过将短程矩张量势 (MTP) 与静电增强 MTP（包括固定电荷和环境相关电荷模型）进行比较，评估了结合显式静电和原子电荷环境依赖性的影响。为此，我们采用基于 D 最优的主动学习 (AL) 来训练短程 MTP，并将以前无法用于长程静电增强 MTP 的 AL 程序扩展到这些模型，从而能够在很大程度上自动生成纯碳酸乙烯酯 (EC) 和碳酸甲乙酯 (EMC)、它们的混合物和 LiPF6 溶液的各种训练集。对于 EC、EMC 及其混合物，静电增强 MTP 的精度可与短程同类产品相当或更好，同时需要更少的机器学习参数。然而，对于 LiPF6 解决方案，只有通过与环境相关的电荷才能实现精度的显着提高，这也增强了大规模分子动力学模拟中的稳定性，与固定电荷模型不同。由此产生的短程 MTP 和环境相关电荷模型再现了平均绝对偏差为 0.2-6% 的实验密度和偏差为 11-15% 的离子电导率，同时捕获了实验观察到的电导率的温度依赖性。

English Abstract

Machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) combine near-ab initio accuracy with the computational efficiency of classical force fields, making them promising tools for modeling molecular liquids and electrolytes. Their predictive accuracy depends strongly on the diversity of the training set, while the explicit treatment of electrostatics is increasingly recognized as an important factor in improving their performance. In this work, we assess the impact of incorporating explicit electrostatics and environment-dependence of atomic charges by comparing short-ranged Moment Tensor Potentials (MTPs) with electrostatics-augmented MTPs, including fixed-charge and environment-dependent charge models. To this end, we employ D-optimality-based active learning (AL) to train short-ranged MTPs and extend the AL procedure, previously unavailable for long-range electrostatics-augmented MTPs, to these models, enabling largely automated generation of diverse training sets for pure ethylene carbonate (EC) and ethyl methyl carbonate (EMC), their mixtures, and LiPF6 solutions. For EC, EMC, and their mixtures, electrostatics-augmented MTPs achieve accuracy comparable to or better than that of their short-ranged counterparts while requiring fewer machine-learned parameters. For LiPF6 solutions, however, a notable improvement in accuracy is achieved only with environment-dependent charges, which also enhance stability in large-scale molecular dynamics simulations, unlike the fixed-charge model. The resulting short-ranged MTPs and environment-dependent-charge models reproduce experimental densities with mean absolute deviations of 0.2-6% and ionic conductivities with deviations of 11-15%, while capturing the experimentally observed temperature dependence of conductivity.

对齐异构 DFT 数据集：跨功能形成能的图神经网络方法#

原题：Aligning heterogeneous DFT datasets: a graph neural network approach to cross-functional formation energies
作者：Yidong Huang、Tenglong Lu、Hanwen Kang、Junfeng Huang、Sheng Meng、Miao Liu。npj Computational Materials，2026-11-10
🤖 AI/机器学习
AI×科学交叉8方向匹配8

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：对齐异构 DFT 数据集：跨功能形成能的图神经网络方法

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural network、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对齐异构 DFT 数据集：跨功能形成能的图神经网络方法

English Abstract

Heterogeneous density functional theory (DFT) calculations, particularly plane-wave implementations, introduce systematic formation energy errors ranging from tens to hundreds of meV/atom, depending on the selection of exchange-correlation functionals, kinetic energy cutoffs, pseudopotentials, and dispersion corrections. As demonstrated by the MatPES dataset, identical structures can exhibit an average energy discrepancy of 107 meV/atom between PBE and r2SCAN calculations. Such method-dependent discrepancies hinder the integration of multi-source DFT data, greatly limiting the scale and quality of datasets for training robust materials AI models. Here, we resolve this fundamental data silo barrier via graph-based transfer learning. Leveraging 380,190 structurally paired PBE–r2SCAN entries from the MatPES database, we train a structure-aware graph neural network to predict cross-functional energy residuals and align inconsistent DFT energy scales. By adopting GPTFF model architecture, the model converts conventional PBE energies to r2SCAN-level accuracy with a mean absolute error of 14.3 meV/atom, compared with 18.2 meV/atom achieved by CHGNet. This versatile approach effectively upgrades massive legacy PBE datasets to high-precision r2SCAN standards. It enables reliable predictions of phase stability, battery voltage profiles, and reaction thermodynamics, while allowing the integration of multi-source DFT data to advance the development of high-performance materials foundation models.
