页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

垃圾填埋场排放是发展中国家快速城市化城市面临的主要环境挑战。从管理不善的垃圾填埋场释放的甲烷、颗粒物和产生气味的化合物会导致空气污染，并对周围社区构成风险。本研究评估了实验室模拟生物覆盖系统的有效性，该系统使用6: 2: 1: 1混合比的垃圾填埋表土、堆肥、稻壳和蚯蚓粪便配制，以减轻尼日利亚拉各斯Ojota垃圾填埋场的微生物和气体排放。在整个垃圾填埋场以15米的间隔建立的六个采样点进行空间信息实地测量，通过QFIELD和QGIS记录和绘制坐标，以提供整个研究区域排放梯度的准确空间表示。监测空气质量参数，包括颗粒物（ PM2.5和PM10 ）、二氧化硫（ SO ₂ ）和一氧化碳（ CO ） ，以确定基线环境条件。为了扩展这种基于点的评估，将六种机器学习方法（随机森林、梯度增强（ XGBoost/LightGBM ）、支持向量机、K-最近邻、高斯过程回归/回归克里格和人工神经网络）应用于地理参考空气质量数据集，以按污染物驱动因素对AQI状态进行分类，并在填埋场足迹中生成连续的、具有不确定性的污染物风险面。测定微生物指标，包括总细菌计数（ TBC ）、总大肠菌群计数（ TCC ）和粪便大肠菌群（ FC ） ，而物理化学表征涉及对采集的土壤样品的pH测量和傅里叶变换红外光谱（ FTIR ）。结果显示，生物覆盖施用后微生物指标显著降低，覆盖材料内的生物调节得到改善， pH值保持在有利于微生物氧化过程的近中性条件下。傅里叶变换红外光谱进一步揭示了与有机物稳定和生物降解相关的官能团。本研究的新颖之处在于将地理空间空气质量测绘和基于机器学习的预测与微生物和基于FTIR的生物化学表征相结合，以评估热带垃圾填埋条件下本地配制的生物覆盖系统，为改善垃圾填埋空气质量管理提供可持续且具有成本效益的策略。

English Abstract

Landfill emissions represent a major environmental challenge in rapidly urbanizing cities within developing countries. Methane, particulate matter, and odor-generating compounds released from poorly managed landfill sites contribute to air pollution and pose risks to surrounding communities. This study evaluates the effectiveness of a laboratory-simulated bio-cover system formulated using landfill topsoil, compost, rice husk and earthworm castings in a 6:2:1:1 mix ratio for mitigating microbial and gaseous emissions at the Ojota landfill in Lagos, Nigeria. Spatially informed field measurements are conducted at six sampling points established at 15m intervals across the landfill, coordinates recorded and mapped via QFIELD and QGIS to provide accurate spatial representation of emission gradients across the study area. Air quality parameters including particulate matter (PM2.5 and PM10), sulfur dioxide (SO₂), and carbon monoxide (CO) are monitored to establish baseline environmental conditions. To extend this point-based assessment, six machine learning approaches — Random Forest, Gradient Boosting (XGBoost/LightGBM), Support Vector Machine, K-Nearest Neighbours, Gaussian Process Regression/Regression Kriging, and an Artificial Neural Network — are applied to the geo-referenced air quality dataset to classify AQI status by pollutant driver and to generate a continuous, uncertainty-aware pollutant risk surface across the landfill footprint. Microbial indicators including total bacterial count (TBC), total coliform count (TCC), and fecal coliform (FC) are determined, while physicochemical characterization involves pH measurements and Fourier transform infrared spectroscopy (FTIR) over soil samples collected. Results show significant reductions in microbial indicators following bio-cover application, improved biological regulation within the cover material, pH values remain within near-neutral conditions favorable for microbial oxidation processes. FTIR spectra further reveal functional groups associated with organic matter stabilization and biodegradation. The novelty of this study lies in integrating geospatial air quality mapping and machine learning-based prediction with microbiological and FTIR-based biochemical characterization to evaluate a locally formulated bio-cover system under tropical landfill conditions, providing a sustainable and cost-effective strategy for improving landfill air quality management.

English Abstract

Exploratory ab initio molecular dynamics is used to construct machine-learning interatomic potentials for NO interactions with highly oriented pyrolytic graphite at 33% bridge-site oxygen coverage. Representative configurations sampled across surface temperatures and incidence energies provide reference energies and forces for training and validation. The selected potential enables relaxation of 300 adsorption configurations and construction of spatial and angular interaction maps. These maps reveal increasingly restricted attractive regions during vertical approach and reduced short-range repulsion upon molecular reorientation. The resulting adsorption geometries and interaction landscapes guide further first-principles sampling toward refining potentials for NO oxidation dynamics.

SimPoly：利用源自第一原理的机器学习力场模拟聚合物#

原题：SimPoly: Simulation of polymers with machine learning force fields derived from first principles
作者：Gregor N. C. Simm、Jean Hélie、Hannes Schulz、Yicheng Chen、Guillem Simeon、Anna Kuzina 等 16 人。Nature Communications，2026-11-09

English Abstract

Polymers are a versatile class of materials with widespread industrial applications. Advanced computational tools could revolutionize their design, but the complex, multi-scale nature of polymers poses significant modeling challenges. Classical force fields often lack the accuracy and transferability that polymers demand, while quantum-chemical methods are prohibitively expensive for the large systems and long timescales involved. Here we develop a fast, scalable machine learning force field and show that, trained on quantum-chemical calculations alone, without any experimental data, it predicts measured bulk properties across a broad range of polymers. It reproduces polymer densities as accurately as, or better than, established classical force fields and captures the glass transition signature in the density-temperature curve, enabling the prediction of glass transition temperatures. To accelerate progress in this domain, we release a benchmark of experimental bulk properties for 130 polymers and an accompanying quantum-chemical dataset, laying the foundation for a fully in silico design pipeline for next-generation polymeric materials.

中文摘要

我们证明，将单个机器学习校正应用于使用两种最常用的能量泛函的密度泛函理论计算的带隙，会产生接近实验值的带隙。这解决了计算高效的电子结构方法的经典问题，给出了可以与实验进行比较的可靠带隙。所采用的方法依赖于机器学习模型的材料特定特征工程，并且被证明与基于 GW 近似或混合泛函的更昂贵的计算具有接近的准确性。这里报告的研究结果开启了在需要可靠带隙的材料设计工作流程中使用高效计算的可能性，这对针对大型计算数据库的研究或本质上完全预测的研究具有影响。

English Abstract

We demonstrate that a single machine-learning correction, applied to bandgaps from density functional theory calculations with two of the most frequently used energy functionals, results in bandgaps that are close to the experimental values. This solves a classical problem of computationally efficient electronic structure methods, to give reliable bandgaps that can be compared to experiments. The employed approach relies on materials specific feature engineering of machine learning models, and is shown to have close accuracy to that of much more expensive calculations based on the GW-approximation or hybrid functionals. The findings reported here open the possibility to use efficient calculations in materials design workflows that need reliable bandgaps, with implications for investigations targeting large computational databases or for investigations that are entirely predictive in nature.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

机器学习力场能够以接近参考的精度进行原子模拟，并大大降低了计算成本，但它们的开发仍然分散在软件框架中，数据格式、配置方案、依赖性和执行过程不兼容。 Orchestr.AI 通过统一的工作流程解决了这一限制，该工作流程协调不同的 MLIP 软件框架（称为引擎），用于数据准备、模型训练、微调、评估和部署。通用的 YAML 接口定义每个实验，而特定于引擎的环境配置文件保留兼容的软件堆栈并避免依赖冲突。该平台目前通过相应的软件实现支持五种MLIP模型架构，即SchNet、PaiNN、NequIP、Allegro和MACE，提供跨这些框架的自动化配置映射和标准化数据集处理。 rMD17 和 3BPA 数据集的基准测试表明，Orchestr.AI 再现了预期的性能趋势，同时实现了受控的跨引擎比较。使用固定配置和发动机配置文件重复执行可产生高度一致的结果，展示了实际工作流程的可重复性。除了训练之外，Orchestr.AI 还提供模块化架构接口，将训练后的模型连接到基于 ASE 的模拟、批量推理、认知不确定性估计、校准和主动学习工作流程。因此，该平台提供了一个可重复和可扩展的基础设施，用于开发、比较和部署机器学习原子间势，我们在这里通过跨引擎准确性和可重复性基准对其进行验证。

English Abstract

Machine-learning force fields enable atomistic simulations at near-reference accuracy and substantially reduced computational cost, but their development remains fragmented across software frameworks with incompatible data formats, configuration schemes, dependencies, and execution procedures. Orchestr.AI addresses this limitation through a unified workflow that coordinates different MLIP software frameworks, referred to as engines, for data preparation, model training, fine-tuning, evaluation, and deployment. A common YAML interface defines each experiment, while engine-specific environment profiles preserve compatible software stacks and avoid dependency conflicts. The platform currently supports five MLIP model architectures, namely SchNet, PaiNN, NequIP, Allegro, and MACE, through their corresponding software implementations, providing automated configuration mapping and standardized dataset handling across these frameworks. Benchmarks on the rMD17 and 3BPA datasets show that Orchestr.AI reproduces the expected performance trends while enabling controlled cross-engine comparisons. Repeated executions with fixed configurations and engine profiles produce highly consistent results, demonstrating practical workflow reproducibility. Beyond training, Orchestr.AI provides modular architectural interfaces that connect trained models to ASE-based simulations, batch inference, epistemic uncertainty estimation, calibration, and active-learning workflows. The platform therefore provides a reproducible and extensible infrastructure for developing, comparing, and deploying machine-learning interatomic potentials, which we validate here through cross-engine accuracy and repeatability benchmarks.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

用于分子和材料属性预测的机器学习 (ML) 模型通常在通过任意或通用采样策略管理的大型数据集上进行训练。这种方法可能会导致与预测任务最相关的化学和材料空间区域的代表性过高或过低。因此，一个重要的挑战是如何有效地管理训练数据，以最大限度地提高预测性能，同时最大限度地减少所需的标记示例的数量。在这里，我们系统地比较了分子和材料特性预测的训练数据获取策略，从随机采样到各种主动学习方法。我们评估它们在不同模型类、表示、数据集和目标属性上的性能。我们的结果表明，数据采集策略的有效性在很大程度上取决于模型、数据集和预测任务之间的相互作用，尽管基于不确定性的采样通常优于替代策略。根据这些发现，我们得出了在给定标签预算下选择数据采集策略的指南。

English Abstract

Machine-learning (ML) models for molecular and materials property prediction are often trained on large datasets curated through arbitrary or generic sampling strategies. Such approaches can lead to the over- or under-representation of regions of chemical and materials space that are most relevant to the prediction task. An important challenge is therefore how to efficiently curate training data to maximize predictive performance while minimising the number of labelled examples required. Here, we systematically compare training data acquisition strategies for molecular and materials property prediction, ranging from random sampling to various active learning approaches. We evaluate their performance across different model classes, representations, datasets, and target properties. Our results show that the effectiveness of data acquisition strategies depend strongly on the interplay between the model, dataset, and prediction task, although uncertainty-based sampling generally outperforms alternative strategies. Based on these findings, we derive guidelines for selecting data acquisition strategies under a given labelling budget.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

前体-基底-反应物组合是否产生原子层沉积（ALD）工艺仍然需要通过反复试验来确定。在这里，我们介绍 AJIN（ALD 联合可解释网络），这是一种机器学习框架，可以从实验结果中学习这一决策。我们编制了 560 个实验观察到的 ALD 和区域选择性 ALD 结果，涵盖 76 种前体、27 种底物、20 种反应物和 25-620 摄氏度。在 ALD 循环之后，AJIN 预测前体和基底的图神经网络嵌入随温度的吸附，以及通过添加反应物的沉积。最佳模型 (CHGNet-MLP) 的吸附平衡精度为 79.2%，沉积平衡精度为 84.8%，并且部分转移到看不见的前体。 SHAP 和烧蚀分析表明，基质化学控制吸附，而前体、基质和反应物均有助于沉积。根据我们实验室的 ALD 实验进行测试，AJIN 正确预测了 83% 的新基质情况和 71% 的新前体-基质吸附情况，为优先考虑 ALD 和区域选择性 ALD 化学提供了一个基于实验的工具。

English Abstract

Whether a precursor-substrate-reactant combination yields an atomic layer deposition (ALD) process is still settled by trial-and-error experiments. Here we introduce AJIN (ALD Joint Interpretable Network), a machine-learning framework that learns this decision from experimental outcomes. We compiled 560 experimentally observed ALD and area-selective ALD outcomes spanning 76 precursors, 27 substrates, 20 reactants and 25-620 degrees Celsius. Following the ALD cycle, AJIN predicts adsorption from graph-neural-network embeddings of the precursor and substrate with temperature, and deposition by adding the reactant. The best model (CHGNet-MLP) reaches balanced accuracies of 79.2% for adsorption and 84.8% for deposition and transfers partially to unseen precursors. SHAP and ablation analyses show that substrate chemistry governs adsorption, whereas precursor, substrate and reactant all contribute to deposition. Tested against our laboratory's ALD experiments, AJIN correctly predicted 83% of new-substrate cases and 71% of new precursor-substrate adsorption cases, providing an experimentally grounded tool for prioritizing ALD and area-selective ALD chemistries.

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

近地轨道（LEO）是大多数活跃卫星的所在地，预计将经历称为凯斯勒综合症的碎片碎片级联。鉴于这种可能性，必须最大限度地延长低地球轨道卫星的使用寿命。实现这一目标的关键是开发具有更高抗原子氧（AO）侵蚀能力的下一代聚合物材料。由于真正的低地球轨道暴露实验非常昂贵，而且低地球轨道条件很难在实验室中重现，因此数据驱动的方法为加速材料发现提供了一种有前途的替代方案。在这里，我们开发了一个基于 OpenAI 的 GPT-4o 的微调大语言模型 (LLM)，以使用从多个 NASA LEO 曝光任务编译的 201 个测量值的精选数据集来预测聚合物的 AO 侵蚀率 (E_y)。该模型的数量级误差 (OME) 为 0.154，对数尺度决定系数 (log-R²) 为 0.719，优于传统的机器学习方法，同时无需进行大量特征工程。值得注意的是，结合薄膜厚度和分层等宏观描述符并没有提高预测性能，这表明 AO 侵蚀主要受化学和环境变量而不是整体结构特征的控制。这一发现强调了化学信息聚合物设计在开发具有改进的 AO 抗性的材料中的重要性，同时证明了现代法学硕士即使在数据有限的情况下也能够有效提取化学上有意义的关系。除了预测准确性之外，所提出的框架还提供了一个可扩展的平台，用于快速筛选候选聚合物，减少对昂贵的航天实验的依赖。

English Abstract

Low Earth orbit (LEO), home to most active satellites, is projected to undergo a debris-fragmentation cascade known as Kessler Syndrome. In light of this eventuality, it is imperative to maximize the operational lifetime of LEO satellites. Instrumental to this goal is the development of next-generation polymeric materials with improved resistance to atomic oxygen (AO) erosion. Because genuine LEO exposure experiments are prohibitively expensive and LEO conditions are difficult to reproduce in the laboratory, data-driven approaches offer a promising alternative for accelerating materials discovery. Here, we develop a fine-tuned large language model (LLM) based on OpenAI's GPT-4o to predict the AO erosion yield (E_y) of polymers using a curated dataset of 201 measurements compiled from multiple NASA LEO exposure missions. The model achieves an order of magnitude error (OME) of 0.154 and a log-scaled coefficient of determination (log-R²) of 0.719, outperforming conventional machine learning approaches while eliminating the need for extensive feature engineering. Notably, incorporating macroscopic descriptors such as film thickness and layering did not improve predictive performance, suggesting that AO erosion is governed primarily by chemical and environmental variables rather than bulk structural characteristics. This finding underscores the importance of chemically informed polymer design in developing materials with improved AO resistance, while demonstrating the ability of modern LLMs to effectively extract chemically meaningful relationships even when data is limited. Beyond its predictive accuracy, the proposed framework provides a scalable platform for rapidly screening candidate polymers, reducing reliance on costly spaceflight experiments.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：active learning。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

贝叶斯优化广泛应用于材料科学的主动学习循环中。高斯过程充当事实上的代理模型系列，但需要依赖于任务的内核选择，并且可能缺乏复杂关系的建模能力。表格基础模型 (TFM) 最近已成为结构化数据预测任务的最先进技术，无需定制内核或超参数调整。我们评估了两个 TFM（TabPFN 和 TabICL）作为两个标准材料科学基准的开箱即用代理模型，并与包括高斯过程、随机森林和 CatBoost 在内的大量基线进行比较。我们的研究结果表明，TFM 在两个基准测试中都是极具竞争力的替代模型，无需针对主动学习或 GP 核选择进行特定任务的训练。我们的结果激励进一步开发 TFM 作为材料发现中主动学习的替代品。

English Abstract

Bayesian optimization is widely used in active learning loops for materials science. Gaussian processes serve as the de-facto surrogate model family, but require task-dependent kernel choices and can lack the modeling capacity for complex relationships. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have recently emerged as the state-of-the-art for predictive tasks on structured data, without requiring bespoke kernels or hyperparameter tuning. We evaluate two TFMs, TabPFN and TabICL, as out-of-the-box surrogate models on two standard materials science benchmarks, comparing against a large field of baselines encompassing Gaussian processes, random forests, and CatBoost. Our findings indicate that TFMs are highly competitive surrogate models across the two benchmarks, without task-specific training for active learning or GP kernel selection. Our results motivate developing TFMs further as surrogates for active learning in materials discovery.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

通用机器学习原子间势可以在化学空间常规中进行激发态模拟，但激发能与基态能量不同，不是原子贡献的总和。非相互作用分子的激发态是它们状态的并集，汇集的多态读数最多可以再现最低态。我们引入 LEH-MACE，其激发能是由以原子为中心的块组装而成的局部激发哈密顿量的特征值，因此该并集完全成立。我们为超过 100,000 个分子的约 300,000 个平衡态、热态和激发态几何结构生成了 TD-DFT 能量和力。 LEH-MACE 相对于直接读数的优势随着与训练数据的距离的增加而增加，并且在 S₁ 之上最大，对于长共轭低聚物，其 S2 和 S₃ 误差约为一半。对于分子对，只有 LEH-MACE 保持其单分子精度，而 softmin 读数对于较大发色团的 S₁ 动力学仍然优越。因此，精确的尺寸一致性是实现这一潜力的一步。

English Abstract

Universal machine-learned interatomic potentials could make excited-state simulations across chemical space routine, but excitation energies, unlike ground-state energies, are not sums of atomic contributions. The excited states of noninteracting molecules are the union of their states, which pooled multistate readouts reproduce at best for the lowest state. We introduce LEH-MACE, whose excitation energies are the eigenvalues of a local excitation Hamiltonian assembled from atom-centred blocks, so this union holds exactly. We generated TD-DFT energies and forces for about 300,000 equilibrium, thermal, and excited-state geometries of over 100,000 molecules. LEH-MACE's advantage over direct readouts grew with the distance from the training data and was largest above S₁, with about half their S₂ and S₃ errors for long conjugated oligomers. For molecule pairs, only LEH-MACE kept its single-molecule accuracy, while a softmin readout remained superior for S₁ dynamics of larger chromophores. Exact size consistency is thus one step toward such potentials.

AI4Plasma I：电容耦合等离子体场的数据高效替代建模#

原题：AI4Plasma I: Data-Efficient Surrogate Modeling of Capacitively Coupled Plasma Fields

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

参考电容耦合等离子体 (CCP) 模拟提供空间分辨等离子体场，但在不同操作条件下生成成本高昂。我们比较了五种替代架构（坐标 MLP、PCA-Net、U-Net、傅立叶神经算子 (FNO) 和 Transolver）的数据效率，用于预测 1D 平面和 2D 轴对称 GEC CCP 基准中的 RF 周期平均电子密度、离子密度、电势和电子温度。我们评估了参数内部和参数边界测试用例实现 1% 和 2% 平均相对 L2 误差所需的参考模拟数量，并检查特定于架构的机制。 FNO 在八项比较中的七项中实现了最低或联合最低的参考案例要求。在 1D 中，FNO 在 50 个训练案例中达到 1% 的内部误差，而 Transolver 为 100 个，U-Net 为 200 个。在 2D 中，FNO 和 U-Net 在 100 个案例中均达到 1% 的内部误差； FNO 和 Transolver 在 200 个案例中均达到 2% 的参数边界误差，而 U-Net 为 400 个。在更严格的 1% 2D 参数边界目标下，Transolver 需要 400 个案例，而 FNO 需要 800 个案例。其他分析研究了 FNO 谱模式和填充、U-Net 跳跃连接、Transolver 自适应切片以及 PCA-Net 投影与预测误差。结果支持 FNO 作为测试 CCP 设置的数据高效基线，同时表明首选架构取决于精度目标和操作参数区域。

English Abstract

Reference capacitively coupled plasma (CCP) simulations provide spatially resolved plasma fields but are costly to generate across operating conditions. We compare the data efficiency of five surrogate architectures--Coordinate MLP, PCA-Net, U-Net, Fourier neural operator (FNO), and Transolver--for predicting RF-cycle-averaged electron density, ion density, electric potential, and electron temperature in 1D planar and 2D axisymmetric GEC CCP benchmarks. We assess the number of reference simulations needed to achieve 1% and 2% mean relative L2 error for parameter-interior and parameter-boundary test cases, and examine architecture-specific mechanisms. FNO achieves the lowest or joint-lowest reference-case requirement in seven of eight comparisons. In 1D, FNO reaches 1% interior error with 50 training cases, compared with 100 for Transolver and 200 for U-Net. In 2D, FNO and U-Net each reach 1% interior error with 100 cases; FNO and Transolver each reach 2% parameter-boundary error with 200 cases, compared with 400 for U-Net. At the stricter 1% 2D parameter-boundary target, Transolver requires 400 cases versus 800 for FNO. Additional analyses investigate FNO spectral modes and padding, U-Net skip connections, Transolver adaptive slicing, and PCA-Net projection versus prediction error. The results support FNO as a data-efficient baseline for the tested CCP settings, while showing that the preferred architecture depends on the accuracy target and operating-parameter region.

具有可微基础模型的超导体和半导体的逆向设计#

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

设计具有目标特性的晶体需要同时选择其元素、化学计量和原子结构，但梯度仅存在于连续结构变量。我们引入了可微的 Equiformer 快速有效原子理论 (DEFEAT)，它使预训练的 EquiformerV3 基础模型接受连续的物种权重和位点占用，而无需对分数数据进行重新训练，从而实现基于梯度的材料优化。来自微调属性模型的梯度和机器学习的能量惩罚然后在单个优化中指导成分、原子计数、原子位置和晶格向量的更新。在寻找声子介导的超导体时，对 45 种结构新颖的设计的密度泛函微扰理论给出了 39 种超过 10 K 的各向同性 Allen-Dynes 临界温度； ZrTiNb2CN3 的最高温度为 24.0 K，H4LiPd3 (23.2 K) 位于静态 DFT 壳上。在寻找光伏吸收器时，SCAN 计算确认了十种新颖的晶体结构，其带隙在各自原始或经验校正尺度上的 1.34 eV 目标的 20% 以内，全部在 DFT 外壳的 70 meV/原子内，并表明训练函数的系统带隙误差会传播到设计中，除非目标对其进行校正。来自机器学习势的准谐波自由能进一步表明，与固相的有限温度竞争可以相对于静态外壳重新排序候选者。

English Abstract

Designing a crystal for a target property requires choosing its elements, stoichiometry, and atomic structure together, yet gradients exist only for the continuous structural variables. We introduce differentiable Equiformer fast effective-atom theory (DEFEAT), which lets a pretrained EquiformerV3 foundation model accept continuous species weights and site occupancies without retraining on fractional data, thereby enabling gradient-based materials optimization. Gradients from fine-tuned property models and a machine-learned energy penalty then guide updates to composition, atom count, atomic positions, and lattice vectors in a single optimization. In a search for phonon-mediated superconductors, density-functional perturbation theory on 45 structurally novel designs gives isotropic Allen-Dynes critical temperatures above 10 K for 39; the highest is 24.0 K for ZrTiNb2CN3, and H4LiPd3 (23.2 K) lies on the static DFT hull. In searches for photovoltaic absorbers, SCAN calculations confirm ten novel crystal structures with band gaps within 20% of a 1.34 eV target on their respective raw or empirically corrected scales, all within 70 meV/atom of the DFT hull, and show that the systematic gap error of the training functional propagates into the designs unless the objective corrects for it. Quasiharmonic free energies from machine-learned potentials further show that finite-temperature competition with solid phases can reorder candidates relative to the static hull.

AI4Plasma II：可变电极间隙 GEC Plasmas 的几何感知替代建模#

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：工业等离子体工艺开发需要对操作和几何参数进行广泛的探索。几何参数，特别是电极间距，影响放电结构和加工均匀性。通过重复的高保真模拟探索这些参数空间的计算成本可能很高。替代模型可以提供快速预测，但几何变化使其应用变得复杂，因为计算域和离散化可能会因样本而变化。在这项工作中，比较了基于规定和学习的几何映射、扩展域表示、连续坐标和几何原生离散化的六种几何感知替代模型，以准确预测晶圆附近的完整等离子体场和离子密度分布的数据要求。仿真数据集涵盖压力…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

工业等离子体工艺开发需要对操作和几何参数进行广泛的探索。几何参数，特别是电极间距，影响放电结构和加工均匀性。通过重复的高保真模拟探索这些参数空间的计算成本可能很高。替代模型可以提供快速预测，但几何变化使其应用变得复杂，因为计算域和离散化可能会因样本而变化。在这项工作中，比较了基于规定和学习的几何映射、扩展域表示、连续坐标和几何原生离散化的六种几何感知替代模型，以准确预测晶圆附近的完整等离子体场和离子密度分布的数据要求。仿真数据集涵盖压力、电压、频率和电极间隙，每个参考案例需要的平均仿真成本为 5.32 CPU 小时。为了在四个等离子体场中实现 2% 的平均误差，指定映射 Geo-FNO 和 Transolver 需要 100 个训练案例，而其他模型则需要 250-1000 个训练案例。预测晶圆附近的离子密度分布要求更高，并且会揭示模型之间更大的差异，而规定映射 Geo-FNO 需要最少的训练案例来实现所考虑的分布精度目标。为了进一步减少晶圆附近准确预测所需的数据，我们在训练损失中引入了电荷分离权重，这将规定映射 Geo-FNO 对 5% 轮廓误差的最低测试训练要求从 1000 个案例减少到 100 个案例。当合适的几何映射可用时，这些结果支持规定映射 Geo-FNO 作为本文研究的可变间隙问题的实际起点，而 Transolver 通过足够的训练数据实现了最高的预测精度。

English Abstract

Industrial plasma process development requires broad exploration of operating and geometric parameters. Geometric parameters, particularly electrode spacing, affect discharge structure and processing uniformity. Exploring these parameter spaces through repeated high-fidelity simulations can be computationally expensive. Surrogate models can offer rapid predictions, but geometric variation complicates their application because the computational domain and discretization may change across samples. In this work, six geometry-aware surrogate models based on prescribed and learned geometry mappings, extended-domain representation, continuous coordinates, and geometry-native discretizations are compared in terms of their data requirements for accurate predictions of full plasma fields and ion density profiles near the wafer. The simulation dataset spans pressure, voltage, frequency, and electrode gap, with each reference case requiring an average simulation cost of 5.32 CPU h. To achieve a 2% mean error across the four plasma fields, prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO and Transolver require 100 training cases, compared with 250-1000 for the other models. Predicting ion density profiles near the wafer is more demanding and reveals larger differences among models, with prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO requiring the fewest training cases for the profile accuracy targets considered. To further reduce the data required for accurate predictions near the wafer, we introduce charge-separation weighting into the training loss, which reduces the prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO's minimum tested training requirement for a 5% profile error from 1000 to 100 cases. These results support prescribed-mapping Geo-FNO as a practical starting point for the variable-gap problem studied here when a suitable geometry mapping is available, while Transolver achieves the highest prediction accuracy with sufficient training data.

数据驱动的非连续光子能量组，用于线控等离子体中的快速多组辐射传输#

原题：Data-driven non-contiguous photon-energy groups for fast multigroup radiation transport in line-dominated plasmas
作者：Shinsuke Fujioka、Yuji Takagi、Akira Sasaki、Yu Yamamoto、Nozomi Tanaka、Muhammad Fauzan Syahbana 等 10 人。arXiv · physics，2026-11-13

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：中高Z等离子体的不透明度主要由数千条重叠的光谱线决定。例如，用于极紫外 (EUV) 光刻的激光产生的等离子体、惯性聚变空腔和恒星内部。在 EUV 情况下，此类等离子体中的辐射传输需要超过 1000 个光子能量组，这使得辐射流体动力学模拟非常昂贵。我们表明，通过适当选择将细分箱放入同一组中，可以恢复小组不透明模型的准确性。我们提出了一种数据驱动的方法，将详细的不透明度和发射率表的精细容器分配给 K 个 emphnon 连续组。每个组内部都…

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

中高Z等离子体的不透明度主要由数千条重叠的光谱线决定。例如，用于极紫外 (EUV) 光刻的激光产生的等离子体、惯性聚变空腔和恒星内部。在 EUV 情况下，此类等离子体中的辐射传输需要超过 1000 个光子能量组，这使得辐射流体动力学模拟非常昂贵。我们表明，通过适当选择将细分箱放入同一组中，可以恢复小组不透明模型的准确性。我们提出了一种数据驱动的方法，将详细的不透明度和发射率表的精细容器分配给 K 个 emphnon 连续组。每个组内部都有固定的光谱形状。这些组遵循一个要求：减少吸收算子对于每个等离子体状态都必须是对角线。它们是根据光谱的 Gram 矩阵确定的，该光谱取自参数空间的一小部分（例如本工作中的 5%）的详细组解。简化模型是一组 K 个非耦合通量限制扩散方程。通过替换不透明度表，它可以在现有的多组代码中使用。对于电子密度为 4×10⁵--1.1×10²^~cm⁻³、电子温度为 5--250~eV、标尺长度为 2.5--6900um 的 Sn 等离子体，K=100 的模型再现了 1500 组参考的总通量，中值误差为 2%，90% 误差为 8%。同等成本下，90%误差比传统100组模型小十倍。

English Abstract

Opacity of mid- and high-Z plasmas is dominated by thousands of overlapping spectral lines. Examples are laser-produced plasmas for extreme-ultraviolet (EUV) lithography, inertial-fusion hohlraums, and stellar interiors. Radiation transport in such plasmas needs more than 1000 photon-energy groups in the EUV case, and this makes radiation-hydrodynamic simulations very expensive. We show that the accuracy of a small-group opacity model can be recovered by choosing properly emphwhich fine bins are put into the same group. We present a data-driven method that assigns the fine bins of a detailed opacity and emissivity table to K emphnon-contiguous groups. Each group has a fixed spectral shape inside it. The groups follow from one requirement: the reduced absorption operator must be diagonal for every plasma state. They are determined from a Gram matrix of spectra, taken from detailed-group solutions of a small part, for example 5% in this work, of the parameter space. The reduced model is a set of K uncoupled flux-limited diffusion equations. It can be used in an existing multigroup code by replacing the opacity table. For a Sn plasma with electron densities of 4×10¹⁵--1.1×10²¹~cm⁻³, electron temperatures of 5--250~eV, and scale lengths of 2.5--6900um, the model with K=100 reproduces the total flux of the 1500-group reference with a median error of 2% and a 90th-percentile error of 8%. The 90th-percentile error is ten times smaller than that of a conventional 100-group model at the same cost.

基于补偿网格的混合交换——大规模分子系统的简单、准确、高效且可微的方法#

原题：Compensated Grid-Based Hybrid Exchange - A Simple, Accurate, Efficient, and Differentiable Recipe for Large-Scale Molecular Systems

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

开发了一种基于补偿网格的方法，用于大规模分子系统中的混合交换。该方法使用对称重叠拟合的基于网格的交换方法，在正交归一化原子轨道基础上进行拟合。通过交换能量的原子密度差密度叠加（dSCF）划分增强了这种拟合，从而产生了最终的补偿的基于网格的交换方法。与原始的基于网格的交换相比，完整的方法被发现具有绝对和相对误差属性的数量级减少，并且保持分析可微分。该方法易于实现，并且比 GPU 积分直接技术提供更高的效率，以实现高质量和/或增强的基组。最近发布的 cuEST v0.3 库中提供了库函数，可帮助用户在自己的量子化学代码中实现该方法。

English Abstract

A compensated grid-based approach is developed for hybrid exchange in large-scale molecular systems. The method uses a symmetrically overlap-fitted grid-based exchange approach, with fitting performed in the orthonormalized atomic orbital basis. This fitting is augmented with a superposition-of-atomic-densities difference-density (dSCF) partitioning of the exchange energy, yielding the finished compensated grid-based exchange approach. The complete method is found to have orders of magnitude reduction of absolute and relative error properties versus raw grid-based exchange, and remains analytically differentiable. The method is easy to implement and provides efficiency gains over GPU integral-direct techniques for high quality and/or augmented basis sets. Library functions to help users implement the method in their own quantum chemistry codes are provided in the recently-shipped cuEST v0.3 library.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：neural network potential、density functional theory、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

我们提出了一种针对 NiTi 形状记忆合金马氏体相的高维神经网络势 (HDNNP)，并根据密度泛函理论 (DFT) 数据进行了训练。这项工作的一个核心方面是对控制结构演化的关键特性（包括平衡晶体结构、弹性常数、广义堆垛层错能和振动谱）的基础 DFT 参考方法的潜力进行系统验证。 HDNNP 准确地描述了 B19^prime 和 B33 相的相对稳定性，包括 meV/原子数量级的细微能量差异。预测的堆垛层错能量景观具有强烈的各向异性，揭示了优先剪切路径，为变形和孪晶机制提供了原子学的见解。有限温度分子动力学模拟进一步能够研究作为温度函数的无约束结构演化。总体而言，开发的 HDNNP 为纳秒时间尺度上包含数十万个原子的马氏体 NiTi 系统的复杂结构和功能行为的原子模拟提供了坚实的基础。

English Abstract

We present a high-dimensional neural network potential (HDNNP) for the martensitic phase of the NiTi shape-memory alloy trained to density functional theory (DFT) data. A central aspect of this work is the systematic validation of the potential with respect to the underlying DFT reference method for key properties governing structural evolution, including equilibrium crystal structures, elastic constants, generalized-stacking fault energies, and vibrational spectra. The HDNNP accurately describes the relative stability of the B19^prime and B33 phases, including subtle energy differences on the order of meV/atom. The predicted stacking-fault energy landscape is strongly anisotropic and reveals a preferential shear pathway, providing atomistic insight into deformation and twinning mechanisms. Finite-temperature molecular dynamics simulations further enable the investigation of unconstrained structural evolution as a function of temperature. Overall, the developed HDNNP provides a robust basis for atomistic simulations of the complex structural and functional behavior of martensitic NiTi systems containing hundreds of thousands of atoms on nanosecond time scales.

Ba-B-C 包合物中键合重建驱动的耦合电子结构和声子输运#

原题：Coupled Electronic Structure and Phonon Transport Driven by Bonding Reconstruction in Ba-B-C Clathrates

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子计数、结构对称性和键合环境的成分驱动变化可以耦合材料中的电子和热响应。在这里，我们利用密度泛函理论和机器学习原子间势 (MLIP)，将立方 BaB3C3 与其 B-C 取代的四方对应物 BaB2C4 进行比较。额外的价电子和伴随的晶体对称性变化驱动了从金属 BaB3C3 到半导体 BaB2C4 的转变以及短/强 C-C 键的形成。重建的键合网络使声子谱硬化，最大频率从 800 增加到 900。在室温下，BaB2C4 的面内晶格导热系数从 7.6 增加到 18.7 W/mK，而热传输从各向同性变为各向异性。这种增强与增强的键合网络、更高的声子频率/群速度、更长的寿命和更大的平均自由程相关。机器学习势准确地再现了 DFT 级能量和力，误差可忽略不计，并且谐波和非谐波特征以明确的第一原理计算为基准。我们的研究结果表明，最小的 B-C 取代提供了一种可行的策略，将组成和结构相关的键合重建与相关的电子和声子改变联系起来。

English Abstract

Composition-driven changes in electron count, structural symmetry and bonding environment can couple the electronic and thermal responses in a material. Here, with density-functional theory and machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs), we compare cubic BaB3C3 with its B-C substituted tetragonal counterpart, BaB2C4. The additional valence electron and accompanying change in crystal symmetry drive a transition from metallic BaB3C3 to semiconducting BaB2C4 and the formation of short/strong C-C bonds. The reconstructed bonding network hardens the phonon spectrum, with the maximum frequency increasing from 800 to 900. At room temperature, the in-plane lattice thermal conductivity increases from 7.6 to 18.7 W/mK, while the thermal transport alters from isotropic to anisotropic for BaB2C4. This enhancement is associated with the strengthened bonding network, higher phonon frequencies/group velocities, longer lifetimes and larger mean free paths. Machine learning potentials accurately reproduced the DFT-level energies and forces with negligible errors, and the harmonic and anharmonic features are benchmarked against explicit first-principles calculations. Our findings demonstrate that minimal B-C substitution provides a viable strategy to link composition- and structure-associated bonding reconstruction with correlated electronic and phononic alterations.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：graph neural networks、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

在过去十年中，我们建立了 Alexandria，这是一个包含约 1500 万个晶体密度泛函计算的开放数据库。它包括 PBE、PBEsol、SCAN 和 TB-mBJ 结果、在高达 150 GPa 的压力下松弛的结构、介电张量和约 145,000 个电子声子计算，以及超过 4 亿个通过机器学习势松弛的结构。根据这些数据，我们训练了晶体结构的生成模型、通用原子间势以及从带隙到超导临界温度等十多种属性的图神经网络。根据凸包，我们计算了 193,416 种稳定化合物之间 174 亿次反应的反应图。在这里，我们将所有这些交给通用语言模型代理。该特工在无人值守的情况下，筛选数据库中的太阳能吸收器，放弃合成释放能量太少的候选者，并编写包含前体、坩埚和大气的合成计划。当被问及经过充分研究的 Ba-Zr-S 系统时，它发现了一种未报告的硫化物，并设计了实验来证实或排除它。当被要求提供一种空气稳定的替代方案时，它设计了一种替代方案，并根据生成模型的新结构对其进行了测试。仅通过网络搜索，同一代理人就提出了文献中已经讨论过的内容。将数据、模型和反应图放在一起，让通用智能体能够完成他们无法单独完成的事情：从简单的问题到实验室可以运行和测试的实验。

English Abstract

Over the last decade we have built Alexandria, an open database of about 15 million density-functional calculations of crystals. It includes PBE, PBEsol, SCAN and TB-mBJ results, structures relaxed at pressures up to 150 GPa, dielectric tensors and about 145,000 electron-phonon calculations, together with more than 400 million structures relaxed with machine-learned potentials. On these data we trained a generative model for crystal structures, a universal interatomic potential and graph neural networks for more than ten properties, from band gaps to superconducting critical temperatures. From the convex hull we computed a reaction graph of the 17.4 billion reactions between its 193,416 stable compounds. Here we give all of this to a general-purpose language-model agent. Working unattended, the agent screens the database for a solar absorber, drops a candidate whose synthesis releases too little energy, and writes a synthesis plan with precursors, crucible and atmosphere. Asked about the well-studied Ba-Zr-S system, it finds an unreported sulfide and designs the experiment that would confirm or rule it out. Asked for an air-stable alternative, it designs one and tests it against new structures from the generative model. With web search alone, the same agent proposes what the literature already discusses. Put together, the data, the models and the reaction graph let a general agent do what none of them does alone: go from a question in plain words to an experiment that a laboratory can run and test.

Li3YCl6 固体电解质中应变相关的离子传输#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：solid-state electrolytes、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

固态电池需要电解质在功能电池的机械环境下维持高离子电导率。由堆压、界面晶格失配以及更重要的是电化学循环引起的晶格应变可以增强或抑制固体电解质中的Li⁺传输，但它如何与Li3YCl6中的潜在扩散机制耦合仍然知之甚少。使用 Li3YCl6 卤化物超离子导体，我们通过由原子团簇扩展 (ACE) 机器学习原子间势驱动的大规模分子动力学模拟来解决这个问题，该模型是根据第一性原理数据训练的。 ACE 模型忠实地再现了 Li3YCl6 的实验和从头开始的结构、机械和输运特性。将模拟扩展到高温揭示了从低温下主要的一维跳跃到高温下通过连接的三维路径网络的扩散的交叉。应变显着调节扩散率：拉伸应变增强扩散率，而压缩应变抑制扩散率。自由断点模型选择解决了大多数应变状态的两个阿伦尼乌斯状态，并且拟合的 T_c 随应变非单调变化。应变以与温度相关的方式改变有效活化能和扩散路径的可达性。这些结果量化了 Li3YCl6 固态电解质中离子电导率和扩散路径的应变敏感性。

English Abstract

Solid-state batteries require electrolytes that sustain high ionic conductivity under the mechanical environment of a functioning cell. Lattice strain, arising from stack pressure, lattice mismatch at interfaces, and more importantly electrochemical cycling, can either enhance or suppress Li⁺ transport in solid electrolytes, yet how it couples to the underlying diffusion mechanism in Li3YCl6 remains poorly understood. Using Li3YCl6 halide superionic conductor, we address this with large-scale molecular dynamics simulations driven by an Atomic Cluster Expansion (ACE) machine learning interatomic potential trained on first-principles data. The ACE model faithfully reproduces experimental and ab initio structural, mechanical, and transport properties of Li3YCl6. Extending the simulations to high temperature reveals a crossover from predominantly one-dimensional hopping at low temperature toward diffusion through a connected three-dimensional pathway network at high temperature. Strain substantially modulates diffusivity: tensile strain enhances it while compressive strain suppresses it. Free-breakpoint model selection resolves two Arrhenius regimes for most strain states, and the fitted T_c varies nonmonotonically with strain. Strain modifies the effective activation energies and the accessibility of diffusion pathways in a temperature-dependent manner. These results quantify the strain sensitivity of ionic conductivity and diffusion pathways in Li3YCl6 solid-state electrolytes.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

硫属化物合金，例如典型的 Ge2Sb2Te5 化合物，由于其在加热时能够在非晶相和晶相之间进行快速且可逆的转变，因此被用于相变电子存储器。非晶相容易老化，导致电阻增加，这不利于器件的运行。利用机器学习原子间势，我们在略低于玻璃化转变的温度下，在分子动力学模拟中研究了导致非晶态 Ge2Sb2Te5 老化的结构弛豫。时效模拟持续约 100 ns，在 400 K 下达到 24 ns 的平衡。在时效过程中，我们观察到轴向键中 Peierls 畸变的增强，这是这些合金的典型特征，我们将其与较小时效非晶模型中密度泛函理论计算出的电子带隙的加宽相关。我们还观察到老化过程中原子迁移率随着键寿命的增加而显着降低。然而，我们没有看到单极键的减少，预计理想的老化玻璃中不会出现单极键，并且应该在更长的时间范围内消除单极键。

English Abstract

Chalcogenide alloys such as the prototypical Ge₂Sb₂Te₅ compound are used in phase change electronic memories due to their ability to undergo a fast and reversible transition between the amorphous and crystalline phases upon heating. The amorphous phase is subject to aging that leads to an increase in the electrical resistance, which is detrimental to the operation of the devices. Using a machine learning interatomic potential, we have investigated the structural relaxations responsible for aging in amorphous Ge₂Sb₂Te₅ in molecular dynamics simulations at temperatures slightly below the glass transition. The aging simulation, lasting about 100 ns, follows an equilibration of 24 ns at 400 K. During aging we observed an enhancement of the Peierls distortion in axial bonds which is a typical feature of these alloys that we relate to the widening of the electronic band gap computed within Density Functional Theory in smaller aged amorphous models. We also observed a marked reduction of the atomic mobility with an increase of the bond lifetimes during aging. However, we do not see a reduction in homopolar bonds, which are expected to be absent in the ideal aged glass and which should be removed on a much longer time scale.

量子分子动力学模型降阶#

原题：Model Order Reduction for Quantum Molecular Dynamics

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics、electronic structure calculation、density functional theory。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

量子分子动力学 (QMD) 提供了一个高保真物理框架，用于根据第一原理对原子和分子现象进行建模。然而，其数学公式需要解决每个物理状态步骤的迭代、高维电子结构问题。在更广泛的数据驱动计算力学范式中，我们提出了一种基于投影的模型降阶框架，用于 QMD 中的电子结构计算。通过在 Kohn-Sham 密度泛函理论中发现电子解空间的低维流形，我们的方法将控制变分公式投影到本质上减少的子空间上。这避免了全空间迭代波函数优化，同时保持严格的物理对称性和状态一致性。我们在水分子上演示了这种方法，通过规范坐标对齐和结构化流形采样，证明了与跨结构轨迹和热力学可观测值的高保真参考解的强烈一致性。

English Abstract

Quantum molecular dynamics (QMD) provides a high-fidelity physical framework for modeling atomic and molecular phenomena from first principles. However, its mathematical formulation requires solving iterative, high-dimensional electronic structure problems at every physical state step. Within the broader data-driven computational mechanics paradigm, we present a projection-based model order reduction framework for electronic structure calculation in QMD. By discovering a low-dimensional manifold of the electronic solution space within Kohn-Sham density functional theory, our approach projects the governing variational formulation onto an intrinsically reduced subspace. This avoids full-space iterative wavefunction optimization while preserving rigorous physical symmetries and state consistency. We demonstrate this methodology on a water molecule, demonstrating strong agreement with high-fidelity reference solutions across structural trajectories and thermodynamic observables through canonical coordinate alignment and structured manifold sampling.

阴离子组成如何控制氯氧化物固态电解质中的结构框架和锂离子传输#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：solid-state electrolytes、crystal structure prediction、interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

混合阴离子氯氧化锂表现出高锂电导率，但氧含量、结构框架和锂离子传输之间的关系仍不清楚。在这里，使用机器学习原子间势的晶体结构预测和分子动力学模拟来检查 Li_yMOₓCl₆₋₂ₓ（M = Nb 和 Ta [第 5 族]，M = Ti、Zr 和 Hf [第 4 族]）。增加氧含量将孤立的富含氯化物的多面体转变为一维链，随后转变为更加高度连接的二维和三维框架。在第 5 族系列中，快速 Li 扩散发生在 x 约 0.67--1.5 范围内，其中 Li 位点形成三维渗透网络。平均力图和微推弹性带计算的潜力表明，快速锂离子传输需要一个低维框架，支持通过低势垒的欠配位锂位点进行长距离迁移，以及跨锂位点的类似能量。最后，我们进行细网格成分搜索，将 LiNbO₀.₆₇Cl₄.₆₇、LiTaO₀.₇₅Cl₄.₅、LiNbO₁.₅Cl₃ 和 LiTaO₁.₅Cl₃ 识别为稳定候选者，预测室温自扩散率高达 sim 10⁻⁶，cm²/秒。这些发现确定了氧含量如何控制框架和锂离子传输，指导卤氧化锂电解质的设计。

English Abstract

Mixed-anion lithium oxychlorides exhibit high Li conductivity, but the relationship between oxygen content, structural framework, and Li-ion transport remains unclear. Here, crystal structure prediction and molecular dynamics simulations with machine-learning interatomic potentials are used to examine Li_yMOₓCl₆₋₂ₓ (M = Nb and Ta [Group 5] and M = Ti, Zr, and Hf [Group 4]). Increasing oxygen content changes isolated chloride-rich polyhedra into one-dimensional chains and subsequently more highly connected two- and three-dimensional frameworks. In the Group 5 series, fast Li diffusion occurs over x approx 0.67--1.5, where the Li sites form a three-dimensional percolating network. Potential of mean force maps and nudged elastic band calculations reveal that fast Li-ion transport requires a low-dimensional framework supporting long-range migration through undercoordinated Li sites with low barriers, together with similar energies across Li sites. Finally, we perform fine-grid compositional search, which identifies LiNbO₀.₆₇Cl₄.₆₇, LiTaO₀.₇₅Cl₄.₅, LiNbO₁.₅Cl₃, and LiTaO₁.₅Cl₃ as stable candidates with predicted room-temperature self-diffusivities of up to sim 10⁻⁶,cm²/s. These findings establish how oxygen content controls framework and Li-ion transport, guiding the design of lithium oxyhalide electrolytes.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、interatomic potential、electronic structure、vacancies。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

有缺陷的黄铜矿最近成为有前途的热电材料，因为它们有序的本征空位可以深刻地重塑晶格动力学和电子结构。在这里，我们通过将第一性原理计算与机器学习原子间势相结合，对 II-III2-VI4 缺陷黄铜矿的晶格热和载流子传输特性进行了全面的理论研究。我们表明，空位排序增强了晶格畸变，导致强烈的非和谐性和亚价键合。软低频声子、强负 Grüneisen 参数和显着扩大的四声子散射相空间的相互作用导致四声子散射主导的热传输，产生超低晶格热导率。同时，VI位点的系统阴离子取代提供了调节电子结构的有效途径：阴离子电负性降低削弱了金属-阴离子杂化，使阴离子p态向上移动，缩小了带隙，从而改善了电传输。受益于空位诱导声子抑制和阴离子调节电子优化之间的协同作用，CdGa2Te4 表现出 0.19 W·m⁻1K⁻1 的超低晶格热导率和 0.957 的高室温 ZT。这项工作不仅预测有缺陷的黄铜矿作为高性能热电材料的一个有前途的平台，而且还通过集成空位排序、高阶声子散射和阴离子相关能带工程提供了实用的设计策略。

English Abstract

Defective chalcopyrites have recently emerged as promising thermoelectric materials because their ordered intrinsic vacancies can profoundly reshape both lattice dynamics and electronic structure. Here, we present a comprehensive theoretical investigation of the lattice thermal and carrier transport properties of II-III₂-VI₄ defective chalcopyrites by combining first-principles calculations with machine-learning interatomic potentials. We show that vacancy ordering enhances lattice distortion, leading to strong anharmonicity and metavalent bonding. The interplay of soft low-frequency phonons, strongly negative Grüneisen parameters, and a substantially enlarged four-phonon scattering phase space results in four-phonon-scattering-dominated heat transport, yielding ultralow lattice thermal conductivity. Meanwhile, systematic anion substitution at the VI-site provides an effective route to tune the electronic structure: reduced anion electronegativity weakens metal-anion hybridization, shifts anion p states upward, narrows the band gap, and thereby improves electrical transport. Benefiting from this synergy between vacancy-induced phonon suppression and anion-regulated electronic optimization, CdGa₂Te₄ exhibits an ultralow lattice thermal conductivity of 0.19 W·m⁻¹K⁻¹ and a high room-temperature ZT of 0.957. This work not only predicts defective chalcopyrites as a promising platform for high-performance thermoelectrics but also provides a practical design strategy by integrating vacancy ordering, higher-order phonon scattering, and anion-dependent band engineering.

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：first-principles calculations、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

神经缩放定律可以预测较大训练数据集的回报，但机器学习原子间势 (MLIP) 数据集是异构的且生成成本昂贵，并且相同数量的结构不需要包含等量的有用信息。我们研究了在组合 MatPES-MP-ALOE 训练池的子集上训练的 M3GNet、MACE 和 PET 固定模型配置下的数据扩展。在从 10,000 个到 640,000 个的训练结构子集以及完整的 116 万个结构训练集中，我们比较了均匀随机采样、三种化学和结构信息分层策略、迭代 DIRECT 以及使用来自三个预训练 MLIP 的嵌入的最远点采样。使用来自父分布的保留测试集和分布偏移 MP-r²SCAN 测试集上的内聚能、力、应力和总损失来评估模型。我们观察到幂律缩放随着数据的增加而改进，但拟合的缩放指数、表观误差底限和相对样本效率取决于模型架构、目标数量、数据采样策略和测试集分布。采样策略排名可以在测试分布中逆转：化学分层可以提高跨数据集性能，同时降低分布内性能，而嵌入空间多样性策略经常表现不佳。相对损失与测试到训练的化学空间分歧密切相关。这些结果表明，结构计数对于 MLIP 来说是必要但不充分的缩放变量。对于训练 MLIP，额外第一性原理计算的回报不仅取决于包含多少额外结构，还取决于它们的化学和构型内容如何与模型的预期用途保持一致。

English Abstract

Neural scaling laws enable the prediction of returns from larger training datasets, but machine-learned interatomic potential (MLIP) datasets are heterogeneous and expensive to generate, and equal numbers of structures need not contain equal amounts of useful information. We study data scaling under fixed model configurations for M3GNet, MACE, and PET trained on subsets of a combined MatPES-MP-ALOE training pool. Across training structure subsets from 10,000 to 640,000, together with the full 1.16-million-structure training set, we compare uniform random sampling, three chemistry- and structure-informed stratification policies, iterative DIRECT, and furthest-point sampling using embeddings from three pretrained MLIPs. Models are evaluated using cohesive-energy, force, stress, and aggregate losses on both a held-out test set from the parent distribution and the distribution shifted MP-r²SCAN test set. We observe power-law scaling improvements with increasing data, but the fitted scaling exponent, apparent error floor, and relative sample efficiency depend on the model architecture, target quantity, data sampling policy, and test set distribution. Sampling-policy rankings can reverse across test distributions: chemical stratification can improve cross-dataset performance while degrading in-distribution performance, whereas the embedding-space diversity policies frequently under-perform random sampling. Relative loss is strongly associated with test-to-train chemical-space divergence. These results show that structure count is a necessary but insufficient scaling variable for MLIPs. For training MLIPs, the return on additional first-principles calculations depends not only on how many additional structures are included, but also on how their chemical and configurational content aligns with the model's intended use.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

当前的机器学习原子间势提供了小型、快速模型和大型、富有表现力的网络之间的选择。这项研究展示了中间势如何以最便宜的现有形式主义为代价接近可转移精度的极限。具体来说，我们使用原子团簇展开对含氦 W-Ta-Hf-Cr-V-Ti 合金进行建模，该原子团簇展开将每个多项式的径向分量学习为独立的线性变换，并使用学习的指数嵌入大量密度。虽然（接近）训练损失很大程度上取决于模型大小，但不同验证集上的误差接近于参数多十倍的领先潜力的误差。利用新结构，我们开发了优化的实现，其运行速度比较大模型快一个数量级以上，比具有等效多项式的较小模型快许多倍。这种潜力是针对系统枚举的小型结构进行训练的，但可以推广到点和平面缺陷，并且可以扩展到更复杂的化学物质。作为演示，我们模拟了十亿个原子系统中数十万个时间步长的辐射损伤级联。

English Abstract

Current machine-learned interatomic potentials offer a choice between small, fast models and large, expressive networks. This study demonstrates how an intermediate potential can approach the limit of transferable accuracy at the cost of the cheapest existing formalisms. Specifically, we model He-bearing W-Ta-Hf-Cr-V-Ti alloys with an atomic cluster expansion that learns the radial components of each polynomial as independent linear transforms and embeds numerous densities with learned exponents. While (near-)training loss appears largely a function of model size, the error on a distinct validation set is close to that of leading potentials with ten times more parameters. Taking advantage of the new structure, we develop optimized implementations that run more than an order of magnitude faster than the larger models and many times faster than smaller models with equivalent polynomials. The potential is trained on systematically enumerated small structures but generalizes to point and planar defects and can be extended to even more complex chemistries. As a demonstration, we simulate radiation-damage cascades in billion-atom systems for hundreds of thousands of timesteps.

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、electronic structure。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

溶液中热力学性质的定量预测对于将原子模拟转化为可靠的化学见解至关重要。作为一个示例系统，CaCO₃ 在水中的行为已被广泛研究，以了解其在海水中的矿化作用，这对碳捕获策略具有潜在影响。然而，做出准确的计算预测一直是一个长期的挑战，需要高度准确的电子结构方法和广泛的统计采样。在这里，我们结合机器学习和电子结构理论的进步，通过显式溶剂化完全解决了 CaCO₃ 的离子配对自由能。我们表明，要获得与实验的定量一致性，需要超越密度泛函理论，达到具有单次、双次和微扰三重激发的“黄金标准”耦合簇理论[CCSD(T)]。我们生成了一套系统可改进的模型，能够可靠地洞察 Ca 和 CO₃ 离子在成核之前的初始关联机制，同时完全量化焓和熵效应。这些定量准确的模型进一步解决了长期以来围绕溶液中钙离子水合机制缺乏共识的问题。我们的结果表明，复杂水系统的 CCSD(T) 级热力学预测现在可以常规实现。

English Abstract

Quantitative prediction of thermodynamic properties in solution is essential for translating atomistic simulations into reliable chemical insight. As an exemplar system, the behaviour of CaCO₃ in water has been widely studied to understand its mineralization in seawater, with potential implications for carbon-capture strategies. However, making accurate computational predictions has been a long-standing challenge, requiring both highly accurate electronic structure methods and extensive statistical sampling. Here, we combine advances in machine learning and electronic structure theory to fully resolve the ion pairing free energy of CaCO₃ with explicit solvation. We show that achieving quantitative agreement with experiment requires going beyond density functional theory up to the ''gold-standard'' coupled cluster theory with single, double, and perturbative triple excitations [CCSD(T)]. We generate a set of systematically improvable models, enabling reliable insights into the initial association mechanism of Ca and CO₃ ions prior to nucleation while fully quantifying enthalpic and entropic effects. These quantitatively accurate models further resolve a long-standing lack of consensus surrounding the hydration mechanism of the calcium ion in solution. Our results demonstrate that CCSD(T)-level thermodynamic predictions of complex aqueous systems can now be routinely achieved.

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

来自局部原子环境的机器学习为电子结构提供了一条有效的途径，但评估大型系统中的全局电子特性仍然需要大量计算。在这里，我们使用根据预测密度构建的 Kohn-Sham 哈密顿量将真实空间电荷密度学习与随机传播结合起来。该框架避免了目标系统自洽迭代和显式对角化，而分层分块可以在有限的 GPU 内存内实现密集网格推理。我们演示了 100 万个 Si 原子的态密度计算以及超过 10 万个原子的光学和传输计算，而无需为每个属性训练单独的模型。该框架扩展了 Kohn-Sham DFT 中电子结构计算的实际规模，为研究原子结构如何在与实际材料和设备相关的长度尺度上控制电子、光学和传输特性提供了机会。

English Abstract

Machine learning from local atomic environments offers an efficient route to electronic structure, but evaluating global electronic properties in large systems remains computationally demanding. Here we combine real space charge density learning with stochastic propagation using Kohn-Sham Hamiltonians constructed from the predicted densities. The framework avoids target system self-consistent iterations and explicit diagonalization, while hierarchical blocking enables dense grid inference within limited GPU memory. We demonstrate density of states calculations for one million Si atoms and optical and transport calculations for over one hundred thousand atoms, without training separate models for each property. This framework extends the practical scale of electronic structure calculations within Kohn-Sham DFT, opening opportunities to investigate how atomic structure governs electronic, optical and transport properties at length scales relevant to real materials and devices.

时空能量一致性使扩散模型能够获得可靠的粗粒度分子动力学#

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

粗粒度分子动力学 (CGMD) 将分子模拟扩展到原子模型无法达到的长度和时间尺度，但建立热力学忠实的 CG 势仍然是一个长期的挑战。基于能量的扩散模型 (EBDM) 为通过零时间能量学习 CG 势提供了一条有前途的途径。然而，由扩散学习势驱动的 CGMD 可能会产生明显偏离目标分布的平衡系综。我们表明，零时间分布误差受到空间得分误差和时间能量导数误差之和的限制，而传统的扩散目标仅直接监督空间分量。基于这一发现，我们引入了时空能量一致性（STEC）监督，它通过明确约束时间能量导数来补充空间分数匹配。我们为这个时间分量导出了一个精确且易于处理的目标，避免了与先前的正则化方案相关的大量计算开销。在肽和快速折叠蛋白质系统中，经过 STEC 训练的 EBDM 可以在化学精度范围内准确地重现具有自由能误差的平衡分布，同时支持恢复关键亚稳态转变模式的稳定的长时间模拟。对于保留的八肽，STEC 相对于最强的竞争基线将自由能误差减少了 5 倍以上，同时在定义的结构覆盖基准下相对于原子分子动力学实现了中值生产成本降低约 1,700 倍。这些结果将 STEC 确立为一个可扩展的框架，用于学习日益复杂的生物分子系统的热力学可靠的 CG 力场。

English Abstract

Coarse-grained molecular dynamics (CGMD) extends molecular simulations to length and time scales inaccessible to atomistic models, but building thermodynamically faithful CG potentials remains a longstanding challenge. Energy-based diffusion models (EBDMs) provide a promising route for learning CG potentials through their zero-time energy. However, CGMD driven by diffusion-learned potentials can produce equilibrium ensembles that deviate substantially from the target distribution. We show that the zero-time distribution error is bounded by the sum of the spatial score error and temporal energy-derivative error, whereas conventional diffusion objectives directly supervise only the spatial component. Based on this finding, we introduce Spatiotemporal Energy Consistency (STEC) supervision, which complements spatial score matching by explicitly constraining temporal energy derivatives. We derive an exact and tractable objective for this temporal component, avoiding the substantial computational overhead associated with previous regularization schemes. Across peptide and fast-folding protein systems, STEC-trained EBDMs accurately reproduce equilibrium distributions with free-energy errors within chemical accuracy, while supporting stable long-time simulations that recover key metastable transition patterns. On held-out octapeptides, STEC reduces free-energy errors by more than 5-fold relative to the strongest competing baseline, while achieving a median production-cost reduction of approximately 1,700-fold relative to atomistic molecular dynamics under a defined structural-coverage benchmark. These results establish STEC as a scalable framework for learning thermodynamically reliable CG force fields for increasingly complex biomolecular systems.

HARMONY：用于预测实验振动谱的高效、准确的深度学习框架#

原题：HARMONY: an efficient and accurate deep learning framework for prediction of experimental vibrational spectra

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

预测实验振动光谱需要有效的分子计算和从理论振动到测量信号的可靠映射。在这里，我们介绍 HARMONY，这是一个两阶段的深度学习框架，它使用谐波谱作为物理锚来弥补这一差距。等变神经网络直接预测原子力和光谱张量，以快速构建谐波光谱，而 HarmoNet 将这些光谱与分子拓扑和测量元数据相结合，以预测实验红外和拉曼光谱。 HARMONY 的红外光谱平均余弦相似度为 0.912，拉曼光谱平均余弦相似度为 0.907，每个分子的平均推理时间为 2.12~s，比评估的 DFT 工作流程快约 600 倍。除了正向预测之外，HARMONY 通过伪实验预训练和候选结构的光谱验证改进了逆向分子推理。将 HARMONY 和 Vib2Mol 作为科学工具集成到通用代理中，可以实现自适应候选生成和验证。根据提供的分子式，所得的 Vib2Mol--HARMONY Agent 在使用红外光谱的 NIST 上实现了 70.53% 的 Top-1 召回率，在使用红外和拉曼光谱组合的 SDBS 上实现了 83.33% 的 Top-1 召回率。使用 19 个氨基酸对照和 8 个肽进行微调，进一步将 15 个保留肽的平均拉曼余弦相似性从 0.669 增加到 0.896。 HARMONY 将谐波锚定建立为分子物理学、实验光谱学和自适应分子结构阐明之间的实际联系。

English Abstract

Predicting experimental vibrational spectra requires both efficient molecular calculations and a reliable mapping from theoretical vibrations to measured signals. Here we introduce HARMONY, a two-stage deep-learning framework that bridges this gap using harmonic spectra as physical anchors. Equivariant neural networks directly predict atomic forces and spectroscopic tensors for rapid harmonic-spectrum construction, while HarmoNet combines these spectra with molecular topology and measurement metadata to predict experimental infrared and Raman spectra. HARMONY achieves mean cosine similarities of 0.912 for infrared and 0.907 for Raman spectra, with a mean inference time of 2.12~s per molecule, approximately 600-fold faster than the evaluated DFT workflow. Beyond forward prediction, HARMONY improves inverse molecular inference through pseudo-experimental pretraining and spectral verification of candidate structures. Integrating HARMONY and Vib2Mol as scientific tools within a general-purpose agent enables adaptive candidate generation and verification. With molecular formulas provided, the resulting Vib2Mol--HARMONY Agent achieves Top-1 recall of 70.53% on NIST using infrared spectra and 83.33% on SDBS using combined infrared and Raman spectra. Fine-tuning with 19 amino-acid controls and eight peptides further increases mean Raman cosine similarity from 0.669 to 0.896 on 15 held-out peptides. HARMONY establishes harmonic anchoring as a practical connection between molecular physics, experimental spectroscopy and adaptive molecular structure elucidation.

用于原子模拟专家级自动化的可扩展代理框架#

原题：An Extensible Agentic Framework for Expert-Level Automation of Atomistic Simulations

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

传统上，原子模拟受到从头计算方法的计算规模限制和经验力场的参数化开销的限制。最近出现的通用机器学习原子间势显着缓解了这些瓶颈，以一小部分计算成本提供了跨不同化学空间的近量子精度和通用性。然而，这种转变将瓶颈转移到了人类维度：耗时的机械过程，例如输入准备和数据分析，现在主导了研究生命周期。我们介绍 Paimon，一个用于材料优化和纳米级模拟的代理集成平台。通过对专家级液体电解质模拟进行的数百次试验，我们表明，Paimon 通过抑制无声错误，显着提高了代理工作流程的可靠性：看似合理但物理上不正确的结果。 Paimon 在不修改现有代理的情况下提供了新功能，我们通过集成外部开发的科学代理来证明这一点。作为原子模拟的代理工具，Paimon 为研究人员在整个模拟生命周期中提供了连续的、以科学为中心的工作流程。

English Abstract

Traditionally, atomistic simulation has been constrained by the computational scaling limits of ab initio methods and the parameterization overhead of empirical force fields. The recent emergence of universal machine learning interatomic potentials has significantly mitigated these bottlenecks, offering near-quantum accuracy and generalizability across diverse chemical spaces at a fraction of the computational cost. However, this shift has relocated the bottleneck to the human dimension: time-consuming mechanical processes, such as input preparation and data analysis, now dominate the research lifecycle. We introduce Paimon, a Platform for Agentic Integration in Materials Optimization and Nanoscale-simulations. Through hundreds of trials on an expert-level liquid electrolyte simulation, we show that Paimon substantially improves the reliability of agentic workflows by suppressing silent errors: plausible yet physically incorrect results. Paimon accommodates a new capability without modifying the agents already present, which we demonstrate by integrating an externally developed scientific agent. As an agent harness for atomistic simulations, Paimon affords researchers a continuous, science-centric workflow throughout the entire simulation lifecycle.

水自电离自由能中竞争效应的偶然抵消：长程静电、系统尺寸和核量子效应#

作者：Samuel Varner、Yinan Xu、Yezhi Jin、Gustavo R. Pérez-Lemus、Pablo F. Zubieta Rico、Zhiying Yi 等 11 人。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-09

页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

对于化学来说，很少有分子过程比水的自电离更重要，其平衡常数 K_w 约为 10⁻14，是酸碱平衡和生命化学的基础。然而，形成和分离离子的自由能成本的分子起源仍不完全清楚。由于难以足够详细地模拟水反应性，解释它的努力受到限制。在这里，我们将机器学习原子间势（MLIP）与光谱自适应偏置力增强采样相结合，将质子转移和水线配置与底层自由能景观连接起来。我们确定了长程静电、系统尺寸、温度和核量子运动对预测电离热力学的竞争影响。自由能表面从中性水延伸到溶剂分离离子对 (SSIP) 之外的分离离子。它揭示了一种过渡态，其中质子在三元水线的第二个和第三个分子之间共享。接触离子对不稳定，SSIP 仅弱亚稳态，因此确定 pK_w 需要采样较长的电线。长程静电将离子分离时的自由能提高到超过 3,mathmmathringA。具有可比力误差的模型在该区域给出了截然不同的自由能分布。增加系统尺寸会降低计算的 pK_w，而核量子效应使其更接近实验，并重现 D2O 相对于 H2O 更高的 pK_w。这些结果为解释 MLIP 模拟的热力学结果建立了基线，其中带电基团和极性基团在有限单元中发生反应和扩散。我们的分析还解释了即使模拟忽略了几个物理贡献，竞争效应之间的抵消也可以产生接近实验的电离常数。

English Abstract

Few molecular processes are more fundamental to chemistry than the autoionization of water, whose equilibrium constant, K_w approx 10⁻¹⁴, underlies acid-base equilibria and the chemistry of life. Yet the molecular origins of the free energy cost of forming and separating ions remain incompletely understood. Efforts to explain it have been limited by the difficulty of modeling water reactivity in sufficient detail. Here we combine machine learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) with spectral adaptive biasing force enhanced sampling to connect proton transfer and water wire configurations to the underlying free energy landscape. We identify competing influences of long-range electrostatics, system size, temperature, and nuclear quantum motion on the predicted ionization thermodynamics. The free energy surface extends from neutral water to ions separated beyond the solvent-separated ion pair (SSIP). It reveals a transition state in which a proton is shared between the second and third molecules of a three-membered water wire. The contact ion pair is unstable and the SSIP only weakly metastable, so determining pK_w requires sampling longer wires. Long-range electrostatics raise the free energy at ion separations beyond 3,mathrmmathringA. Models with comparable force errors give substantially different free energy profiles in this region. Increasing system size lowers the calculated pK_w, while nuclear quantum effects bring it closer to experiment and reproduce the higher pK_w of D₂O relative to H₂O. These results establish a baseline for interpreting thermodynamic results from MLIP simulations in which charged and polar groups react and diffuse in finite cells. Our analysis also explains how cancellation among competing effects can yield ionization constants close to experiment even when simulations omit several physical contributions.

使用经验赝势网络学习跨 s、p、d 块的电子能带结构#

原题：Learning Electronic Band Structures Across the s, p, d Blocks with an Empirical Pseudopotential Network
作者：Qiang Zhu。arXiv · cond-mat，2026-11-10

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：电子能带结构控制晶体传导电子和吸收光的方式。密度泛函理论 (DFT) 通过成本高昂的自洽迭代来计算它们，而现有的机器学习模型要么预测孤立的标量属性，要么输出仍需要 DFT 求解器的哈密顿量。在这里，我们提出了 EpNet，这是一种学习的晶体经验赝势，跨越 s、p 和 d 块的 63 个元素。等变神经网络根据物理驱动的局部势和非局部投影构建其哈密顿量。 EpNet 仅根据能带能量进行训练，直接从原子坐标预测完整的能带结构。在不可见的化学系…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：equivariant neural network。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

电子能带结构控制晶体传导电子和吸收光的方式。密度泛函理论 (DFT) 通过成本高昂的自洽迭代来计算它们，而现有的机器学习模型要么预测孤立的标量属性，要么输出仍需要 DFT 求解器的哈密顿量。在这里，我们提出了 EpNet，这是一种学习的晶体经验赝势，跨越 s、p 和 d 块的 63 个元素。等变神经网络根据物理驱动的局部势和非局部投影构建其哈密顿量。 EpNet 仅根据能带能量进行训练，直接从原子坐标预测完整的能带结构。在不可见的化学系统中，其中值误差对于带能来说是 winAll eV ，对于带隙来说是gapAll eV 。在来自独立数据库的 600,000 个结构上，其带隙误差总体上不到现有模型的一半，约为 DFT 成本的 1%。因此，EpNet 能够快速筛选电子特性，用于材料发现和大型系统的电子结构模拟。

English Abstract

Electronic band structures govern how crystals conduct electrons and absorb light. Density-functional theory (DFT) computes them through costly self-consistent iterations, while existing machine-learning models either predict isolated scalar properties or output Hamiltonians that still require DFT solvers. Here we present EpNet, a learned empirical pseudopotential for crystals spanning 63 elements across the s, p and d blocks. An equivariant neural network builds its Hamiltonian from physics-motivated local potentials and nonlocal projectors. Trained solely on band energies, EpNet predicts complete band structures directly from atomic coordinates. On unseen chemical systems, its median errors are winAll eV for band energies and gapAll eV for band gaps. On 600,000 structures from an independent database, its band-gap errors are overall less than half those of existing models, at about 1% of DFT cost. EpNet thus enables fast screening of electronic properties for materials discovery and electronic-structure simulations of large systems.

用于反应训练数据的系统独立元动力学策略：在气相有机反应中的应用#

原题：A System-Independent Metadynamics Strategy for Reactive Training Data: Application to Gas-Phase Organic Reactions
作者：Wanrun Jiang、Jinzhe Zeng、Manyi Yang、Tong Zhu、Han Wang。arXiv · cond-mat.supr-con，2026-11-13

📄 摘要（中文翻译）：有机反应的通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）需要准确描述用于评估反应能量学的最小能量路径（MEP）和用于模拟反应动力学的更广泛的构型空间。现有的气相反应通用数据集很大程度上依赖于 MEP 附近的准静态优化，导致 MEP 外区域未被覆盖，并且反应分子动力学模型的可靠性得到弱支持；差距是方法论上的问题，而不是数据集大小的问题。我们引入了一个时空解析的、与系统无关的集体变量（CV）：在随机分区的局部域内针对扩展的时间平均参考几何形状列表的笛卡尔…

🔗 与我们工作的关联与启发

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：interatomic potential、molecular dynamics。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

有机反应的通用机器学习原子间势（MLIP）需要准确描述用于评估反应能量学的最小能量路径（MEP）和用于模拟反应动力学的更广泛的构型空间。现有的气相反应通用数据集很大程度上依赖于 MEP 附近的准静态优化，导致 MEP 外区域未被覆盖，并且反应分子动力学模型的可靠性得到弱支持；差距是方法论上的问题，而不是数据集大小的问题。我们引入了一个时空解析的、与系统无关的集体变量（CV）：在随机分区的局部域内针对扩展的时间平均参考几何形状列表的笛卡尔 RMSD。 CV 作为探索引擎驱动元动力学，并辅以过渡态 (TS) 优化以扩大 TS 周围的覆盖范围。在并发学习工作流程中，这会产生 OpenRxn26，这是一个 1.8~M DFT 标记配置的数据集，涵盖 H/C/N/O 化学空间 (N_heavy ≤ 30) 中的中性单线态单分子反应，包含社区数据集中代表性不足的反应原子环境。在 OpenRxn26 上训练的 DPA4 模型 (DPA4_rxn26) 是基准套件中唯一能够在势垒高度、反应能量和非 MEP 反应轨迹上实现可转移的亚 kcal/mol 能量精度的模型，相对于训练 DFT 参考，证明了采样策略的效率和通用性。在静态基准上表现良好的域 MLIP 在非 MEP 反应轨迹（例如 MACE-OMol25）上性能下降了数倍，这表明 MEP 锚定基准在保证动态可靠性方面的不足。该采样策略能够有效开发可靠的通用反应性 MLIP，并以 OpenRxn26 作为气相中性单线态有机反应的 MD 就绪训练数据的演示。

English Abstract

General-purpose machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) for organic reactions need to accurately describe both minimum-energy paths (MEPs) for evaluating reaction energetics and the broader configurational space for simulating reaction dynamics. Existing general datasets for gas-phase reactions largely rely on quasi-static optimization near MEPs, leaving off-MEP regions under-covered and model reliability in reactive molecular dynamics weakly supported; the gap is methodological, not a question of dataset size. We introduce a spatiotemporally resolved, system-independent collective variable (CV): Cartesian RMSD within randomly partitioned local domains against an expanding list of time-averaged reference geometries. The CV drives metadynamics as the exploration engine, supplemented by transition state (TS) optimization to augment coverage around TSs. Within a concurrent-learning workflow, this produces OpenRxn26, a dataset of 1.8~M DFT-labeled configurations covering neutral singlet unimolecular reactions in the H/C/N/O chemical space (N_heavy ≤ 30), containing reactive atomic environments underrepresented in community datasets. Trained on OpenRxn26, a DPA4 model (DPA4_rxn26) is the only model in the benchmark suite to achieve transferable sub-kcal/mol energy accuracy on barrier heights, reaction energies and off-MEP reactive trajectories, relative to the training DFT reference, demonstrating the efficiency and generality of the sampling strategy. Domain MLIPs performing well on static benchmarks degrade several-fold on off-MEP reactive trajectories (e.g. MACE-OMol25), showing the insufficiency of MEP-anchored benchmarks for guaranteeing dynamics reliability. The sampling strategy enables efficient development of reliable general-purpose reactive MLIPs, with OpenRxn26 as a demonstration of MD-ready training data for gas-phase neutral singlet organic reactions.

原题：High-Throughput Surface Screening Reveals Energetically Competitive Low-Symmetry Facets in Wurtzite Semiconductors

关联：与你方向的关键词重合：density functional theory、interatomic potential。

阅读原文 ↗
页内定位所在专题
中文摘要

表面和界面决定着纤锌矿半导体在电子、传感和太阳能燃料转换方面的性能。迄今为止，由于低对称高折射率表面建模的计算成本和复杂性，第一原理研究主要集中在高对称低折射率面。然而，实际上，表面形成能是由平面原子密度和解理时切断的键的强度决定的，这两者都本质上排除了高米勒指数表面的能量竞争。在这里，我们结合自动表面生成、机器学习原子间势筛选和密度泛函理论，探索了纤锌矿 GaN、LaN 和 ZnO 的 226 个干净的化学计量表面模型，涵盖所有米勒方向 |h|、|k|、|l| 。 le 3 及其不等价终止符。我们发现，低对称性高折射率棱柱面相对于传统的 m 面和 a 面在能量上具有竞争力：15 个能量最低的非极性表面中有 9 个是高折射率的，并且每种化合物的 5 个最佳候选面仅位于最小表面能的 2.5-5.3% 范围内。然而，混合功能计算表明，这种能量接近并不能转化为类似的电子排列。在 GaN 中，两个最稳定的表面的形成能仅相差 0.08 J m-2，但在真空参考带边缘位置相差近 0.8 eV，而在 LaN 中，近简并的 m 面和 a 面相差 0.16 eV。因此，具有相似形成能的纤锌矿晶面可能表现出非常不同的电离势和电子亲和势。我们的结果能够对纤锌矿中实际出现的晶面的稳定性和能带排列进行联合、系统的评估，并为相关纳米结构和异质界面的合理设计提供候选表面图。

English Abstract

Surfaces and interfaces govern the performance of wurtzite semiconductors in electronics, sensing, and solar-fuel conversion. To date, first-principles studies have focused mostly on high-symmetry low-index facets, owing to the computational cost and complexity of modelling low-symmetry high-index surfaces. In practice, however, surface formation energy is determined by the planar atomic density and the strength of the bonds severed upon cleavage, neither of which inherently excludes high-Miller-index surfaces from being energetically competitive. Here, we combine automated surface generation, machine-learning interatomic potential screening, and density functional theory to explore 226 clean, stoichiometric surface models of wurtzite GaN, LaN, and ZnO, spanning all Miller orientations with |h|,|k|,|l| le 3 and their inequivalent terminations. We find that low-symmetry high-index prismatic facets are energetically competitive with respect to the conventional m- and a-planes: nine of the fifteen lowest-energy nonpolar surfaces are high-index, and the five best candidates of each compound lie within only 2.5-5.3% of the minimum surface energy. Hybrid-functional calculations reveal, however, that this energetic proximity does not translate into similar electronic alignment. In GaN, the two most stable surfaces differ by just 0.08 J m⁻² in formation energy but by almost 0.8 eV in vacuum-referenced band-edge positions, whereas in LaN the nearly degenerate m- and a-planes are separated by 0.16 eV. Wurtzite facets with similar formation energies may therefore exhibit very different ionization potentials and electron affinities. Our results enable the joint, systematic assessment of stability and band alignment across the facets that can actually occur in wurtzites, and provide a map of candidate surfaces for the rational design of related nanostructure and heterointerfaces.
